  1. **Copies only when the exclusions changed the document.** Seam: a copy-serving backend (`identity.servedAsCopy`) gets bytes only then (`DocumentRewrite.rulesOnly` marks a rules-only change, served as the original); an in-place backend (Firefox stream) still gets the rules-stripped text. Chromium drops `Speculation-Rules` from every released session document via `Fetch.continueResponse` (code + phrase + filtered headers; verified on CFT 156 and Chrome 155: works at the Response stage for Document, page stays `secure`/`h2`, the click goes to the network) with a plain-continue fallback (`chromium/pause-release.ts`); inline rules on continued documents are removed by the guard. Live HTTPS through the real extension: example.com (engine unchanged) ⇒ `secure`, `h3`, `copy:false`, `guarded:true`; wp-rocket.me with a matching Delay JS row ⇒ fulfilled, `copy:true`, `nextHopProtocol ""`, next page `neutral`; wp-rocket.me unchanged (inline conservative rules present) ⇒ `secure`/`h2`, `copy:false`, click to `/fr/` intercepted (`deliveryType "cache"`, never prefetch; record = clicked page).
  2. **Guard silent:** the observer callback is guarded too, and `getAttribute`/`querySelectorAll`/`remove` are captured from the prototypes before page scripts run (test: a page patching them after start, and a hostile node, raise nothing).
  3. **Origin-scoped guard:** `speculationRulesGuardSource(origin)` acts only where `location.origin` is the session's; seam additive `arm(tabId, generation, origin?)` / `resume(tabId, generation, origin?)` (controller passes the session/stored origin; P10E backend ignores it). One guard per tab: kept on a same-origin re-arm, removed (`Page.removeScriptToEvaluateOnNewDocument`) and re-added on an origin change; it ends with its session on detach (live: armed ⇒ JS-inserted rules gone; `localhost:8497` in the same tab ⇒ rules kept; after Stop ⇒ rules kept). The service-worker bypass and `Network.enable` stay tab-wide (the protocol has no per-origin scope) — recorded per document as `serviceWorkersBypassed` (item 5).
  4. **Monotonic ordering:** backends stamp `receivedAt` with `utils/monotonic-stamp.ts` (`timeOrigin + performance.now()`, floored, strictly increasing); the controller derives `served.order` = stamp + a per-lifetime offset fixed at the first document so the order continues past every stored one (seeded from `session:` reads like `startedAt`) — a restarted background whose clock reads behind never drops later documents (test: stored order far ahead ⇒ new documents continue after it, relative order kept). The machine compares `order`, never wall time.
  5. **`serviceWorkersBypassed`** on the served identity (Chromium true, Firefox false).
  6. (a) "already attached" ⇒ detach + attach afresh (a clean session, no stacked guard; still attached after the detach ⇒ `attach-denied`). (b) header dropped on ineligible session documents and unreadable bodies too. (c) **No query in the record:** `served.urlPath` = origin + path (≤ 2 048), `served.urlDigest` = SHA-256 hex of the whole URL without its fragment (`lib/exclusion-session/served-url.ts`; the overlay hashes `location.href` minus `#…` to compare). (d) worst-case active record 490 779 B (≈ 479 KiB, 33 KiB under the 512 KiB bound). (e) registry doc fixed.
  - **Contract (reshaped before any consumer — `served` is unreleased):** `ExclusionServedDocument { urlPath, urlDigest, token, order, receivedAt, speculationRules: { removedFromDocument, headerRemoved, guarded }, copy, serviceWorkersBypassed }`, `SERVED_DOCUMENT_LIMITS { urlPathChars: 2048, tokenChars: 128, speculationRules: 10000 }`; seam `InterceptedResponseIdentity` + `speculationGuard`, `serviceWorkersBypassed`. `headerRemoved` reports the backend's intent: a refused `continueResponse` falls back to a plain continue (header kept; logged).
  - **Budgets:** Chromium background 112.2 KiB raw ⇒ row 109 → 113; Firefox background 108.3 ⇒ 107 → 109 (digest, served contract, monotonic stamp, release module, guard bookkeeping); Firefox worker 26.4 / 28 unchanged.
  - **Changed expectations:** controller arm/resume calls now carry the origin; two document-transform pins gained `rulesOnly`; an abandoned fulfill is released once (no plain continue after "Invalid InterceptionId"); arm call-sequence pins split into unguarded (no origin) / guarded (origin); "adopts an existing session" ⇒ "replaces it (detach + attach)".
- **Final verification (2026-10-21, on `88f4d23`; no MAJOR+), fixed test-first:** (1) a fulfill that finds the request gone (detach-replacement race, abandoned navigation) or refused left a false `rewritten` record — the release now answers `fulfilled | refused | gone` and the backend calls the new host method `documentNotServed(tabId, url, identity?)`, which records `failed{not-served}` (additive reason) with the same identity — same order, so it replaces only its own response's record, never a later page's; the Firefox stream backend reports the same when writing the host's rewrite fails (its pre-record abandonment stays `abandoned?`). (2) The guard sweeps each inserted node in its own try/catch (test: a throwing node then a rule in one mutation record ⇒ the rule still removed, no page error; a no-op `finally` fails it). (3) Wake order seeding from every tab's records pinned (another tab's stored order far ahead ⇒ the next document continues after it; removing the seed fails it). (4) Overlay contract (also in `ExclusionServedDocument` TSDoc): `u = new URL(location.href); u.hash = ''; hex = lowercase hex SHA-256(UTF-8(u.href))`, compare with `served.urlDigest`; after `history.pushState`/`replaceState` a mismatch means "can't tell", not "not intercepted"; low-entropy query digests are guessable ("no raw query stored", not "query private"); URLs with embedded credentials unverified; the guard can be disabled by a hostile page for itself (patching `RegExp.prototype.test` / NodeList iteration — only its own prefetches come back).
- **Unverified / owner manual checks:** infobar **Cancel** — _passed, owner 2026-10-21 (feedback round below)_; `DeveloperToolsAvailability = 2` (managed policy; expect `debugging-policy`) — _deferred, check later (owner 2026-10-21; how-to in the feedback round)_; background restart while attached (adoption path unit-tested; Chromium holds a debugger keepalive on the worker, `debugger_api.cc`, so it is not a normal path); a session after a renderer crash; guard copies stacking on an adopted session after a background restart (harmless: idempotent); `Network`-event cost on very heavy pages (measured only on the local site).

### P10E — Firefox streaming backend ✅

Depends:P10A, P10D.0. Parallel:P10D. Goal: a `filterResponseData` backend implementing the P10D.0 seam (same engine, same session semantics by construction).

- [x] `InterceptionBackend` + registry row (detection by `'filterResponseData' in browser.webRequest`): blocking main-frame `onBeforeRequest` over `URL_PATTERNS`, registered synchronously at background top level, gated per request; `arm`/`resume`/`disarm` over that per-tab bookkeeping; builder-composed patterns port-stripped (P10A finding 2). **The Firefox build is MV2** (verified: `.output/firefox-mv2/manifest.json` `manifest_version: 2`, `background.scripts` with no `persistent: false` ⇒ a persistent background page — no suspension/wake; state is lost only on browser restart or extension reload/update). Permissions: MV2 needs `webRequest` + `webRequestBlocking` + host permissions (MDN); MV3 would additionally need `webRequestFilterResponse` (Firefox 110+, MDN). MDN does not say whether `filterResponseData` may be called after an `await` inside a blocking `onBeforeRequest` (its example calls it synchronously) ⇒ verify, or keep a synchronous in-memory armed-tab set for the gate and let `host.rewriteDocument` apply the origin + eligibility gates (an unarmed origin's document is then just written through).
- [x] Streaming filter: buffer ≤ `DOCUMENT_BODY_BYTE_LIMIT` (overflow → write through + `host.documentBodyUnavailable`), `onstop` → `host.rewriteDocument` → write once → close/error exactly once (old stream-cleanup defect class closed); status/method/`Content-Type` from `onHeadersReceived` for eligibility + the charset step (ineligible ⇒ write through, unrecorded); own release deadline above `INTERCEPTION_HOST_BOUNDS_MS.rewriteDocument` ⇒ write the buffered bytes through.
- [x] Preempting runner: background-page worker entry calling `handleTransformWorkerMessage`, driven by `createWorkerTransformRunner` (new worker bundle + budgets row).
- [x] Verify Firefox reload semantics (unverified): temporary add-on reload fires `onInstalled` `update` and/or clears `session:` (the P10D.0 reset relies on one of them; Chromium's `session:` clearing on reload/update is documented).
- [x] Unit tests with injected stream shims; manual runtime evidence on the docker Firefox build.

DoD: parity apply on Firefox; cleanup contract proven; `pnpm build:firefox` green. Effort M · difficulty medium.

#### P10E receipt (2026-10-20)

Implemented `94fee5f` on `feat/p10e-firefox-backend` (from `feat/p10` @ e7f8ca9); audit fixes uncommitted on top (audit line below). Files: `lib/exclusion-session/stream-machine.ts` (pure per-response machine) + `stream-backend.ts` (backend over injected filter/host/timer ports), each + test; `entrypoints/background/stream-filter-backend.ts` (+test: the row — feature detection, four `webRequest` hooks, worker runner); `entrypoints/exclusion-transform-worker.ts` (**new unlisted-script entrypoint**, `include: ['firefox']` — worker → `handleTransformWorkerMessage`); `utils/last-error.ts` (+test). Edited: `interception-backends.ts` (the row), `wxt.config.ts` (`webRequestBlocking`, Firefox only), `lib/exclusion-session/{backend,controller,timeouts}.ts` (additive seam arg + stream bounds), `lib/messaging/tab-send.ts` + 4 `lastError` readers, `scripts/check-budgets.mjs` (+test: per-row `build`), `scripts/budgets.json`, `exclusion-sessions.test.ts`. AGENTS.md (supervisor): new entrypoint, `utils/last-error`, budgets `build` field.

- **Design.** Gate = synchronous in-memory armed table (tab → generation): `arm` sets/re-tags (a different generation withdraws the tab's in-flight streams), `disarm` deletes + withdraws, `resume` = true only for a tab this background armed — a fresh background has an empty table, so every stored session is stale at start. All settle synchronously ⇒ "latest wins" trivially; `resume` awaits nothing. Hooks (`URL_PATTERNS`, `main_frame`, registered in `create`): blocking `onBeforeRequest` → filter for armed tabs only (a redirect re-entering with the same `requestId` withdraws the earlier filter); blocking `onHeadersReceived` (+`responseHeaders`) → facts by `requestId`; non-blocking `onErrorOccurred` → `failed`, `onCompleted` → `completed`. `stream-machine` (`opening → buffering → settling → [awaiting-facts] → rewriting → done`): facts last-write-wins, only a final status (not 1xx/3xx) decides; withdrawals (abort, gate denied, network failure) latch before start, end the stream unmodified after; the body is rewritten only once the request completed (before or after `onstop`), a failure serves it unmodified + `documentBodyUnavailable`, no completion within `STREAM_SETTLE_WAIT_MS` (1 000) ⇒ trusted (bounded fallback); longest hold declared as `STREAM_HOLD_WORST_CASE_MS` (settle + facts + release); release deadline host bound + `STREAM_RELEASE_MARGIN_MS`; late facts wait `STREAM_FACTS_WAIT_MS` (all in `timeouts.ts`); one timer per stream, stale waits ignored. Pinned: full 13-state × 17-event matrix (next state incl. withdrawal reason + ordered effects) and guarantees over every sequence ≤ 3 events + 8 000 seeded random ones (ended once unless the filter failed, bytes written through in order unless served rewritten, withdrawn ⇒ never rewritten, rewrite only after completion/fallback with the latest final facts, ≤ 1 report, eligible-only, ≤ 8 MiB, nothing before start). Seam (additive): `rewriteDocument(document, abandoned?)` — the controller re-checks `abandoned()` right before recording, so a stream ended unmodified (deadline, failure, filter error, withdrawal) never leaves `rewritten` recorded; `rewriting` + filter error now reports `body-unavailable`. Runner: `createWorkerTransformRunner` over `new Worker(browser.runtime.getURL('/exclusion-transform-worker.js'))` (classic worker); no `Worker` ⇒ in-process (logged). No match pattern is composed (P10A finding 2: nothing to strip). **Divergence vs CDP:** nothing ends a Firefox session when the tab leaves http(s) (no attachment ⇒ `interceptionEnded` never called; the session persists to the next http(s) document — G-5 Stop/tab close/reload still end it); CDP ends it `unsupported-page`. Not done (optional): registering the blocking hooks only while a tab is armed.
- **Verified Firefox facts** (Firefox 158.0.1, headless, temporary add-on over RDP; scratch probe add-on + fixture server, `.scratch/p10e-firefox/`): (1) `filterResponseData` after an `await` in a blocking `onBeforeRequest` works (50/500 ms + `storage.local`); sync table kept anyway. (2) A non-blocking `onHeadersReceived` fired ≈ 200 ms **after** `onstop`; with a blocking one, headers preceded `onstart` in every arm. (3) `disconnect()` before `onstart` throws `NS_ERROR_FAILURE`. (4) Bodies arrive decoded (gzip); output longer than `Content-Length` renders whole ⇒ no header fix-up. (5) A 3xx's filter fails `Invalid request ID`; `onBeforeRequest` re-fires with the same id. (6) A conditional request (ETag fixture: server logged `304`; docker `/fdt/` normal reload) reports `onHeadersReceived` `304` then the cached `200` before `onstart` (audit B1). (7) Truncation: a dropped connection ends the filter with `onstop` (partial body). `onErrorOccurred` `NS_ERROR_NET_PARTIAL_TRANSFER` came in the same tick (chunked) or **≈ 25 ms after** `onstop` and after a `setTimeout(0)` (identity `Content-Length`) — a one-macrotask settle is not enough; `onCompleted` arrived before the filter was closed (before or ≈ 10 ms after `onstop`) in every run with a delayed close — the earlier "waits for close" reading came from probes that closed at `onstop`. A truncated **gzip** body reports `onCompleted` (undetectable via webRequest). (8) `Worker` exists in the MV2 background page; a classic worker from the extension's URL runs WXT's unlisted-script wrapper fine. (9) Temporary add-on reload fires `onInstalled {reason: 'update', temporary: true}` **and** clears `storage.session`. (10) Built manifest: MV2 `background.scripts`, `persistentBackgroundScript: true`. (11) Firefox sets `runtime.lastError` to `null` after a successful callback (audit M2).
- **Runtime evidence** (built `.output/firefox-mv2`, RDP ports 6125/6126, fixtures 8897; apply/stop via `runtime.sendMessage` from the tab's top-frame content-script context — real controller + sender rules; logs `.scratch/p10e-firefox/{audit-before,audit-after,product-evidence-full,latency}.log`): docker `/fdt/?fdt-delay=1` row `fdt-delayed-lifecycle` ⇒ served live + executed, `rewritten`, persisted across navigation, Stop ⇒ unmodified + record cleared; charset UTF-8 + windows-1252 round-trip; tab closed mid-body ⇒ record removed; 9 MiB ⇒ whole page + `body-unavailable`; 302 ⇒ final rewritten; `text/plain` untouched/unrecorded; `.*jquery` vs 100 KiB inline script (6.1 s uninterrupted) ⇒ reload 2 272 ms, `over-budget{delay_js, '.*jquery', 2000}`, background answering in 1–2 ms; extension reload ⇒ `session:` empty, next load unmodified. Audit arms, pre-fix build `94fee5f` → fixed: ETag fixture normal reload ×2 + re-navigation unmodified (record still `rewritten`) → rewritten each time; docker cached `/fdt/` row `navigation/view` same (delayed → live `type="module"`); truncated identity body rewritten + `rewritten` recorded → served unmodified (script still delayed) + `failed{body-unavailable}`; background `chrome.tabs.sendMessage(getFdtData)` never resolved (3 s) → resolved `{res}`. Completion wait cost: docker `/fdt/` loading→complete unarmed 25–55 ms vs armed 42–75 ms (fallback never hit).
- **Gates:** compile/lint/format green; `pnpm test` 218 files, 4 129 green (+1 skipped), coverage 98.31/96.68/100/98.98, new modules 100 %; `build` + `check:budgets` + `build:firefox` green. Budgets: Chromium package carries none of the backend nor the worker (no `*transform*` file; `background.js` 92.0 KiB, +0.1 from the `lastError` fix shared by every surface; isolated content script 121.6 / 122); new Firefox rows (`build: "firefox-mv2"`, skipped while that build is absent; CI now runs `build:firefox` before `check:budgets`): background page 102.3 / 106 KiB raw, engine worker 24.4 / 26 raw, 8.8 / 10 gzip. After merging `feat/p10` (1382e04, work-bounded `regex-risk.ts`): worker 24.4 → 26.4 KiB raw (+2 092 B), 8.8 → 9.6 gzip — the whole delta is `regex-risk.ts` (pre-merge tree + only the merged `regex-risk.ts` reproduces the worker byte-for-byte); ceilings raised to 28 / 11 (~1.5 KiB headroom, like the other rows); Firefox background 102.3 → 104.4 / 106 (+2 201 B: `regex-risk.ts` 2 099 B + `timeouts.ts` 102 B) — ceiling kept.
- **Deviations:** (a) the registry row is gated at build time (`import.meta.env.BROWSER === 'firefox'` — under WXT's vitest plugin the env booleans are truthy strings; feature detection still selects at run time). (b) `exclusion-sessions.test.ts` "ships an empty backend registry" → Chromium-build exclusion + a Firefox-build inclusion test (`vi.stubEnv`). (c) Blocking `onHeadersReceived`; `onCompleted` consumed (audit M3 policy said settle one macrotask — evidence (7) showed it misses identity truncations; the completion signal is deterministic where it arrives, with a bounded fallback).
- **Audit (adversarial, 2026-10-20) — all fixed test-first** (each regression failed on `94fee5f`): B1 a 304 latched `passing`, every revalidated document served unmodified with a stale `rewritten` record → facts last-write-wins, final statuses only decide, withdrawal reasons separate (live: ETag + cached docker `/fdt/`); M2 `tab-send.ts` read `.message` on Firefox's `null` `lastError` ⇒ successful tab sends never resolved → `utils/last-error.ts` (`undefined`/`null` = unset) used by all 5 readers (`tab-send`, context menus, devtools panel + lazyload sidebar, inspected-scripts; `inspected-page-url` was already null-safe) — the only `lastError` comparisons in the repo; M3 truncated body rewritten + recorded → `onErrorOccurred`/`onCompleted` signals, `failed` ⇒ original + close + report, late host answer ignored; host-consistency: additive `abandoned` arg (controller test: an abandoned rewrite records nothing); m4 mutants (timer cancel on re-arm and on done, redirect supersede, withdrawal table deletion, plus completion/settle/abandon/withdrawal mutants) all killed — the one survivor (`verdict === 'ineligible'` vs `!isRewritable` after the final-status guard) is equivalent; matrix now pins full next state; Firefox-build registry test; m5 worker out of the Chromium package (Firefox-only unlisted script) + Firefox budget rows; notes: stream bounds moved to `timeouts.ts`, `DONE` TSDoc, divergence documented. Test expectations changed (intended): stream timer pins (settle wait, `timer` events), worker spawn path, the registry test (b).
- **Audit round 2 (verification of `48079d9`, 2026-10-20) — minors, fixed test-first where code:** (1) truncated gzip + `Content-Length` still rewritten (`onCompleted`, no error) — no reliable signal: `onCompleted` details are identical to a whole response (`fromCache: false`, `responseSize: 0`, `requestSize: 0` on every load, Firefox 158), decoded bytes cannot be compared with the encoded length; no `</html>` heuristic ⇒ **known residual** (TSDoc + below); WP Rocket's precompressed cache files are served this way. (2) settle fallback 250 → 1 000 ms (late completion 238 ms at a cold start, failure 60 ms under load); `STREAM_HOLD_WORST_CASE_MS` declared, release timer armed only after settling (pinned); live re-run: armed `/fdt/` 41–60 ms vs unarmed 28–46 ms (fallback not hit), truncation still `body-unavailable`, revalidation still rewritten. (3) CI: `pnpm build:firefox` step before `check:budgets` (YAML validated), AGENTS CI line updated; the per-row decision extracted as `measureRow` with skip/absent/measured tests. (4) `view-source:` of an armed tab: verified not detectable — its request reaches every hook with the plain URL and the same details as a normal load, and without `tabs` the tab's `view-source:` URL is hidden ⇒ documented (source view shows the rewritten HTML and records it). (5) service-worker-served navigations: the filter fails `Invalid request ID`, the document passes unmodified and unrecorded while the session shows active; MDN `filterResponseData` documents no service-worker bypass (only the `webRequestFilterResponse.serviceWorkerScript` permission for SW **script** loads) ⇒ documented; the backend records nothing for such loads, so P10D's per-document identity in the record can surface "not intercepted" without backend changes.

(2) The completion-before-close ordering is observed, not specified (bounded 1 s fallback). (2b) `view-source:` loads of an armed tab are rewritten and recorded; service-worker-served navigations are silently not intercepted (see round 2). (3) Eligible-document filter `onerror` and the facts-wait path not reproducible live (unit-pinned). (4) Streaming/never-ending documents held to completion or 8 MiB. ### P10F — Overlay UI in the inspected page ✅

Depends:P10B+C (+G-3/G-4 decisions). Parallel:P10D/P10E wiring-free parts. Goal: the whole builder UX, shadow-isolated, per the UI bar.

- [x] Mount/teardown via the shared injected-UI machinery (promoted to `components/injected-ui/`); tokens + live theme; top frame only; z-index/cleanup contract for hostile pages. **Location deviation:** a runtime content script `entrypoints/exclusion-overlay.content/`, not `isolated.content/exclusion-builder/` (lazy loading, below).
- [x] Editor: per-feature pattern/literal rows with inline validation, disable toggles (G-4), counts from the engine report; virtualized always (P-2: one code path).
- [x] Click-to-pick + "On this page" list (keyboard path; the only path to scripts) → candidate row in the right lane; Esc cancels; SPA-safe (debounced rescan, observer cleanup).
- [x] State matrix (S-1) incl. the G-3 warning per cause, "not intercepted" and "can't verify"; indicator = word + glyph (K-5).
- [x] Apply/Reset/Stop through the injected session port; keyboard-complete (K-1/K-2).
- [x] UI bar §D receipts below.

DoD: full editor + states green in isolation (engine + contracts injected), bar receipts recorded. Effort L · difficulty hard.

#### P10F receipt (2026-10-21)

Commits on `feat/p10f-overlay` (from `feat/p10` @ `7310f78`): `6710298` promote mount + live theme to `components/injected-ui/` · `f4a5bd0` pure logic · `a21577a` overlay UI + injector + `scripting` · `ff309e3` dead-context stop, banner locks, stop notice, budgets · `901f654` event isolation · docs commit.

- **Lazy loading (the design driver).** WXT bundles content scripts as IIFE, so a dynamic `import()` is inlined — no lazy chunk. Alternatives: `import()` of a web-accessible module (exposes the JS to every page, IIFE→module handoff through isolated-world globals, Firefox behavior unverified) vs a **runtime-registered content script** (`registration: 'runtime'`, WXT-native: own context, own `cssInjectionMode: 'ui'` stylesheet) injected with `scripting.executeScript({target: {tabId, frameIds: [0]}, files})`. Chosen: the latter. Cost: the **`scripting` permission** (both builds; no install warning; acts within the existing host permissions; MDN: Firefox exposes `scripting` in MV2). `entrypoints/background/exclusion-overlay.ts` `openExclusionOverlay(tabId) → injected|unsupported-page|tab-gone|failed` (never rejects) is the opener P10G wires (context menu); **no product caller in P10F** (no surface integration) — verification injected the same file from the service worker. Re-injection replaces the running instance (WXT's newer-context invalidation). Isolated content script unchanged by the overlay itself (124 478 B vs 124 483 B base); later rounds add the shared debounce max wait and the page-key guard (receipts below).
- **Isolation contract.** One shadow root (host appended to `<html>`), everything inside one `popover="manual"` layer shown in the **top layer** (above any page z-index/transform; fixed `z-index` max fallback), px sizes (pages own `rem`), layer `pointer-events: none` except panel/pill/glass. **Events:** every Solid-delegated event + keypress/wheel/clipboard/cancel stops at the shadow root and Solid delegates there (`delegateEvents(DelegatedEvents, shadowRoot)`) — finding: on the local WP site the Delay JS runtime saves first interactions at `window` and replays them on `composedPath()[0]`, so a first click on an overlay toggle ran twice (list opened then closed); fixed + verified. Page is only read (scan, geometry); highlight drawn in the overlay. **Dead context:** WXT detects invalidation only when something checks; the overlay checks on page mutations, `visibilitychange` and pointer presses → removed (verified after `runtime.reload()`). Window capture-phase page listeners still see events retargeted to the host (unpreventable).
- **Styles.** Hand-written shadow CSS on the tokens (the injected-UI precedent): Tailwind in a shadow root needs its `@property` rules registered on the page document (WXT's split injects them into `<head>`) — a page leak. `components/ui` primitives are Tailwind/Kobalte-classed, so only `VirtualList` is reused (`class="xo-scroller"` carries its geometry). Promotion candidates: an injected-UI button/switch/chip set (`overlay.css` `.xo-*`), and a `VirtualList` without `tailwind-merge` (its `cn` costs this chunk ~22 KiB minified; panel callers would need checking).
- **Logic (pure, injected, tested):** `session/machine` (loading · load-failed · ready{status, pending, notice} · reloading{stalled}; 11 event types; full matrix pinned: 11 representative states × 13 events; a Stop ignores the status push that precedes its own answer); `session/port` (interface + messaging adapter over the P10C channels with their deadlines/guards; `context-invalidated` named apart); `session/page-match` (digest contract via `utils/sha256` — sync, because `crypto.subtle` is secure-context-only and content scripts on `http:` pages lack it; also compares the navigation entry's URL, so a history-API move still verifies; a matching record received > 2 s before `timeOrigin` → "can't verify"); `session/session-view`; `exclusions/draft` (stable row ids, blank rows = unfinished, per-row validation memoized); `exclusions/pick-target` (lane + row per element; rows proven against the real engine transforms); `exclusions/page-scan`; `banner-model`; `copy`; `controller` (autosave 800 ms via `saveExclusionSet`, background `invalid` reasons mapped to row ids, reload-stall exit after 8 s).
- **Decision to flag (owner/supervisor):** the port's production implementation is the thin messaging adapter over the existing P10C channels (they were registered by P10D.0), so Apply/Stop/Reset are real in the browser. P10G keeps: re-injection after the reload, the surfaces (context menu, panel row, palette removal), the mode registry, end-to-end receipts.
- **Words vs the reference builder (W-2):** "Enter exclusions (one per line)" textareas → one input per row (multi-line paste splits); "Enable/Disable Delay…" buttons → "Disable feature" switches; "Apply changes and exclusions to the HTML file" → "Apply" / "Apply changes" / "Re-apply"; "Reset HTML File to original state" → "Reset" (discard edits, re-apply the applied set); new: Stop, every state banner, cause/remedy words per `INTERCEPTION_UNAVAILABLE_CAUSES` arm. Feature names from `lib/detection/labels`.
- **§D receipts** (built extension, CFT via Playwright 1.64.1 `channel: 'chromium'` headless; scratch fixture page with a hostile header — `z-index` max, transformed `<body>`, `html {font-size: 62.5%}`, `* {box-sizing: content-box !important}` — plus the local WP site `/fdt/`; screenshots in the session scratchpad `p10f/shots/`):
  - D-1: 320 / 480 / 1280 viewports, light + dark: panel body `scrollWidth === clientWidth` (286/422/422), 0 horizontally overflowing descendants; every control reachable.
  - D-2: system/dark/light; live switch while mounted (`system` → `dark`, no reload); reduced motion emulated → `shadowRoot.getAnimations()` empty after mount.
  - D-3: keyboard-only Tab walk visits every control in reading order with a visible 2 px outline; Escape collapses to the pill (focus on the pill), Enter reopens (focus on the `h1`); Escape cancels picking; row keys Enter/Backspace/ArrowUp/ArrowDown/paste.
  - D-4: axe-core 4.14.0 on the overlay — 0 violations in every width × palette (populated draft with an invalid row).
  - D-5: pushed statuses (unavailable, not intercepted, armed) and the SPA rescan kept the draft rows and the open overlay (state walk); row UI state lives in the controller keyed by row id. Focus retention across a refresh was not measured separately.
  - D-6: the grep gate over the change's files is empty.
  - D-7: open → first row 50–58 ms, **0 long tasks** opening with 1 000 rows (500 Delay JS + 500 Lazyload) and while typing/scrolling them; ≤ 38 row inputs rendered.
  - Live end-to-end on the fixture: picks → Apply → CDP interception → reload → re-opened overlay shows "Applied, with rows that matched nothing", per-lane "1 of 3 delayed scripts restored", per-row "1 match"/"No match", copy/SW/prefetch notes; Stop → "Session stopped — reload…". Driven through the real push channel: unavailable (retryable / policy), not intercepted, armed.
- **Gates:** compile/lint/format green; `pnpm test` 245 files, 4 690 passed (+1 skipped), coverage 98.49/96.81/100/99.11; slowest new test < 100 ms; `build` + `build:firefox` + `check:budgets` green. **Budgets:** new row "exclusion builder overlay" 188.6 / 190 KiB raw, 63.3 / 65 gzip (solid-js, virtual-core, tailwind-merge via `VirtualList`, engine validation incl. `regex-risk`, storage/messaging); whole-build backstop 1 225 → 1 265 (1 074.1 → 1 262.7, the overlay alone); background 112.6 and isolated 121.6 unchanged.
- **Known limits / unverified:** Firefox runtime not exercised (build only; `scripting` in MV2 per MDN); `VirtualList`'s focus restore reads `ownerDocument.activeElement` (the shadow host), so a scroll-driven reorder inside the overlay does not restore focus; the pick glass forwards wheel to the window only (nested page scrollers do not scroll while picking); after Stop the page keeps the rewritten render until reloaded (said in the notice); the overlay is gone after Apply's reload until P10G re-injects it; the T-1 `h1` is 14 px (px, not `text-base`: the page owns `rem`).

- **Audit round 1 (2026-10-21) — closed** (`fd03608`, `9048aee`, `6dd0923`, `bbd7d70`, `7351f7f`, `fe611bd`, `5a4c74a`; all findings fixed test-first unless noted):
  - **Majors.** C-M1 the mount re-checks the context after the stylesheet wait (`mount-overlay.ts`; a superseded instance removes itself — verified: 20 re-injections ⇒ 1 host); C-M2 the row validator checks the wire contract first (`isWireRowText`, exported — one declaring site), excerpts cut on code points, unstorable candidates not offered, autosave never asks with a refused draft; C-M3 reopening an active session restores the saved draft (marked dirty); C-M4 rows name paths as served (raw UTF-8 or percent-encoded), bounded ids (`␠id="x"`, `\sid="x"`), lazy-render class tokens unique among stamped peers else the quoted class value, no `<tag` fallback — all proven through the real engine. UI-M1 held buttons `aria-disabled`, banner actions land focus on the heading; UI-M2 `VirtualList` reads focus from the list's own root (shadow-safe) and a press restores the previous mask before planning (panel suites unchanged); UI-M3 page modal ⇒ paused pill (also when opened under one), page popovers opened later ⇒ the overlay re-takes the top layer; UI-M4 page CSS zoom undone (`1/currentCSSZoom`); UI-M5/M6 picking without a glass: window-capture pointer/click/key listeners (wheel/touch native — app-shell scrollers verified, 1× per wheel step), Escape from anywhere, Tab kept on the picking bar, frames guarded (pointer input never reaches a frame); UI-M7 fix-rows notice leaves with the last problem, dismissals are corner close controls; UI-M8 overdue reload ⇒ status re-read, editable ready + notice (Reload/Stop/edit); UI-M9 names start with visible words, skeleton is a status with text — axe 0 violations, no name-mismatch "incomplete" (only `color-contrast` incomplete: axe cannot resolve top-layer backgrounds; the auditor's contrast script reports 0 text failures).
  - **Minors/nits.** Close ends the context (`ctx.abort`; open/close ×20: DOM nodes/listeners flat); a push during the load re-reads status; edits ignored while an action runs; page-match: document URL only when known, record age windows ≤ 100 ms this page / > 2 s other load / between can't tell, `served.receivedAt` now wall clock in the session core (monotonic stamp keeps ordering — contract doc updated); `classifyInjectionFailure` case-insensitive, outcome `injected` (the opener cannot see the mount); `utils/debounce` `maxWaitMs` (rescan ≤ 2 s under endless churn); scans sliced ≤ 8 ms per task (3 000-section page: 124–139 ms long tasks ⇒ none); pointer picks look through 8 layers; script text read lazily, first 512 chars; layer sized by insets (off the page scrollbar); single scroller until a list passes 8 (lanes) / 6 (candidates) rows; candidate rows 36 px + hover; header host-only origin, aligned badge column, full row text on hover; Reset held when clean; visible pick-miss/frame message; live regions only on words; input/switch boundaries on `--fg-muted` (≥ 5:1); `[data-fdt-tokens]` re-declares the token values inside the shadow tree (page `*{--token}` rules no longer recolor it; values unchanged); arrow moves accumulate (12 frames); 16 px heading, no programmatic ring; primary hover; patterns in reasons as code; "5000+" when capped; budgets em-dash churn reverted; constant-equals-literal test dropped; non-retryable hint; top-layer failure logged; `auxclick` + composition events isolated; bundle −14 % (`tailwind-merge` out of `VirtualList`).

- **Final verification round (2026-10-21) — closed** (`2f3e926`, `a7553bd`, `09b9a62`, `05d4d09`, `0617123` + this one; each new test checked to fail with its fix reverted):
  - **MAJOR-1 (Firefox keys dead)** — Firefox ignores `composed: false` for constructed `KeyboardEvent`s (also page-made ones; `Event`/`UIEvent`/`InputEvent`/`CompositionEvent`/`ClipboardEvent` honor it — probed in Firefox 158), so the copy escaped, re-entered the guard and never reached the overlay. The copy is now a plain non-composed `Event` carrying the read fields as own properties (visible to the overlay's content-script world in both engines); a copy the engine would compose anyway is never sent (the original goes on), and an escaped one is let through, never re-copied (`WeakSet`). Tests: Firefox-shaped `KeyboardEvent` stub, an always-composing stub, a lying-`composed` stub, key aimed at the host itself (also NIT-2's surviving `target === host` mutant). Live Firefox 158 (BiDi `input.performActions`, keyhog page): `a/b` typed, Backspace, Enter ⇒ row 2, ArrowUp/ArrowDown move, Escape collapses, page keys `[]`. Chromium (CFT): no-overlay identity (type/edit/IME/clipboard/repeat+dead/contenteditable/other shadow/iframe all identical), overlay walk 16/16 steps with 0 page events, clipboard copy/cut/paste unchanged.
  - **MINOR-1 (Firefox guard after head scripts)** — not reproduced once MAJOR-1 was fixed: on reload and fresh navigation an inline `<head>` script's probe is already guarded (`pageSaw=false`; control with an unguarded host name `pageSaw=true`); only a page loaded before the add-on was installed lacks it. The earlier reading came from the broken copy failing open. Kept as a P10H re-check on the real flow.
  - **MINOR-2** an `&` image path's `&`-free stretch (longest first, ≥ 4 chars) must be in no other lazy image's tag, else no row (`a&amp;b.jpg` next to `thumb.jpg` ⇒ none; engine-proven, also the next-longest stretch case); the page's lazy image tags are read once, on first need. **MINOR-3** a multi-line paste splits the row at the input's selection like a text editor (text before/after kept; cap trims pasted lines only; live: `日本(` + paste ⇒ `日本(one.js`, `two.js`, `three.js`). **NIT-2** lane `focusin` clear pinned (pending move + focus elsewhere ⇒ next arrow from the focused row).
  - **Pre-merge NITs** (`0d13831`, `015aa16`): copy fields `isComposing`/`clipboardData` pinned (each test fails with its field removed); copies of one `&` image (same `data-lazy-src`) no longer block its stretch (duplicate pair ⇒ 1 row, 2 matches; `a&amp;b.jpg` vs `thumb.jpg` still none); tests 4 769 passed, overlay 172.8 / 58.6, whole-build backstop 1 248.5 → 1 248.6 (measured 1 248.5).
  - **Not fixed: NIT-1** — the lazy-render fallback ` id="x"` matches 0 when the page serves `id='x'` (or unquoted). Lazy-render rows are literal substrings of the served tag, and the DOM always serializes double quotes, so no single literal holds for both quotings and the served one is unknowable from the page. Offered only when the bare value is shared with a stamped peer; the post-Apply per-row count shows "No match". P10H/P10G option: the background knows the served text — a served-tag lookup could pick the right quoting.

### P10G — Chromium apply wiring + surfaces integration ✅

Depends:P10D+F. Parallel:—. Goal: the agreed flow live end-to-end.

- [x] Overlay Apply → background session → reload → fulfilled render → overlay re-injects and shows applied state (from persisted session state, not heuristics). Re-injected after the load (`tabs.onUpdated` `complete`), not at `document_start` — owner-confirmed 2026-10-21 (receipt).
- [x] Shell mode registry: Exclusions mode (P10) plus the plug-in seam for P13/P14 tool modes (config + results panels); no second editor surface per tool.
- [x] Mode matrix wired: attach attempted at Apply → armed (interception) or the G-3 warning state (cause + remedy + Retry); indicator + honest words (W-1); session persistence per G-5 reflected in the active-state indicator.
- [x] DevTools panel: builder entry/status row (link + active state via push channel); context-menu "Exclusion Builder" item opens the overlay (re-points the existing `contextMenus` consumer comment). **Remove the panel command-palette "Open Exclusion Builder" row** (`entrypoints/panel/palette/actions.ts` + `palette/exclusion-builder.ts`) and retire the old unlisted `exclusion-builder.html` entrypoint + its budgets row (G-1 amendment 1) — from a devtools-attached panel the overlay is the only correct surface; no second editor surface.
- [x] E2E-adjacent manual receipts on the docker site; budgets: content-script + background deltas receipted. _Docker daemon was down: receipts ran on a scratch fixture server instead — receipt._

DoD: owner flow "pick → apply → reload → real render with exclusions" works on Chromium; statuses honest in every mode. Effort M · difficulty medium.

#### P10G receipt (2026-10-21)

Commits on `feat/p10g-apply` (from `feat/p10` @ `57d45f4`): `00f9ab2` retire tab flow + palette row · `306fb06` re-open core + contract rows · `0ee7934` background re-open + channels · `8fd35a4` panel entry · `9e4def2` mode registry · `2a8fcfc` persistence note · `b003308` panel summary contract · `f58e530` explicit open resets the fold · `39e7d3f`/`0a4d58c` budgets · `0365d7b` reduced-motion focus fix · docs commit.

- **Re-open (`lib/exclusion-session/overlay-reopen.ts`, `entrypoints/background/exclusion-overlay.ts`).** On `tabs.onUpdated` `status: 'complete'` (tab URL readable through the host permissions, no `tabs`/`webNavigation` permission), the background reads the tab's record through the controller (`tabStatus`: after the wake re-validation, queued behind the tab's events; unreadable ⇒ nothing opens) and re-opens only for an **active** session whose origin is the page's. One overlay per document via the isolated-world overlay slot (claim/live handle — superseded the first tag-name probe; see the audit rounds). The overlay then renders the applied state from the record + the served-document digest (P10F `page-match`, unchanged). _Why not `document_start`:_ without `webNavigation` (install warning "Read your browsing history") there is no commit signal that targets the new document; `tabs.onUpdated` `loading` may precede the commit. Measured: overlay up 38 ms after `load`.
- **Presentation.** Re-opens come back the way the user left the overlay (open panel / folded pill; close = folded, so a running session's indicator stays visible until Stop); the overlay reports fold/unfold/close (`exclusionOverlayPresented`, sender-resolved top frame) and an explicit open reports open (resets an older fold — found live). Hand-off: the slot claim carries the presentation (superseded the first `__wprFdtOverlayPresentation` global). A re-open never takes focus from the page; an explicit open focuses the heading. Remembered per tab in `session:` (superseded the first memory-only map; audit rounds).
- **Explicit opens** (context menu, panel) — _superseded by the audit round below: a live overlay is revealed, never replaced._ Context menu: same `action`-context entry, id renamed `exclusionBuilder`, opens the overlay on the clicked tab. Panel: `openExclusionOverlay({tabId})` answered only for `isExtensionPageSender` (like `getExclusionSessionForTab`).
- **Panel entry** (`entrypoints/panel/status-bar/builder-entry.tsx` + pure `builder-session.ts`): trailing status-strip control — "Exclusions" + badge Off/On/Unavailable (none while checking or when unread), words in `title` + accessible name ("On" = the record says active; whether _this_ page was rewritten stays the overlay's verdict), pushes scoped to the inspected tab and tab-less senders, an answer never overrides an earlier push; click ⇒ open ⇒ toast (success, or the remedy per outcome); held outside DevTools. `StatusBar` gained a `trailing` slot outside its answers' boundary (four answers unchanged). Panel validates a **summary** (`lib/messaging/exclusion-panel.ts`: state + active origin, tolerant) — the full contract cost the panel ~5 KiB more.
- **Mode registry** (`entrypoints/exclusion-overlay.content/modes.ts`): `OverlayMode { id, label, Config, Results | null }` + `OverlayModeContext` (controller, page tools, panel-open, pick with mode labels, highlight, announce, frame/virtualizer seams). Exclusions = one row (`exclusions/mode.tsx`, moved unchanged; results inline). Decision: no mode switch until a second row exists (adding one = row + switch UI, a local change).
- **Mode matrix / W-1 / G-5.** Apply → attach attempted (P10D) → `applying` (reload, re-open, "Applied"/no-match/unmodified/not-intercepted/can't-verify) or `unavailable` (cause + remedy + Retry when retryable) — P10F's states, now reached end to end. G-5: every active banner leads with "The session stays on in this tab until you stop it or close the tab: each page of this site you open here loads with these exclusions."; panel "On" words say the same.
- **Retired:** palette row + `palette/exclusion-builder.ts` (+test), `EXCLUSION_BUILDER_PAGE`, the never-built `exclusion builder JS` budgets row (no `exclusion-builder.html` existed in this port — nothing to delete in `entrypoints/`); AGENTS tree, UI bar L-2 (change-log line), textarea doc, e2e menu-id list updated.
- **NIT-1 evaluated — not fixed (unsound cheaply):** the served HTML is not kept anywhere (the record holds digests only); keeping it would hold up to 8 MiB per tab in background memory for a corner case, and a re-fetch is a new request (dynamic pages differ, side effects). Stays a known limit; the post-Apply per-row "No match" shows it.
- **Found + fixed (P10F carry-over):** under `prefers-reduced-motion`, Enter on the pill opened the panel with focus on the page body (the global 0.01 ms transition delays the panel's visibility a frame) — `focusSoon` now re-tries ≤ 3 frames.

- **Audit round 3 (2026-10-21, on `2e1bef2`; e2e 197/197) — fixed test-first** (`fc3b7cb`, `5f0f2b6`, `ddf66b7`, `44cf38d` + budgets/docs):
  - **R1 clock domains (verified):** records are stamped by the background's wall clock; the page compared them with `performance.timeOrigin` (+ `responseEnd`) on the renderer's monotonic timeline. MDN `Performance.now()` (read 2026-10-21): "The specification (Level 2) requires that `performance.now()` should tick when the operating system sleeps … It appears that only browsers on Windows keep ticking during sleep" (crbug.com/1206450, bugzil.la/1709767), and "`Date.now()` may have been impacted by system and user clock adjustments, clock skew"; MDN `timeOrigin`: translated timestamps "may be offset by the duration of the sleep" — so the domains can sit minutes apart. Fix: `navigationStart = Date.now() − performance.now()` read in one instant (`readPageFacts`, wall-clock seam), anchored at the first read and kept for the document (a re-read after a sleep would shift it); the age check and the response-window bound both use it. Residual: a sleep between the load and an explicit open's first read shifts the anchor. Tests: timeline ±10 min / 0 off ⇒ an honest record stays "this-page", the N1 repro stays "can't verify"; the anchor survives a simulated hour of sleep.
  - **R2 late stamps:** both backends read the wall clock at arrival with the order stamp (Chromium on `Fetch.requestPaused`, Firefox at request start) and pass it as the identity's additive `wallAt`; the host records it (own clock only without one) — a body-unavailable or not-served record written after the release keeps the arrival time. The rewritten path stamped before the fulfil already; it now records the arrival too.
  - **R3** a reveal of a still-mounting overlay answers `pending` (background ⇒ `injected`), `revealed` only once on screen.
  - **Nit** the bare-string presentation shape (this branch only) is dropped — a stored value is `{presentation, at}` or junk.
  - **Changed expectations:** backend identity pins gain `wallAt`; the mid-mount explicit open expects `pending`; the tolerant-read test reads a bare value as absent; fixtures `timeOrigin` → `navigationStart`.
  - **Budgets (bytes; before = `2e1bef2`, rows = measured + 0.1 KiB):** Chromium background 121 559 → 121 631 (row 118.8 → 118.9); overlay 179 302 → 179 467, gzip 59.42 KiB (rows 175.2 → 175.4, gzip 59.4 → 59.6; both builds); Firefox background 117 510 → 117 549 (row 114.9 kept); panel 336 948 unchanged; whole build 1 317 063 → 1 317 300 (backstop 1 286.3 → 1 286.6).
  - **Gates:** compile/lint/format green; `pnpm test` 263 files, 5 155 passed (+1 skipped), coverage 98.52/96.92/100/99.18; `build` + `build:firefox` + `check:budgets` green; D-6 grep gate empty.
  - **Live (CFT 156 headless, fixture `:8961`):** apply → reload → "Applied" (also `/next`, and close → explicit open → apply); fold → pill "Applied"; other origin none; Stop → none; audit harness (two opens, reveal with unsaved row, spoof, history moves, rapid navigations, modal) unchanged; bfcache restore inside a session ⇒ "Can’t verify". A real sleep/clock-drift run was not possible here (unit-pinned).

- **Audit round 4 (2026-10-21, on `9e49e79`; e2e 197/197) — fixed test-first** (`daba983`, `80482d6` + budgets/docs):
  - **Open after sleep:** the round-3 anchor was read at the overlay's first read, so an explicit open after a system sleep moved it by the sleep ⇒ "not intercepted". Now the always-on isolated content script (built manifest: `run_at: document_start`, top frame only — no `all_frames`) records `Date.now() − performance.now()` once into the extension's isolated world (`lib/exclusion-session/navigation-anchor.ts`); the scripting-injected overlay shares that world and uses it (verified live, CFT 156: injected code read the value — equal to its own `Date.now() − performance.now()` to the ms — the page's main world saw `undefined`). Fallback (a page loaded before the extension started): the read-at-mount anchor, and the page match never concludes "not intercepted" from timing alone (`anchoredAtStart` false ⇒ a record that only looks too old reads "can't verify"; a sleep can only move that anchor later, so a false "this page" is practically unreachable — it needs a page loaded before the extension started, a sleep within a window ≈ `responseEnd` + 200 ms wide, and a newer record of the same URL; a different URL's record still reads "not intercepted"). Residual removed for pages loaded with the extension running.
