- **Decided 2026-10-21 → [P10K](#p10k--mirror-wp-rockets-stored-and-runtime-semantics)** (owner: mirror WP Rocket exactly; auditor M1/m3, verified in WP Rocket 3.23.5.1; engine unchanged until P10K): beyond whitespace, WP Rocket normalizes on save and matches differently at runtime — (1) `rocket_clean_wildcards` on `delay_js_exclusions`: per `/` segment `*`, `.*`, `(*)` → `(.*)`; (2) `sanitize_text_field` on all three fields: collapses inner whitespace runs, strips tags and `%xx` octets, drops invalid UTF-8; (3) `array_unique` drops duplicates, `array_filter` drops a `"0"` line; (4) Delay JS runtime (`inc/Engine/Optimization/DelayJS/HTML.php`): rows get `+` → `\+`, `?ver` → `\?ver`, `#` → `\#` and match as `preg_match("#{$pattern}#i")` — **case-insensitive** — while `compile.ts` compiles rows raw and case-sensitive. Each changes what a row matches here vs. on the live site; all mirrored by P10K. Corrected by the K.1 inventory: `rocket_clean_wildcards` rewrites every `*` (the `/` split is a no-op), `sanitize_text_field` also HTML-escapes an unclosed `<` run, and the save also drops rows that fail PCRE compilation.

### P10K — Mirror WP Rocket's stored and runtime semantics

Owner 2026-10-21: "I want to mirror what WP Rocket does exactly." A row must mean here what it means on the live site: the set the engine applies, the counts, validation and copy-all all use the form WP Rocket **stores** and the way its runtime **matches** it. Reference: WP Rocket 3.23.5.1 (`/Volumes/Shared/projects/wp-media/wpr-front-debugging-tool/docker/wpr/wpr-rocket.zip`) + WordPress 7.1.3 core (`sanitize_text_field` lives in `wp-includes/formatting.php`, not the plugin). Facts: the K.1 inventory below (supersedes the decided block above where they differ). Stack: installed; dev-time PHP CLI ≥ 8.1 with ext-dom (versions per K.1), no new npm dependency; K.6 adds one CI-only action (`shivammathur/setup-php`).

**Owner decisions (2026-10-22):** (1) PHP parity in CI — "If not much trouble, PHP CI is okay (as separated worker from the rest)" → [P10K.6](#p10k6--php-parity-job-in-ci). (2) P21 (WP Rocket simulation) runs **before release** → [P21](./21-phase-21-wpr-simulation.md) slot. (3) A saved draft keeps "the stored version, it means what WP Rocket does (Full WP Rocket mirroring)" → the stored-form rule below; **reverses** the 2026-10-21 rule "editor keeps typed text … persisted drafts keep typed rows".

Order: K.1 (done) → K.2 → K.4a ∥ K.4d → K.3 ∥ K.4b ∥ K.4c → K.4e → K.5a → K.5b → K.5c; K.6 after K.2, parallel with all of them.

Rules for every sub-phase:

- **Rows hold WP Rocket's stored form from commit on** (owner 2026-10-22). While a row is being edited the text is the user's. When it is **committed** (the moment the shipped trim-on-leave runs: focus leaves the row; paste-created and restored rows commit on arrival) it becomes the K.3 stored form, and a visible per-row note says what changed — "WP Rocket stores this as `…` (you typed `…`)" (final words per W-1) — never a silent rewrite. A row WP Rocket drops (empty, `"0"`, duplicate — the row being committed; the rows already in the lane win (same stored set; a full-field save keeps the first) —, `pcre-invalid` pattern) is **removed from the draft on commit**, with an announced lane note naming the row and the reason. Exceptions: rows whose save outcome is `unknown` (K.4a `saveOutcome`, "unverified") keep their stored form from the verified per-row stages (trim, sanitize, wildcards); only WP Rocket's keep/drop verdict is unchecked; the note says so; safe-mode rows (O3) stay, with the conditional note. The draft, the set, persistence, Apply and copy-all all hold or read the stored form — a restore shows stored rows and no "you typed" note (the typed text lives only in the overlay's memory).
- [x] **K.3's set/draft must hold the stored form per the rule above** — done in `98e9212` (K.3 rework).
- **One declaring site per concept:** save normalization in `lib/exclusion-builder/field-text.ts` (feature-aware: `(feature, rows) → stored rows + per-row diff`); PCRE→JS translation in one `lib/exclusion-builder/` module (K.4a); runtime matching in the engine's per-feature matcher (`compile.ts` + the feature module) — consumers (draft→set, counts, validation, dedupe, copy-all, per-row match lookup, the picker, the panel's undefined-references exclusion helper) only call them.
- **PCRE vs JS:** every construct where `preg_match` and a JS `RegExp` disagree is either translated (provably same result, fixture-proven) or **rejected as a row problem** naming the construct ("PCRE-only syntax: `(?>…)` — not checked here"), never silently compiled with different semantics. Rows PCRE itself rejects stay invalid with the plugin's outcome named.
- **Mirror the settings-form path.** Other write paths (Abilities `SetOption`, settings import, code-level `update_option`, third-party `pre_update_option` callbacks) are out of scope and named in copy only where a user would meet them.
- **Parity proven against the real plugin**, not re-read memory: fixture table rows carry PHP-derived expected outputs.
- Changed test expectations (e.g. engine tests that pinned case-sensitive matching) are listed in the receipt with the reason (AGENTS test rule).

#### P10K.1 — Spike: inventory save + runtime code per feature (no product code)

Depends:P10G. Parallel:P10L's e2e flake item. Goal: every fact K.2–K.5 build on is read from the plugin source and recorded here, with file:function anchors.

- [x] Save chain per field (`exclude_defer_js`, `exclude_lazyload`, `delay_js_exclusions`; ALR has no field — confirm `rocket_lrc_exclusions` and its defaults/merges): exact order, `rocket_clean_wildcards` exact rewrite, `sanitize_text_field` steps at the docker site's WP version; empty-after-sanitize edge.
- [x] Runtime matching per feature (Delay JS, Defer JS, Image Lazyload, ALR): function, subject, literal vs regex, case, delimiter/escaping, flags, invalid-pattern behavior, default exclusions.
- [x] PCRE↔JS difference inventory with a disposition per row.
- [x] Engine gap list: differences today, pinning tests, consumers.
- [x] Harness shape for K.2 + fixture-table format/location.

**Inventory (2026-10-21; audited + corrected the same day).** Versions: WP Rocket **3.23.5.1** (`docker/wpr/wpr-rocket.zip`, sha256 `e42f4b1a…0e31`; the container runs the same version) · WordPress **7.1.3** (container `wprfdt-wordpress-1`, image `wordpress:7.1.3-php8.4-apache`; its `wp-includes` files used below are byte-identical to `wordpress.org/wordpress-7.1.3.zip`, sha1 `13eb1d04…210c`) · container PHP **8.4.27** / PCRE2 **10.44** (2024-06-07) / libxml **2.9.14**, JIT on, `pcre.backtrack_limit` 1 000 000, `recursion_limit` 100 000 · local `php -n` **8.1.34** / PCRE2 **10.49** (2026-09-28) / libxml **2.9.13**, same limits, JIT on · Node **24.21.0** / V8 13.6.233.17. Paths: `P:` = plugin root, `WP:` = WordPress root. "Run" = observed with the real code (harness §5; save chain also re-run inside the live site's full WP bootstrap — identical output, no `sanitize_text_field`/`esc_html` filters registered there).

**1 · Save chain** (settings form → `WP:wp-admin/options.php` `wp_unslash` per field (round-trips `wp_magic_quotes`; backslashes survive) → `update_option` → `WP:wp-includes/option.php:886` `sanitize_option` → `P:inc/Engine/Admin/Settings/Settings.php:sanitize_callback` (+ `rocket_input_sanitize` filters) → `option.php:901` `pre_update_option_wp_rocket_settings` (observed order in admin context, live site: prio 10 `Minify/CSS/AdminSubscriber:regenerate_minify_css_key` → `Admin/Database/Subscriber:save_optimize` → `Cache/Config/ConfigSubscriber:change_cache_reject_uri_with_permalink` → `ThirdParty/Plugins/Ads/Adthrive.php:maybe_add_delay_js_exclusion` (adds a Delay row when AdThrive is active) → `ThirdParty/Plugins/Optimization/WPMeteor.php:disable_delay_js_on_option_update` → `P:inc/admin/options.php:rocket_pre_main_option`; prio 11 `DelayJS/Admin/Subscriber:maybe_disable_combine_js`. AdThrive and WPMeteor run first, so the AdThrive row is re-sanitized and validated too. Conditional, not registered on this site: `classes/admin/class-logs.php:enable_debug`, `Addon/Cloudflare/Subscriber.php:77`).

| Step                                                                    | `delay_js_exclusions`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `exclude_defer_js`                                  | `exclude_lazyload`                                             |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Sanitize site                                                           | `DelayJS/Admin/Settings.php:sanitize_options` (filter prio 13)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `Admin/Settings/Settings.php:sanitize_callback:203` | `Media/Lazyload/AdminSubscriber.php:sanitize_exclude_lazyload` |
| `rocket_sanitize_textarea_field` (`P:inc/functions/formatting.php:175`) | `sanitize_text_field`, `rocket_clean_wildcards`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | `sanitize_text_field`                               | `sanitize_text_field`                                          |
| Safe mode                                                               | rows equal to `Settings::get_safe_mode_exclusions()` (3 fixed patterns, `DelayJS/Admin/Settings.php:160`) removed (`array_diff`) when `delay_js_execution_safe_mode` is on                                                                                                                                                          | —                                                   | —                                                              |
| `rocket_pre_main_option` (form submit only)                             | re-runs `rocket_sanitize_textarea_field` (idempotent, run), then **drops every row where `@preg_match('#'.str_replace('#','\#',$row).'#', 'dummy-sample') === false`** (no `i`, no runtime escapes); notice "The following pattern is invalid and has been removed:" (plural when > 1), one `<li>` per row as `Label: <em>row</em>` | same                                                | not in the list — no validation                                |

`rocket_sanitize_textarea_field` order (read + run): non-array → `explode("\n")` → `array_map('trim')` (PHP set ` \t\n\r\0\x0B`) → `array_filter` → **for each sanitizer: `array_filter(array_map(sanitizer))`** → `array_unique` (first occurrence kept; keys preserved — stored arrays can have gaps, K.2 compares `array_values`). Because `array_filter` re-runs after each sanitizer, a row that sanitizes to `''` or `"0"` is **dropped, never stored as `''`** (run: `<b></b>` → dropped, `<b>0</b>` → dropped, `"00"`/`"0.0"` kept); dedupe runs on the final form (run: `*`, `(*)`, `(.*)` after `.*` → one `(.*)`).

`rocket_clean_wildcards` (`formatting.php:33`): `explode('/')` → `strtr` per segment with `.*`/`*`/`(*)`/`(.*)` → `(.*)` → `implode('/')`. No key contains `/`, so it equals one `strtr` on the whole row: longest key first, left to right, no re-scan. **Every `*` is rewritten, quantifier or not** (run: `\d*x` → `\d(.*)x`, `jquery-?[0-9.]*(.min)?.js` → `jquery-?[0-9.](.*)(.min)?.js`, `[a-z]{2,}*` → `[a-z]{2,}(.*)`, `(.*?)` → `((.*)?)`, `[*]` → `[(.*)]` (a class of `(`, `.`, `*`, `)`)); `.+` untouched. An escaped star breaks: `\*` / `\.*` → `\(.*)`, which does not compile, so a Delay row with `\*` is **dropped at save** with the notice (run: `/\* c \*/ var q = 1;`, `\.*`). Defer has no wildcard step: `\.*` is stored and, matching the empty string, excludes every script (run).

`sanitize_text_field` → `_sanitize_text_fields($str, false)` (`WP:wp-includes/formatting.php:5656/5708`), in order: (1) `wp_check_invalid_utf8` (`:1127`, `wp_is_valid_utf8` → `mb_check_encoding`): **invalid UTF-8 → whole row `''`** (dropped); (2) only if the row contains `<`: `wp_pre_kses_less_than` (`:5263`, `%<[^>]*?((?=<)|>|$)%`): a `<…` run with no `>` before the next `<`/end is **`esc_html`'d** (`<`→`&lt;`, `&`→`&amp;` but an existing entity is not double-encoded, `"`→`&quot;`, `'`→`&#039;` — inside that run only), then `wp_strip_all_tags` (`:5600`: `<script|style>…</…>` blocks removed with content, PHP `strip_tags`, trim), then `"<\n"` → `"&lt;\n"`; (3) `/[\r\n\t ]+/` → one space; (4) `trim`; (5) loop: remove every `/%[a-f0-9]{2}/i` match until none, then if any were removed collapse `/ +/` and trim; (6) `apply_filters('sanitize_text_field')`.

| Input (run)                                                | Stored                                                                              | Why                                                          |
| ---------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `a<b` · `x<y<z>w` · `a&b<c&d"e'f`                          | `a&lt;b` · `x&lt;yw` · `a&b&lt;c&amp;d&quot;e&#039;f`                               | unclosed `<` run escaped; `<z>` stripped                     |
| `a&amp;<b`                                                 | `a&amp;&lt;b`                                                                       | no double encoding                                           |
| `a<b>c</b>d` · `<script>bad()</script>ok` · `a<!-- x -->b` | `acd` · `ok` · `ab`                                                                 | tags, script blocks, comments stripped                       |
| `a < b > c`                                                | `a < b > c`                                                                         | `strip_tags` ignores `<` + whitespace                        |
| `a<b"c>d"e>f` · `1<2 and 3>2` · `[^<>]+x`                  | `af` · `12` · `[^]+x` (Lazyload only: Delay/Defer drop it, it fails the PCRE check) | `strip_tags` tracks quotes inside a tag; `<…>` spans removed |
| `x%41y` · `%2541` · `%zz%2F%2f` · `%E2%80%8B`              | `xy` · `41` · `%zz` · dropped                                                       | octet loop                                                   |
| NBSP / U+200B inside or around text                        | kept                                                                                | not in `[\r\n\t ]`, not trimmed                              |

PCRE syntax the save mangles (run, live site too): `(?<n>a)` → `(?a)` (`<n>` stripped as a tag); `(?P<n>a)` → `(?Pa)`; `(?<=a)b` → `(?&lt;=a)b`, `(?<!a)b` → `(?&lt;!a)b`; `\k<n>` → `\k`; any `[<…]` class → `&lt;` text. `(?&lt;…`, `(?Pa)`, `\k` fail validation and are dropped (Delay/Defer). **`(?a)` compiles on PCRE2 ≥ 10.43** (both test PHPs; it sets the ASCII-restrict option and matches the empty string), so it is **stored and excludes every script** in Delay and Defer (run) — JS rejects it. In Lazyload the mangled text is stored as a literal.

Other save paths (not mirrored): Abilities `P:inc/Engine/Abilities/Options/SetOption.php:381-395` sanitizes the same way, appends by default (`array_values(array_unique(array_merge($existing, $sanitized)))`) and keeps invalid patterns (not a form submit); settings import and code-level `update_option` also skip the invalid-pattern drop.

ALR: **no settings field** (no `exclude_*` key). `LazyRenderContent/Frontend/Controller.php:get_exclusions` = `wpm_apply_filters_typed('string[]', 'rocket_lrc_exclusions', [])`; the only in-plugin callback is `DynamicLists/Subscriber.php:add_lrc_exclusions` → `array_merge($exclusions, DynamicLists::get_lrc_exclusions())` = the dynamic list `lazy_rendering_exclusions` (server-updated; bundled seed `P:dynamic-lists.json`, 48 rows, e.g. `class="parallex"`, `id="nav`, `no-wpr-lazyrender`). No sanitize, no trim, no dedupe on that path: a row reaches ALR only through PHP code (theme/mu-plugin filter) as written.

**2 · Runtime matching** (`rocket_buffer` priorities: CDN rewrite 2, ALR hashes 16, lazyload 18, minify JS 22, defer 24, defer-inline 25, delay 26 — each sees the buffer as rewritten by the earlier ones).

|                        | Delay JS                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Defer JS                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Image Lazyload                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | ALR                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ---------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Code                   | `Optimization/DelayJS/HTML.php:delay_js` → `replace_scripts:219`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | `Optimization/DeferJS/DeferJS.php:defer_js:60`, `get_excluded:195`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | `Dependencies/RocketLazyload/Image.php:canLazyload:506` → `isExcluded:537`; rows merged by `Media/Lazyload/Subscriber.php:add_exclusions:466`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `Frontend/Processor/Dom.php:add_hash_to_element` (`get_exclusions_pattern:55`)                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Subject                | whole **pre-delay** element `<script…>…</script>` (attributes + inline body, multi-line + end tag) as matched by `/<\s*script(?<attr>\s*[^>]*?)?>(?<content>.*?)?<\s*\/\s*script\s*>/ims` — after defer stamped `data-rocket-defer defer` (run: `^<script src`, `type="text/javascript"` match; `rocketlazyloadscript` does not); scripts inside `<svg>`/`<xmp>` are masked first and never tested (`Optimization/RegexTrait.php:162/182`) | the raw `src` value only, captured by `#<script\s+(?:[^>]+[\s'"])?src\s*=\s*['"]\s*?(?<url>[^'"]+)\s*?['"]…#i` — includes surrounding whitespace (run: `^/a` misses `src=" /a.js "`), entities raw, comments stripped first; no attributes (run: `id="k"` never matches)                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `<img>`: `atts` = text after `<img` up to the **first** `>` even inside a quoted value (`#<img(?<atts>\s.+)\s?/?>#iUs`; no `<img`; run: with `alt="a>b"` the row `alt="a` hits, `b"`/`loading="lazy"` miss; `<img` never matches), then the trimmed `src`; also `<picture>…</picture>` whole, `<iframe…>…</iframe>` whole (`Iframe.php:80`), bg-image elements' opening tag minus `style` plus the decoded, `esc_url`'d style URL against `…_excluded_src` (`Image.php:132`), CSS bg selector/style (`Media/Lazyload/CSS/Subscriber.php:533`) | the element's opening tag **as re-serialized by DOMDocument** `saveHTML` (run: `CLASS='Hero'` is tested as `class="Hero"`; boolean attributes bare — `hidden` hits, `hidden=""` misses; `&amp;` stays `&amp;`); no end tag (run: `</div>` never matches) |
| Kind                   | regex, **one row at a time**, first hit wins                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | regex, **all rows joined**: `#(r1\|r2\|…)#i`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | literal `strpos`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | literal: `preg_quote($row,'/')`, joined `/(r1\|r2…)/i`                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Escaping               | `str_replace(['+','?ver','#'], ['\+','\?ver','\#'])` on every row (incl. defaults): every `+` quantifier becomes a literal `+` (`\s+` never matches whitespace alone)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `#` → `\#` only (`DeferJS.php:212`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | none                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `preg_quote`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Flags / case           | `i` only: ASCII-insensitive (run: `été` misses `ÉTÉ`), no `s` (run: `foo(.*)bar` misses across `\n`), no `m`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `i` only                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | **case-sensitive**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `i` only                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Invalid pattern        | `preg_match` → `false` + E_WARNING per script tested ("Compilation failed…"); row skipped, other rows still tested, page still delayed (run: `(?+1)(x)`, valid at save, becomes `(?\+1)(x)` → a warning per script, other rows still apply; `(?+1)x` alone has no group 1 and is dropped at save)                                                                                                                                          | pre-check `if (!@preg_replace('#(…)#i','','dummy-string')) return $html;` → **a joined pattern that fails to compile disables `defer_js` for the whole page** (run: one bad row via a filter; from the form an individually invalid row is dropped at save, so only rows that fail together reach this); the same check also disables it when the joined pattern consumes all of `dummy-string` (run: `.*`). `defer_inline_js` (`DeferJS.php:103-155`) has no pre-check and still wraps inline jQuery | n/a                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | n/a (quoted)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Backtrack limit        | `false`, no warning → no match → script delayed (run: `(a+)+$`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `false` → that script deferred                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | n/a                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | n/a                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Empty / match-all rows | `''` → `##i` matches every script (unreachable via settings; reachable via filters, run); stored match-empty rows such as `(?a)` exclude every script                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | empty alternative excludes every script (run: `zzz\|`); no rows and no defaults → `#()#i` → nothing deferred (run); `\.*`, `(?a)` exclude every script                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | images: `isExcluded` `array_filter` ignores `''`/`"0"` (run); iframes: `strpos($tag, '')` is `0` on PHP 8, no filter → an empty row excludes every iframe (filter-only)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `['']`/`['0']` alone: no-op (`! empty($pattern)`); `['','zzz']`: empty alternative excludes every element (run; reachable — rows come only from filters)                                                                                                 |
| Defaults merged        | dynamic list `delay_js_exclusions` (`set_exclusions`), `delay_js_exclusions_selected_exclusions` (one-click), safe-mode list when on, filter `rocket_delay_js_exclusions` (3rd-party callbacks)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | dynamic list `defer_js_external_exclusions` + `array_unique` + filter `rocket_exclude_defer_js`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `rocket_lazyload_excluded_attributes` defaults (`data-src=`, `data-no-lazy=`, `skip-lazy`, `loading="eager"`…), `…_excluded_src` defaults + above-the-fold/LCP sources (`AboveTheFold/Frontend/Controller.php:add_exclusions:239`), iframe/YouTube lists; user rows merged into all four filters                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | dynamic list `lazy_rendering_exclusions`                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Side effects           | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | joined string matching `/(jquery(?:.*)?\.js)/i` turns off inline-jQuery deferral (`defer_inline_js:110`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | an excluded `<img>` also loses `loading="lazy"`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | see ALR traversal below                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

Defer JS joined-pattern effects (run). **Page off** (the pre-check fails, nothing deferred): the joined pattern does not compile — rows valid alone but not together, e.g. the same group name in two rows (`(?'n'a)x` + `(?'n'b)y`) — or it consumes all of `dummy-string` (`.*`). A row that is invalid on its own never reaches runtime from the form (dropped at save). **Defer stays on, matching changes**: an unscoped inline flag leaks into later rows (`(?-i)zzz` + `A.JS`: every script deferred, `A.JS` no longer matches `/a.js`); group numbers shift (`(a)\1` alone matches `aa`, joined it refers to the wrapper group and matches nothing); an empty alternative or match-empty row excludes every script.

ALR traversal (run, real `Dom::add_hashes`): from `<body>`, recursion depth `rocket_lrc_depth` (default 2) = **three element levels** (`body>l1>…>l5` hashes l1–l3); only `rocket_lrc_processed_tags` (`DIV/MAIN/FOOTER/SECTION/ARTICLE/HEADER`) are hashed and **traversal stops at any other tag** (children of `<nav>`, `<p><span>` not hashed); an element containing SVG `<use>` is skipped with its subtree; `rocket_lrc_max_hashes` (200) caps the count; an excluded element's subtree is skipped. **Re-keying:** `hash = md5(opening_tag . count)` and `count` advances only for non-excluded elements (`Dom.php:166-168`), so adding an exclusion changes the hash of every later element; the stored below-the-fold hashes stop matching until LRC data is regenerated — a page-wide effect. Without ext-dom, `rocket_lrc_processor` defaults to `regex`, whose processor never applies exclusions.

**3 · PCRE↔JS** (method: same pattern + subject through `preg_match('#P#i')` on both PHPs and `new RegExp(P, '' / 'i')` on Node 24.21; booleans identical on PCRE2 10.44 and 10.49, only error offsets differ). Delay subjects are whole elements (multi-line inline bodies), Defer subjects are one URL — the subject shape decides which rows matter.

| Construct                                                                                                                       | Observed (PCRE vs JS)                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Disposition                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Delimiter `#`, Delay escapes                                                                                                    | runtime `\#`; a typed `\#` becomes `\\#` (ends the pattern)                                                                                                                                                             | translate: apply the plugin's own escapes per feature before compile; `\#` → "WP Rocket drops this row" (save validation)                                                                                                                         |
| Delay `+`/`?ver` escapes                                                                                                        | `a+b` ≡ literal `a+b`; `a\+b` → `a\\+b` (one-or-more backslashes); `x\?ver` → `x\\?ver`; possessive `a++` → literal `a++`; any `+` quantifier → literal (`SCRIPT\s+defer`, `(a+)+$` match literally); `(?+1)` → invalid | translate (same `str_replace`); note when the escape changes meaning                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `i` folding                                                                                                                     | PCRE ASCII only; JS `i` folds Latin-1/Unicode (`é`~`É`: PCRE 0, JS 1); Kelvin/long-s: both 0                                                                                                                            | translate: no JS `i`; expand ASCII letters to `[aA]`                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Byte vs UTF-16                                                                                                                  | `^a.b$` on `aéb`: 0 vs 1; `^a..b$`: 1 vs 0; `^é{2}$`, anchored `^[é]$`, `[^x]` differ (unanchored `[é]` matches in both); emoji `^.$`: 0 both                                                                           | translate (proposal, prove in K.2): match over a byte string (each UTF-8 byte → U+0000–U+00FF) with the pattern encoded the same way + manual ASCII case expansion; else reject rows with non-ASCII in a class/quantified, or `.`/negated classes |
| `.` vs CR / U+2028; `.` vs `\n`                                                                                                 | `a.b` on `a\rb`: 1 vs 0; `\n` excluded by both (no `s`)                                                                                                                                                                 | translate `.` → `[^\n]` (matters for Delay inline bodies)                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `$` before final `\n`                                                                                                           | `ab$` on `ab\n`: 1 vs 0                                                                                                                                                                                                 | translate `$` → `(?=\n?$)` (Delay subjects end with `>`; matters for Defer URLs)                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `\s`, `\h`, `\v`, `\R`                                                                                                          | `\s` on NBSP: 0 vs 1; `\h` on space, `\R` on CRLF, `\v` on `\n`: 1 vs 0 (`\v` on VT: 1 both)                                                                                                                            | translate `\s` → `[\t\n\v\f\r ]`; `\h`, `\v`, `\R` → explicit byte classes or reject                                                                                                                                                              |
| `\A \z \Z \G`                                                                                                                   | 1 vs 0; `\A` matches literal `A` in JS                                                                                                                                                                                  | translate `\A`→`^`, `\z`→`(?![\s\S])`, `\Z`→`(?=\n?(?![\s\S]))`, `\G`→`^`                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `\Q…\E`, `\x{…}`, `\e`, `\cX`, octal `\101`, `\0`, `\xHH`                                                                       | `\Q`, `\x{41}`, `\e`: 1 vs 0 (`\x{41}` is 41 × `x` in JS); `\c`, octal, `\0`, `\xHH`: same                                                                                                                              | translate `\Q…\E` (escape body), `\x{h}`→`\xhh`/byte form, `\e`→`\x1B`; others same                                                                                                                                                               |
| `[[:alpha:]]`                                                                                                                   | 1 vs 0 (JS class `[[:alph]` + `]`)                                                                                                                                                                                      | translate to ASCII class                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `[]a]`, `[^]a]`                                                                                                                 | 1 vs 0 (JS `[]`/`[^]`)                                                                                                                                                                                                  | translate (`]` first → `\]`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `{,n}`                                                                                                                          | PCRE2 ≥ 10.43 quantifier `{0,n}`; JS literal                                                                                                                                                                            | translate                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| PCRE2 ≥ 10.43 option letters `(?a)`, `(?aD)`, `(?aS)`, `(?aW)`, `(?aP)`, `(?aT)`, `(?r)`                                        | `(?a)` reaches storage through the save (§1) and matches every subject; JS SyntaxError                                                                                                                                  | reject as row problem naming the outcome ("WP Rocket stores this; it matches every script") — owner decision O1                                                                                                                                   |
| `\p{…}`, `\N`, `\K`                                                                                                             | 1 vs 0                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | reject `\p`/`\N`; `\K` translate (drop — a boolean match is unchanged)                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Unknown letter escape `\i`, the `\u`+hex form, `\1` with no group                                                               | PCRE compile error; JS 1                                                                                                                                                                                                | "WP Rocket drops this row" (save validation) / ignored at runtime                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Possessive, atomic, recursion `(?R)`, subroutine `(?1)`, conditional, branch reset `(?\|`, verbs `(*FAIL)`, callouts, `(?'n'…)` | PCRE 1, JS SyntaxError                                                                                                                                                                                                  | reject: "PCRE-only syntax: `…` — not checked here" (Delay: possessive becomes literal after escaping — translate)                                                                                                                                 |
| `(?P<n>…)`, `(?<n>…)`, `\k<n>`, lookbehind `(?<=`/`(?<!`                                                                        | mangled by the save (§1)                                                                                                                                                                                                | note the stored form (K.3); the stored form is then validated like any row                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `(?P=n)`, `\g1`, `\g{-1}`                                                                                                       | 1 vs 0/SyntaxError                                                                                                                                                                                                      | translate `\gN`→`\N` where provable, else reject                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Inline `(?i)`, `(?-i)`, `(?s)`, `(?x)` unscoped                                                                                 | PCRE ok; JS SyntaxError (JS has only scoped `(?flags:…)`: Chrome 125, Firefox 132, Node 23 per MDN BCD `javascript.regular_expressions.modifier`; spec `RegularExpressionModifiers`, ES2025; run on Node 24 only)       | translate leading `(?i)` (no-op under `i`); others reject; Defer: an unscoped flag also changes later rows (`joined-changes-matching`)                                                                                                            |
| `(?#…)`                                                                                                                         | PCRE: `#` ends the pattern → error                                                                                                                                                                                      | "WP Rocket drops this row"                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Unbounded lookbehind                                                                                                            | PCRE error, JS ok                                                                                                                                                                                                       | irrelevant (lookbehind cannot survive the save)                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `a{2}{2}`                                                                                                                       | error both                                                                                                                                                                                                              | same                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Catastrophic `(a+)+$` (25 chars)                                                                                                | PCRE: `false` ("Backtrack limit exhausted") in ~2 ms, no warning = no match; JS 0 after ~0.6–1.4 s                                                                                                                      | keep `regex-risk.ts` reject (outcome differs: live = not excluded)                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Polynomial `a*a*a*a*a*c`                                                                                                        | PCRE 0 instantly; JS 0 after ~0.4 s (60 chars) to ~30–37 s (130 chars), machine-dependent                                                                                                                               | gap: `regex-risk` accepts polynomial shapes — owner decision O5                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Empty alternative `zzz\|`                                                                                                       | 1 both                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | same; Defer: excludes every script (`match-all` row problem; Defer stays on)                                                                                                                                                                      |

**5 · Harness decision for K.2.** Load the real code under `php -n` (≥ 8.1 with ext-dom; the table stores booleans and stored rows, never PCRE error text, so 10.44 vs 10.49 does not move it — re-check by running the same harness in the container with `docker exec … php -n`): plugin from the zip unzipped to a scratch dir (`inc/constants.php`, `inc/functions/formatting.php`, `inc/admin/options.php`, `inc/API/bypass.php`, `inc/functions/options.php`, `vendor/autoload.php`); WP core = seven unmodified `wp-includes` files of the pinned version (`plugin.php` + `class-wp-hook.php` + `class-wp-filter-sentinel.php`, `formatting.php`, `kses.php`, `utf8.php`, `compat-utf8.php`) **downloaded from `wordpress.org/wordpress-<ver>.zip` and sha1-verified into scratch, never committed** (they match the container byte for byte at 7.1.3); stubs only for unreached I/O: `get_option('blog_charset')`→`UTF-8`, `is_utf8_charset`, `_canonical_charset`, `wp_trigger_error`, `__`/`_n`, `WEEK_IN_SECONDS`, and no-op `rocket_pre_main_option` neighbours (`rocket_is_ssl_website`, `wp_get_environment_type`, `get/delete_transient`, `add_settings_error` (captures the message), `wp_next_scheduled`, `create_rocket_uniqid`, `rocket_generate_advanced_cache_file`, a `rocket_container` filter returning a beacon double). Runtime calls use the real classes with doubles only at the I/O seam: `DelayJS\HTML`/`DeferJS` with real `Options_Data` + an anonymous `DefaultLists\DataManager` subclass returning fixed lists; `RocketLazyload\Image::lazyloadImages` with the rows added through the real `add_filter`; `LazyRenderContent\…\Processor\Dom::set_exclusions/add_hashes`. Proven 2026-10-21 (scratch only, not committed): every "run" above came from these runs. Fixture table: `lib/exclusion-builder/wp-rocket-parity.fixtures.json` (next to the engine tests), header `{plugin, pluginZipSha256, wp, wpZipSha1, php, pcre2, libxml, generatedBy}`, rows `{"kind":"save","feature","typed":[…],"stored":[…],"dropped":[{"row","reason":"empty|zero|duplicate|invalid-pattern|safe-mode"}]}` (Delay rows carry `safeMode: bool`) and `{"kind":"match","feature","rows":[…],"html","excluded":[subject indexes],"featureOff":bool}` (ALR rows also `hashed` element ids); strings that are not valid UTF-8 carry a `b64` twin. Match rows drive the plugin's whole-buffer method (the HTML in, which elements stayed un-optimized out), so subject extraction is proven too.
