#### P10K.2 — Parity fixture table + PHP dev harness

Depends:P10K.1. Parallel:P10L's e2e flake item. Goal: one committed table of `(feature, typed row(s)) → stored rows` and `(feature, stored rows, html) → excluded elements` with expected outputs produced by the real plugin code.

- [x] Fixture table (format/location per K.1 §5): every K.1 "run" case, the whitespace/`"0"`/duplicate/tag/`strip_tags`-quirk/octet/invalid-UTF-8/wildcard/`\*` cases, safe-mode rows (on/off), each PCRE↔JS construct (incl. `(?a)` and the 10.43+ option letters), Defer joined-pattern cases (bad row, `dummy-string`, empty alternative, flag leak, backref shift), ALR depth/stop/SVG/re-keying/empty-row cases, the picked-row table (current and safe spellings), NBSP/zero-width rows, empty-after-sanitize rows.
- [x] Dev script (new `scripts/` entry + `package.json` script, AGENTS.md Commands row): unzips the plugin and downloads + sha1-verifies the pinned WP core zip to a scratch dir, runs the `php -n` harness that calls the real functions (no re-implementation in PHP), writes the expected outputs, and fails with a diff when the committed table is stale. Records plugin, WP core, PHP, PCRE2 and libxml versions in the header; fails fast without ext-dom.
- [x] A vitest suite reads the table (expected rows only; no PHP in `pnpm test`) — rows for not-yet-mirrored behavior marked pending so K.3/K.4a–d flip them test-first.

DoD: script reproduces the committed table byte-for-byte; `pnpm test` runs the table without PHP; gates green. Effort M · difficulty medium.

**Receipt (2026-10-22, audit fix round folded in).**

- **Files:** `lib/exclusion-builder/wp-rocket-parity.fixtures.json` (table) · `wp-rocket-parity-table.ts` (format + strict parser, the one declaring site; type-only imports, also loaded by the script) · `wp-rocket-parity.test.ts` (replay) · `wp-rocket-parity-table.test.ts` · `scripts/wp-rocket-parity.ts` (+ `.test.ts`) · `scripts/wp-rocket-parity.php` · `pnpm parity:wp-rocket` (check; `--write` regenerates) · `.gitignore` `.cache/` (pinned WordPress zip, sha1-verified, `.cache/wp-rocket-parity/`; 300 s download timeout). No product module imports the table or its parser.
- **Versions (header):** WP Rocket 3.23.5.1 (zip sha256 `e42f4b1a…0e31`) · WordPress 7.1.3 (zip sha1 `13eb1d04…210c`) · PHP 8.1.34 / PCRE2 10.49 / libxml 2.9.13. Cross-check (re-run after this round): the same harness inside the docker site (PHP 8.4.27 / PCRE2 10.44 / libxml 2.9.14) gives identical outputs for all 289 rows.
- **Rows:** 289 = 79 save (Delay 42 · Defer 21 · Lazyload 16) + 210 match (Delay 76 · Defer 95 · Lazyload 16 · ALR 23); 14 `filterOnly`; 40 with an `engine` disposition. **Pending is per field** (`pending: {owner, fields}`): 646 compared fields, **361 asserted now, 285 pending**; 83 rows fully asserted, 206 rows with pending fields (K.3 79: `dropped` on all, `stored` too on 47 — 32 rows assert `stored` now · K.4b 32 · K.4c 67 · K.4d 28, of which 9 ALR rows pend only `rekeyed` and assert `candidates`/`excluded`/`hashed` now).
- **Dispositions** (`engine`, authored; the PHP outputs stay as the live record): `pcre-only` 15 (Defer possessive, atomic, recursion, subroutine, conditional, branch reset, verb, callout, `(?'n'…)` + `\k`, `(?P=n)`, unscoped `(?-i)`/`(?s)`/`(?x)`, `\p{L}`; Delay `(?+1)(x)`), `match-all` 12 (`(?a)` and the six other option letters — **O1 still open: encoded with its recommended default, a row problem that blocks Apply**; Defer `zzz|`, `\.*`; Delay `''`; ALR `['', 'zzz']`), `joined-page-off` 4 (`dummy-string`, `.*`, duplicate group names ×2), `joined-changes-matching` 4 (flag leak, `(a)\1` ×2, `(a)\g1`), `backtracking-risk` 2 (new id, added to K.4a's list: the kept `regex-risk.ts` reject), `pcre-invalid` 1 (filter-only `(`), residual 2 (`match-lazyload-gt-*`, broken served markup: the engine sees `i8` only). The replay asserts the disposition instead of WP Rocket's outcome on these rows; all but the two residuals keep `problems` pending until K.4a's problem ids exist.
- **Format choices (beyond K.1 §5):** subjects keyed by `id`, else `#n` (n-th id-less subject tag, 0-based, comments skipped — harness and replay scan in document order and the replay checks its keys equal the row's `subjects`); Lazyload and ALR subjects always carry ids. Match rows carry `served` (page served with no user rows = engine input), `subjects`, `candidates`, `excluded` ⊆ `candidates`; Defer `featureOff`; ALR `hashed` + `rekeyed` (no `subjects`). `dropped[]` has `index`; a non-UTF-8 typed line is `{"b64": …}`; `from` makes a match row use a save row's stored rows (picked rows); `filterOnly` rows only a PHP filter can deliver (the script proves the other authored row sets survive the form save unchanged, and `filterOnly` ones do not); `engine` disposition (above). The file is byte-for-byte the Prettier-formatted output of `--write`.
- **Replay:** compares candidate **keys** (the engine's are the subjects it restores under the feature-wide disable; Lazyload, which has no toggle, under the literal `=`, held by every candidate's `src`), excluded keys, Defer `featureOff` (engine: always `false` today), ALR `hashed` (candidates − excluded) and `rekeyed` (engine: `null` — no re-key report yet, so `rekeyed` is pending K.4d on every ALR row), save `stored` + `dropped` (engine `dropped`: `null` — no per-line drop report yet, so `dropped` is pending K.3 on every save row). Each compared field (`comparedFields` in the table module) is asserted on its own: a field outside `pending.fields` must equal now, a pending one must still differ and fails once it matches ("drop it from its pending fields"); an `ExclusionValidationError` is an answer (its entries' `problem` ids), any other throw fails the test.
- **Harness:** real code, I/O stubs only. Save = each field's own sanitize site (Delay `DelayJS\Admin\Settings::sanitize_options` incl. safe mode, Lazyload `AdminSubscriber::sanitize_exclude_lazyload`, Defer `rocket_sanitize_textarea_field` behind `Settings::sanitize_callback`'s one-line `! empty()` ternary — the callback itself needs a full WordPress) → `rocket_pre_main_option` as a form submit (CRLF-joined text); per-line drop reasons observed stage by stage with the real functions and cross-checked against the whole-field result. Runtime: every pass wrapped by the real `IEConditionalSubscriber` extract/inject (priorities 1/34) — `DelayJS\HTML::delay_js` (only its own `preg_match` warnings from `DelayJS/HTML.php` are tolerated), `DeferJS::defer_js` (dynamic list = one never-matching sentinel; `featureOff` = `defer_js` returned its input, and a Defer fixture must have a deferred script), `Image::lazyloadImages` with rows merged by the real `Subscriber::add_exclusions` (fixtures may not contain `<script>`/`<noscript>`/`<picture>` — the Subscriber's hide/picture steps are not run; `srcset` filter not wired), ALR `Dom::add_hashes` + `Controller::optimize` (every baseline hash below the fold). Delay inputs are the pre-delay page. Not covered: picture/iframe/background lazyload.
- **Facts contradicting K.1** (now reconciled inline in K.1 §1–§4, K.4b, K.4d): (1) `(?+1)x` is dropped at save (no group 1); the "valid at save, invalid at Delay runtime" case is `(?+1)(x)`. (2) Delay's `+` escape breaks every `+` quantifier (`match-delay-rebuild-upper-tag`; the backtrack-limit row uses `(a{1,}){1,}$`). (3) `[^<>]+x` → `[^]+x` is stored only by Lazyload. (4) ALR never hashes an element libxml re-serializes differently, yet hashes its children; no `<meta charset>` → Latin-1. (5) `alt="a>b"` breaks Lazyload's served markup. (6, this round) An attribute-less `<script>` is served as `<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">` (leading space), so K.4b's rebuild rule needed both spellings (`match-delay-bare-*`).
- **O4 evidence (restated):** a throwaway prototype (scratch, not committed; K.4a re-proves it) matched rows over the UTF-8 bytes of their subjects (one byte → one U+0000–U+00FF unit; row bytes likewise; feature escapes first; `\s`→`[\t\n\x0b\f\r ]`, `\h`→`[\t \xa0]`, `\v`→`[\n\x0b\f\r\x85]`, `\R`, `.`→`[^\n]`, `$`, `\x{hh}`; ASCII letters expanded to `[aA]`, never JS `i`, which would fold bytes 0xC0–0xDE with 0xE0–0xFE). Counted rows — the byte-sensitive ones only: 23 Defer (`match-defer-pcre-` `fold-non-ascii`, `byte-dot`, `byte-two-dots`, `byte-quantified`, `byte-class-anchored`, `byte-class-unanchored`, `byte-negated-class`, `byte-emoji-dot`, `byte-hex-escape`, `byte-hex-pair`, `byte-braced-hex`, `byte-word`, `byte-non-word`, `byte-range-anchored`, `byte-range-unanchored`, `byte-range-ascii-start`, `byte-unicode-property`, `space-nbsp`, `space-nbsp-byte`, `horizontal-space`, `horizontal-space-byte`, `vertical-space-byte`, `dot-line-separator`) + 9 Delay (`match-delay-ascii-fold-only`, `non-ascii-exact`, and over a multi-line inline body `byte-dot`, `byte-two-dots`, `byte-negated-class`, `byte-quantifier`, `byte-horizontal-space`, `byte-fold`, `byte-dot-newline`). Result: **31 agree with PCRE, 0 disagree, 1 rejected by design (`\p{L}`)**; plain JS (UTF-16 + `i`) disagrees on 21 of the 32. **Answer: yes for every construct in the table** (`\p{…}` stays rejected). Residual: it needs the page's UTF-8 bytes — re-encoding decoded text is exact only for UTF-8 pages (WordPress `blog_charset` default).

#### P10K.4a — PCRE→JS translator and row problems (pure)

Depends:P10K.2. Parallel:P10K.4d. Goal: one pure module turns a stored regex row (+ feature) into either a JS matcher source proven equal to WP Rocket's `preg_match` on that feature's subjects, or a typed problem.

- [x] Parser/translator per the K.1 §3 dispositions: feature escapes first (Delay `+`/`?ver`/`#`; Defer `#`), then translate (`\A \z \Z \G`, `\Q…\E`, `\x{…}`, `\e`, `[[:class:]]`, `]`-first classes, `{,n}`, `.`, `$`, `\s`/`\h`/`\v`/`\R`, `\K`, `\gN`, leading `(?i)`), ASCII-only case folding without the JS `i` flag; byte-string matching (proven on the K.2 byte rows — O4 evidence in the K.2 receipt).
- [x] Typed problems, **fail closed** (no PCRE here: anything the parser does not recognize is `pcre-only`): `pcre-only {construct}` (not checked here); `pcre-invalid` only for constructs the K.2 fixtures prove PCRE rejects (WP Rocket drops the row at save — the `#…#` no-`i` check); `match-all` (`(?a)`-style rows and empty alternatives — O1); Defer set-level: `joined-page-off` (WP Rocket's pre-check fails and it defers nothing on the page; two causes: the joined pattern does not compile though every row does alone — e.g. a group name repeated across rows — or it consumes all of `dummy-string`, which is not a compile failure; fixtures `match-defer-duplicate-names`, `match-defer-dot-star`, `match-defer-dummy-string`; `match-defer-bad-row` is the filter-only variant) and `joined-changes-matching` (unscoped inline flags, backreferences, named groups — Defer stays on, other rows match differently; row problem only); `backtracking-risk` (the `regex-risk.ts` reject, kept). The ids are the table's `ProblemId` (`lib/exclusion-builder/wp-rocket-parity-table.ts`); K.4a declares them in its module and the table imports the type from there; problems surface as `ExclusionValidationError` entries carrying a `problem` id (the replay reads that field).
- [x] `regex-risk.ts` still guards backtracking on the translated source; polynomial handling per O5.

DoD: for every Delay/Defer match row of the table the translator, fed the row's stored rows only (no subject — K.4a needs no subject extraction), yields exactly the row's `engine.problem` when it has one and no problem otherwise; unit tests per K.1 §3 construct; engine untouched; gates green. Match equality over real subjects is proven by K.4b/K.4c's replay rows. Effort M · difficulty hard.

**Receipt (2026-10-22, audit fix rounds 2–4 folded in).** O1–O6 still open: recommended defaults encoded (O1 `match-all` blocks, O4 byte strings, O5 polynomial accepted).

- **Files:** `lib/exclusion-builder/pcre/` — `problems.ts` (`ProblemId` + `PROBLEM_IDS`; leaf, imported by the parity table as `./pcre/problems.ts` so the dev script still loads it natively), `byte-set.ts` (256-byte sets, shared literal sets, memoized JS class emission, POSIX incl. caseless `lower`/`upper` = `alpha`), `parse.ts` (PCRE2 non-UTF parser → tree + issues `invalid` / `untranslated` / `unknown`), `translate.ts` (escapes, delimiter scan, `saveOutcome`, `emitRow`, `analyzeRow`, `toByteString`, `PCRE_LIMITS`), `rows.ts` (`translateRegexRows`, `compileMatcher`, `regexRowProblems`, Defer joined checks) + tests. `errors.ts`: `InvalidExclusionRow.problem?: ProblemId` (additive; K.4d adds the same field). Engine untouched (no product module imports `pcre/`) → bundles unchanged, budgets green.
- **API for K.3/K.4b/K.4c:** `translateRegexRows(feature, storedRows) → {rows: [{row, matcher: {source, flags: ''} | null, problem: {problem, construct, reason} | null}], page: 'on' | 'off' | 'unknown'}`; `compileMatcher(matcher) → (subject) => boolean` (encodes the subject to its UTF-8 byte string itself); `matcher` exists whenever the runtime pattern translates and is safe to run, whatever the problem. `regexRowProblems(feature, rows)` → `InvalidExclusionRow[]` with `problem`. `saveOutcome(row) → kept | dropped {construct} | unknown {construct}` — K.3 drops only `dropped`. One problem per row: `pcre-invalid` (save drops it) > `pcre-only` (save fate not modeled, or row > 8 192 bytes) > `joined-page-off` > `match-all` > `pcre-only` (no proven translation) > `joined-changes-matching` > `backtracking-risk`. Delay: no joined checks, `page: 'on'`.
- **Translation:** byte sets for every position (case folded before negation, as PCRE), never JS `i`; `.`/`\N` → `[^\x0a]`, `$`/`\Z` → `(?=\n?$)`, `\z` → `$`, `\A`/`\G` → `^`, `\R` → `(?:\r\n|\r(?!\n)|[\n\x0b\f\x85])`, `\K`/`(?i)`/`(?J)` → nothing, `(?i:`/`(?J:` → `(?:`, `{,n}` → `{0,n}` (a literal before PCRE2 10.43 — PHP 8.3 bundles 10.42; references are 10.44/10.49), `\N{n}` → `\N` repeated, `\E`/`\Q…\E` transparent (in classes too), named groups → plain groups, `\g{-n}` → absolute. Back-references only to an unquantified top-level group closed earlier in the branch holding no byte JS folds differently, as `(?i:\N)`; lookbehinds only with fixed-length branches and no group/back-reference/`\K`.
- **Save model:** option letters modeled (`i m n s x J U r a`, `a` + one of `D S W P T`, `^` first without `-`, one `-`; others rejected); lookbehind limits (unbounded rejected; 255 once a branch varies, else 65 535 per branch); `x`/`n` options, subroutine calls, `\p` names, branch-reset names, verbs/callouts/conditionals, bare `\x` (NUL on 10.44, error on 10.49), NUL bytes → `unknown`; past the first `unknown` construct no later `invalid` is claimed (missing-group references excepted when nothing else is unknown). Lookbehinds repeating an empty item without bound (`x(?<=x(?:)*)`, `(?<=\b+)`, `(?<=(?<=a)*)`; a repeated lookahead `(?<=(?=a)*)` is kept) are dropped, counts past 10 001 unknown; a pattern whose repeated groups may pass PCRE's compiled size (estimate > 30 000 units — `(?:a){65535}` is rejected, `a{65535}` kept) is unknown.
- **Defer page:** a stored row the save would drop turns the page `off` only when its error survives any join (`fixed`: not a group reference — the wrapper is group 1 and other rows add groups/names —, not a construct open at the row's end that `|`/the next row/the wrapper `)` can complete, not a repeated name after an earlier row's `J`; a parenthesis imbalance is `off` only when the joined rows provably don't balance); otherwise `unknown`, as is every row after one that may merge into it (open end, open `\Q`). Then, over the kept rows: an open `\Q` quoting the wrapper, a group name repeated across rows without `J` in force at the repeat (top-level `J` leaks forward; a branch reset licenses nothing across rows), or the exact `preg_replace` replay consuming `dummy-string` → `off` (rows flagged); `unknown` when a row cannot be replayed exactly (save not `kept`, no translation, risky, numbered back-reference, leaking option) or the replay passes its budget; else `on`. A Defer row with `joined-changes-matching` gets no matcher (joined it matches differently).
- **Work bounds:** `PCRE_LIMITS` — a row over 8 192 UTF-8 bytes is rejected before being encoded (`row.length` first); a set stops being checked past 512 KiB (later rows `pcre-only` "not checked here", page `unknown`; a wire-valid set is ≤ 192 KiB); the replay is capped at 200 000 regex executions (charged before compiling), reuses each row's last search and skips rows whose minimum match length exceeds what is left of `dummy-string`. Measured on the final code, Apple M4 Pro, 12 cores, Node 24.21, load ≈ 5, median (min–max) of 7 warm runs, Delay / Defer: wire cap 500 × 131 = 14 (14–35) / 14 (14–32) ms; 2 500 × 200 = 236 (228–250) / 228 (225–231) ms; 2 500 × 1 000 = 101 (100–234) / 102 (100–238) ms (the set budget stops at ~524 rows); 500 × 64 KiB = 0–2 ms. Before round 2: 1 176 / 2 169 ms and 5 908 / 10 773 ms; round 2's `compiledSize`-free code measured 252 / 256 and 1 237 / 1 287 ms. Six adversarial wire-cap shapes: 12–30 ms. Remaining hot spot: `regex-risk` on the translated sources.
- **Proof:** unit table of 89 construct rows × subjects with booleans from real `preg_match` on PCRE2 10.49 and 10.44 (identical); `saveOutcome` pinned on 79 dropped / 19 kept / 11 unknown rows, each verdict observed on both versions (`\x`, `\xZ` moved to unknown: they differ); every stored row of the save fixtures is `kept` except `(?+1)(x)` (`unknown`). Auditor harnesses on the final code, every verdict re-derived on both versions: `k4a-fuzz` (compare adapted to the tri-state page/save outcomes) — 144 000 single rows, 1.9 M subject comparisons, 40 000 Defer sets; `k4a-fuzz2` (`compare3`, run on the worktree source) — 105 000 single rows (incl. a fresh `gen3` seed), 0.98 M comparisons, 60 016 Defer sets (`genset2` ×2 + the round-3 examples) — 0 match mismatches, 0 false drops, 0 kept-but-dropped, 0 no-problem-but-dropped, 0 page mismatches on either version; `unknown` pages: 4 709 / 40 000 and 28 509 / 60 016 (genset2 is built from repairable references, branch resets, `\G`/`\K` mixes). The 62 Defer match rows without a disposition still reproduce WP Rocket's `excluded` with raw-`src` byte subjects (scratch).
- **DoD test** (`pcre/parity.test.ts`): all 171 Delay/Defer match rows yield exactly `engine.problem` / no problem. No pending field flips (engine not switched).
- **Changed tests (own, this sub-phase):** `pageOff: boolean` → `page` tri-state (an `unknown` page was reported as "on"); `['(', '.*']` now `off` (a stored rejected row breaks the joined pattern); `(?|(?<n>a)|(?<n>b))` + `c` now `unknown` (branch-reset names not modeled); the three "is kept" rows `(?Q)`, `\p`, `(?R)` were wrong — PHP drops all three: `(?Q)`/`\p` now `dropped`, `(?R)` `unknown`; `\x`/`\xZ` moved from dropped to `unknown` (10.44 keeps them); `(?+1)(x)` now reports its save as not modeled; reasons reworded (`pcre-invalid` names the filter-path outcome per feature). Round 3: only the 8 192-byte reason changed; it now reads "not checked here (longer than 8192 bytes)".
- **Contradictions / decisions:** (1) `match-delay-escaped-hash` (`a\#b`, filter-only) gained `engine: {problem: pcre-invalid}` + `pending K.4b [problems]` (PHP outputs unchanged; compared fields 645, pending 286). (2) K.1 §3 says reject `\N`; fixture `match-defer-pcre-any-char-not-newline` expects a match → translated. (3) A Defer row's leaking option reports `joined-changes-matching` over its own `pcre-only` (fixture `match-defer-flag-leak`); a numbered back-reference does not outrank `pcre-only` (`match-defer-pcre-branch-reset`). (4) Page-off causes beyond the plan: a stored rejected row, an open `\Q`. (5) PCRE scans variable-length lookbehinds forwards (`-(?<!$\n?)`, `(?<=\R)[^a]` differ from JS) → only fixed-length branches translate. (6) Translated sources are ~4× longer per letter: `regex-risk`'s 4 096-char cap trips at ≈ 1 000 letters (reason now says so in row terms). (7) Round 3 decision: rows WP Rocket drops are judged against the join (filter path) — a missing-group reference compiles joined (`\1` → the wrapper), so it no longer turns the page off.
- **Round 4** (after merging `feat/p10` @ `16769dd`, K.4d — `errors.ts` keeps one `ProblemId` import from `./pcre/problems` and K.4d's broader doc line; budgets unchanged): (R1) an open `\Q` turns the page `off` only when no later row contains `\E` (raw text), else `unknown` (`['a\\Qb','c\\E']` is on in PHP); (R2) nested repeats of items that can match empty with a count product ≥ 16 are `unknown` at save (PHP drops `(?:|){20}`, `(?:(?:|){5}){4}`, `((?:|){4}){5}` but keeps `(?:|){19}`, `x(?:|){100}`, `(?:a?){999}` — not modeled), so no `match-all` claim; (R3) a group (assertions included) repeated `{0}` is untranslated and never `match-all` — PCRE's start-of-match optimization still reads it (`(?=(?:a|b){0})` matches "b"/"ab"/"bc" but not "a"; the fuzz also hit `(?<!…){0}`); found by the fuzz: `\K` inside an assertion is untranslated (it can push the start past the end; a `preg_replace` then fails). Fuzz on the final code, both versions, all verdicts re-derived: `k4a-fuzz2` + `k4a-fuzz3` cases (`gen4` and `genreal` fresh seeds) — 190 008 single rows, 1.55 M comparisons, 100 022 Defer sets (10 000 realistic `genreal` sets: 0 `unknown`), 0 mismatches of any kind; `k4a-fuzz` — 0 errors. Perf (M4 Pro, load ≈ 3.5, median (min–max) of 7): wire cap 15 (14–37) ms; 2 500 × 200 = 236 / 231 ms; 2 500 × 1 000 = 108 / 105 ms. `saveOutcome` table now 79 dropped / 19 kept / 13 unknown, each re-checked on both versions.

#### P10K.3 — Save normalization at the declaring site

Depends:P10K.2, P10K.4a (`pcre-invalid` drop reason). Parallel:P10K.4b, P10K.4c (P10K.4d if still open). Goal: `field-text.ts` yields exactly the rows WP Rocket stores, per feature.

- [x] Feature-aware stored-rows function, order exactly per K.1 §1: split (shipped `splitFieldText`) → PHP trim → drop empty and `"0"` → `sanitize_text_field` port (pure: UTF-8 validity on the encoded form, the unclosed-`<` `esc_html` run without double encoding, `<script>`/`<style>` blocks, a `strip_tags` port with its quote/comment/`< `-whitespace quirks, whitespace collapse, trim, `%xx` loop) → drop empty and `"0"` again → Delay: `rocket_clean_wildcards` (every `*`) → drop empty/`"0"` → first-occurrence dedupe → Delay: safe-mode `array_diff` (only when told safe mode is on) → Delay/Defer: drop rows the K.4a `pcre-invalid` check rejects. Lazyload: no wildcard, no PCRE check. **ALR: identity** (rows reach WP Rocket only through the `rocket_lrc_exclusions` filter).
- [x] Per-row diff helper for the note: `unchanged | stored-as {stored} | unverified {stored, construct, detail} | dropped {reason: empty|zero|duplicate|invalid-pattern|safe-mode}`, every state carrying `typed` (undo) — pure, table-tested. Safe mode: the extension cannot read `delay_js_execution_safe_mode` (no source exposes it), so a Delay row equal to one of the three safe-mode patterns is kept with `dropsInSafeMode` (O3; K.5a's conditional note).
- [x] Draft and set hold the stored form (owner 2026-10-22): commit-time conversion (row left, insert, paste, candidate pick, restore, before an apply) through the one save function; consumers read the stored form directly; no second normalization anywhere (grep receipt).

DoD: all save rows of the table green — the replay's save adapter switches to the per-row diff helper for `dropped`; consumers switched; gates green. Effort M · difficulty medium.

**Receipt (2026-10-22, owner-decision rework folded in).** O1–O6 still open: recommended defaults encoded (O3: flag, row kept). **Replaced:** the first round's typed-set design (`9285dd6`: set kept typed rows, the session rewrote with `storedExclusionSet(session.set)`) — critical in audit: the report check (`parseTransformReport`, `lib/messaging/exclusion-session.ts`) compares reports against the typed set, so any rewritten row (`jquery-*.js`, `%xx`, double spaces) failed every document as `engine-failed`; it also contradicted the owner's 2026-10-22 mirroring decision. `stored-set.ts` deleted.

- **Files** (`lib/exclusion-builder/`): `field-text.ts` (`saveExclusionRows(feature, typed, {safeMode?}) → {rows, diffs}`, `saveExclusionRow(feature, row, ahead)` (one row against the forms already held — the commit primitive), `storedText(diff)`, `storedExclusionRows`, `storedRowKey` (form before dedupe/safe mode/pattern check), `notStoredRows(set)` (background gate), `exclusionFieldText(stored)`, `trimExclusionRow`) · `wordpress/sanitize-text-field.ts` (`sanitizeTextField(text)`, `submittedText(row)` — UTF-8 read once) · `php/trim.ts`, `php/strip-tags.ts` (PHP primitives; `lazyload.ts`'s and `delay-js-rewrite.ts`'s own PHP trims folded in) · `stored-row-drops.ts` (leaf: drop-reason ids, imported by the parity table) · `pcre/rows.ts` (`rowSaveOutcome` — the save check read from the translator's bounded analysis memo: one cache layer; unverified rows get no problem and no matcher) · `pcre/translate.ts` (escaped NUL `\<NUL>` → `unknown`, fuzz find).
- **Model:** `entrypoints/exclusion-overlay.content/exclusions/draft.ts` — pure `commitRows(draft, lane, ids)`, `commitAll`, `restoreDraft(set)` → `{draft, commits: {lane, id, index, diff}[]}`; kept rows become their stored text, dropped rows leave the lane, blank rows are untouched (unfinished edits), unverified rows stay in their per-row-stage form (trim/sanitize/wildcards are verified; only keep/drop is not), safe-mode rows stay flagged; idempotent and identity when nothing changes (tested). Duplicate on commit: the rows already in the lane win (the committed copy leaves, whatever its position); on restore, first occurrence wins (the save itself). `addCandidate` stores the candidate or returns its drop diff. `draftToSet` = rows as they are minus blanks; `draftChars` reads that. Controller (`controller.ts`): `commitRow`/`insertRows`/`pasteRows` (all filled rows but the caret's)/`addCandidate`/load/apply (commits everything first) convert and publish `commits()` (changed rows only) for K.5a's notes, undo and announcements.
- **Validator:** wire contract (row cap included) on the stored form — `*` → `(.*)` and `<` → `&lt;` growth is checked; a broken half character is still named on the typed text; then the merged PCRE reading (`(` → "WP Rocket drops this row when saving…", shown while typed; the commit removes it); cache key lane + stored form.
- **Unverified rows:** K.4a `save.outcome === 'unknown'` → no row problem (never blocks, other rows unaffected) and `matcher: null` (matches nothing, K.4a's unproven-translation rule). Parity: `match-delay-invalid-runtime` now needs no disposition (WP Rocket skips that row too); 7 Defer rows (`\p{L}`, recursion, subroutine, conditional, verb, callout, `(?x)`) moved from `problem: pcre-only` to a documented residual (engine excludes none with them). Changed tests: `rows.test.ts` ×3, `validate.test.ts` deep-nesting row.
- **Background:** apply = wire parse → `notStoredRows` (refused as `invalid`, each row named) → `prevalidateExclusionSet`; rewrite runs on `session.set` as stored. End-to-end test: a committed `jquery-*.js` (`jquery-(.*).js`) applies and rewrites a document through the controller with the real in-process runner (`rewritten`, 1 match).
- **Consumers (stored form directly):** validator; candidate dedupe (`addCandidate`, list "added" via `laneRowSet` + `holdsCandidate` = candidate's stored key ∈ lane rows); `rowMatches` (row text, trimmed while typed); copy-all (lane rows minus blanks; `allDropped` string removed); wire char count; engine empty-row check (`validate.ts`, via `trimExclusionRow`); panel undefined-references test helpers (`storedExclusionRows` + `compileFirstMatch`); Defer gets deduped stored rows (test: duplicate named-group rows no longer turn the page off). Picker (`pick-target.ts`): the Lazyload literal's "stored as written" check and stretch trim go through `storedRowKey`/`trimExclusionRow` (was JS `.trim()`, which also trims NBSP, and a hand-written `"0"` rule); the regex encoder's `"0"` case is decided by `storedRowKey`; new test: every picked row of 9 tricky pages is its own stored form.
- **Grep receipt:** `grep -rlnE "PHP_TRIM|%\[a-f0-9\]|clean_wildcards|cleanWildcards|sanitize_text_field|sanitizeTextField|exclusionFieldLines|committedFieldRows"` over `lib entrypoints components utils` (non-test) → `field-text.ts`, `php/{trim,strip-tags}.ts`, `wordpress/sanitize-text-field.ts`, a doc comment in `model.ts` — no second normalization.
- **Replay:** all 79 save rows green (pending K.3 0; table pending 0 after the K.4b/K.4c merge); `pnpm parity:wp-rocket` reproduces byte-for-byte.
- **Differential fuzz** (scratch, final code): PHP 8.1.34 / PCRE2 10.49 — `sanitize_text_field` + trim + `rocket_clean_wildcards` 360 000 rows, whole form save through the K.2 harness 60 000 sets / 209 470 lines; docker PHP 8.4.27 / PCRE2 10.44 — 240 000 rows, 40 000 sets / 139 898 lines: **0 mismatches** (unverified lines accept either PHP fate: 1 353 local, PHP kept 187 / dropped 1 166; 900 docker, 370 / 530). First round (typed design, same port): 670 000 + 300 000 rows, 160 000 sets, 0 mismatches.
- **Changed tests (rework):** `draft.test.ts` — set from an uncommitted insert/paste is sent as is (commit first to get the stored form), `trimRow` → `commitRows`, candidate results carry `diff`, wire count reads stored rows, paste oracle runs through the save; overlay `controller.test.ts` — paste-cap and wire-cap fillers made distinct (duplicates now leave the lane on insert), "keeps saved after trim" → "saves the stored form once committed", `invalidRowsById` builds its blank row directly (restore drops blanks); session `controller.test.ts` — apply refusal of non-stored rows, `(?a)` invalid case; `lane.test.tsx` — `allDropped` case removed; `counts.test.ts` back to its pre-K.3 form; `sanitize-text-field.test.ts` — bytes go through `submittedText`; `delay-js-rewrite.test.ts` `phpTrim` case moved to `php/trim.test.ts`.
- **Contradictions:** (1) the plan's mirroring rule says unverified rows "stay as typed (shipped trim only)"; per the coordinator's rework brief they keep their per-row-stage form (verified stages applied). (2) Commit-time conversion was listed under K.5a; K.3 now does the model and conversion, K.5a keeps notes/undo/announcements. (3) Apply commits a row still holding focus (the set must be stored). (4) A committed duplicate leaves even when it sits above the row it duplicates (the rule says "the later copy"). All accepted (2026-10-22); the rule text and K.5a/K.5b are updated.
- **Open follow-ups (verifier MINOR):** constructs PHP always drops but the parser leaves `unknown` (`(**)`, `(?(`, `(*UTF)`) — kept as unverified until a drop is proven on both PCRE2 versions; the `commitRows` duplicate-vs-`invalid-pattern` attribution (K.5a); unverified page-off rows unnamed (K.5b).
- **Budgets (raw KiB `049cf0d` → this; gzip):** background 171.1 → 175.9 (57.0 → 58.6); Firefox background 167.1 → 171.9 (55.5 → 57.1); overlay ×2 220.1 → 228.7 (73.6 → 77.2); Firefox engine worker 78.4 → 78.3; whole build 1 383.7 → 1 397.2 (411.7 → 416.8). Rows set to measured + 0.1 in `build(budgets)`.

#### P10K.4b — Delay JS matcher, pre-delay subject, picked rows

Depends:P10K.4a. Parallel:P10K.3, P10K.4c. Goal: a stored Delay row matches here exactly the scripts it excludes on the live site, and every picked row survives WP Rocket's save and runtime.

- [x] Matcher: K.4a translation of the escaped row, tested one row at a time (first hit wins) against the **rebuilt pre-delay element**: drop the injected type — ` type="(text/)rocketlazyloadscript"` with its leading space when WP Rocket rewrote an attribute-less `<script>` (`<script>` → `<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">`), else `type="(text/)rocketlazyloadscript" ` with its trailing space (a lone-space attribute list stays `<script >`; fixtures `match-delay-bare-*`) — rename `data-rocket-type=` → `type=` and `data-rocket-src=` → `src=` in place (WP Rocket replaced them in place; only the undo choices WP Rocket's rewrite turns back into the served text are kept — see the receipt), keep body, `data-rocket-defer defer` and end tag. The greedy src rename is **not** a residual (`data-data-rocket-src=` renames back to `data-src=`). Named residual (fixture-pinned; WP Rocket's rewrite is lossy here, so these cannot be rebuilt exactly): whitespace it trimmed inside the opening tag and around `=` in `type`/`src` (`type = "…"` → `type="…"`); a valueless `type` removed by `/(\s+type\s+)|(^type\s+)|(\s+type$)/i`, which also deletes `type` inside other attribute values (`title="a type b"` → `title="ab"`); `<script`/end-tag spelling it normalized; a source already spelling `data-rocket-src=` where both readings fit and agree. Never candidates (WP Rocket leaves them un-delayed): scripts inside `<svg>`/`<xmp>` and IE conditional comments (`IEConditionalSubscriber`, prio 1/34 — wired in the K.2 harness around every pass; `delay_js` alone would delay them: `match-delay-ie-conditional`), and scripts whose `type` regex hits a non-JS value — including a `data-type="foo"` attribute (no word boundary) — run.
- [x] Cache keys include the feature; wall-time contract (P10B M6) unchanged.
- [x] Picked rows (all lanes, one encoder in `pick-target.ts`): **literal** characters `+ ? * < %` are written `\x2B \x3F \x2A \x3C \x25` (never `\+ \? \* \#`); the picker's own regex syntax stays as is — the id row's `\s`, `["']?` and lookahead `(?=["'\s/>])`, the `(?:&|&amp;)` alternation (all survive save and runtime, run); Lazyload literals pick a stretch without `%xx` or `<` (they cannot be encoded in a literal). Test: `pick-target.test.ts` "every picked row matches its element after WP Rocket's save and runtime", reading the K.2 picked-row fixtures (fails today on `a+b.js`, `?ver` excerpts, `*`/`<` excerpts, `%xx` paths).
- [x] Changed expectations listed (`compile.test.ts:11`, `delay-js.test.ts:161`, `pick-target.test.ts` rows).

DoD: every Delay + picked-row table row green or asserting its `engine` disposition; `build` + `build:firefox` + `check:budgets` green with receipted deltas. Effort M · difficulty hard.

**Receipt (2026-10-22).** O1–O6 still open: recommended defaults encoded (O1 `match-all` blocks Apply; O6 hex spellings).

- **Files:** `lib/exclusion-builder/delay-js-match.ts` (new: `preDelayElement` + `compileDelayRows`, the Delay matcher module; declares `DELAY_TYPE_ATTRIBUTE`, re-exported by `delay-js.ts`) · `delay-js.ts` (matches through it) · `validate.ts` · `pcre/rows.ts` · `entrypoints/exclusion-overlay.content/exclusions/pick-target.ts` · `lib/messaging/exclusion-session.ts` · fixtures (K.4b marks only) + tests. **`compile.ts` untouched** (no feature dispatch there: Delay no longer calls `compilePatternRows`, which stays Defer-only for K.4c).
- **Shared-file edits:** `validate.ts` — one registry `TRANSLATED_ROW_PROBLEMS: Partial<Record<feature, rows → InvalidExclusionRow[]>>` with the single entry `delay_js: regexRowProblems('delay_js', …)` (K.4c adds its row), `translatedRowProblems` (a trimmed-empty row keeps the empty reason, `''` carries `problem: 'match-all'`; others get the translator's entry), and a 2-line dispatch at the top of `featureRowProblems` for `pattern` rows. `pcre/rows.ts` (additive) — `compileMatcher` delegates to the new `compileByteMatcher` (pre-encoded subjects; compiled regex memoized by source), `TRANSLATION_CACHE_LIMIT` 512, row analyses memoized by `feature\0row` (FIFO-bounded) inside `translateRegexRows`.
- **Matcher:** each stored row through K.4a (`translateRegexRows` → `compileByteMatcher`), first hit wins; the subject is encoded to its byte string once per script. Subject = served element with the delay `type` dropped (trailing space, else the leading one: `<script>`, `<script >` rebuilt) and `data-rocket-type=`/`data-rocket-src=` renamed back in place up to the first `>` (WP Rocket's own attribute span). The greedy `src` rename is **not** a residual for matching: renaming `data-data-rocket-src=` back yields `data-src=` (`match-delay-rebuild-data-src` green). Residuals as fixture `engine` dispositions (5): `rebuild-spaced-type`, `-title-type`, `-multiline`, `-upper-tag-spacing`, `-bare-type`.
- **Validation path:** Delay row problems (`pcre-only`, `pcre-invalid`, `match-all`, `backtracking-risk`) are `ExclusionValidationError` entries with `problem` from the prevalidator, the transform and the overlay row validator alike. **Fixed on the way:** the apply-reply wire parser refused any entry carrying `problem` (latent since K.4d's ALR `match-all`), so an invalid reply became a broken one; it now accepts an optional known `problem` id (additive; unknown ids refused).
- **Picked rows (one encoder, `literalPattern`):** `+ ? * < %` → `\x2B \x3F \x2A \x3C \x25`; beyond O6's list, also proven needed by the fuzz: tab/CR/LF → `\x09 \x0D \x0A` and a space that is leading, trailing or followed by another → `\x20` (the save collapses/trims them), a whole row `0` → `\x30` (dropped as empty), an id row whose value starts `ver` → `\x76er` (the optional quote's `?ver` would be escaped). Lazyload literals: longest stretch between `&`, `<`, `%`, tab/CR/LF, space runs (trimmed); a lone `0` gives no row. `nameUrl`: a host-less value with no path (`/`, `/?q=1`) gets no name (the old `example.com` row matched nothing live).
- **Replay:** K.4b 33 rows / 34 fields → 0 (28 green, 5 residual dispositions); table pending 179 rows / 229 fields → 146 / 195 (K.3 79/126, K.4c 67/69); `pnpm parity:wp-rocket` reproduces byte-for-byte.
- **Fuzz (scratch, real `DelayJS\HTML::delay_js` + real form save through the K.2 harness, PHP 8.1.34 / PCRE2 10.49):** random pages (1–7 scripts: src paths with `+ ? * % %xx & # ' é`, ids, types incl. non-JS, inline bodies with every special, tabs, space runs, non-ASCII), each served by the plugin; rows = every delayed script's picked row (one case each) + 3 random sets (page slices + PCRE fragments) per page; save first, then runtime with the stored rows. Realistic corpus (5 seeds × 500 pages): 16 316 cases (8 816 picked) — **0 wrong**, 0 candidate mismatches, 16 095 agree, 221 refused as row problems; picked rows: 0 changed by the save, 0 not excluded live. Adversarial corpus (+ residual shapes: `=`, valueless `type`, `a type b`, `<SCRIPT`, `</SCRIPT >`, multi-space/newline separators, `data-src` first; 5 × 500 pages): 16 459 cases (8 959 picked) — **0 unexplained**; 134 excluded-set mismatches, 9 picked misses and 307 + 4 key shifts all on scripts whose original the rebuild cannot reproduce (named residuals; a valueless `type` removal also glues the next attribute, `"module"id=`, shifting the harness's keys).
- **Wall time (M6 contract unchanged; Node 24.21, M4 Pro, median of 7):** 4.1 MiB page, 400 delayed scripts × 50 rows, no match: 75–90 ms (before: 10–20 ms — byte-string encoding + ASCII-folded classes); 492 rows: 156–177 ms; budget `DOCUMENT_TRANSFORM_BUDGET_MS` 2 000. Firefox runs the same pipeline in its engine worker (built; not timed in SpiderMonkey).
- **Budgets (raw KiB, before → after; gzip) — not edited, owner decision:** background 131.9 → 167.0 (44.7 → 55.4); Firefox background 127.9 → 163.1 (43.3 → 54.0); Firefox engine worker 39.5 → 74.6 (14.8 → 25.5); overlay ×2 182.7 → 217.7 (61.9 → 72.7); whole build 1 307.2 → 1 377.3. Cause: the PCRE translator (`pcre/`) now ships in every engine and validator bundle; K.4c adds no further translator code.
- **Changed expectations:** `delay-js.test.ts:161` — case test now ASCII-insensitive (`A.JS` restores, `ÉTÉ` does not). `validate.test.ts` — Delay invalid rows carry `pcre-invalid` + the save reason (Defer keeps the JS wording); `''` carries `match-all`; the exponential probe for Delay is `(a{1,}){1,}b` (Delay reads every `+` literally, `(a+)+b` is plain text there), `compilePatternRows` probe moved to Defer; reason-wording tests run on Defer. `draft.test.ts` (3) and `lane.test.tsx` (1) — same two reasons. `pick-target.test.ts` — `nameUrl('/')` now `null` (was `example.com`, which no served markup holds). `compile.test.ts:11` **unchanged** (raw case-sensitive compile is Defer's until K.4c).
- **For K.5a:** the backtracking reasons' examples (`(a+)+`) mislead for Delay (its `+` is literal); every regex reason now comes from the translator.
- **Integration with K.4c + audit round (2026-10-22, supersedes the Files/Shared-file/Matcher/Picked/Fuzz/Wall-time/Budget bullets above where they differ).** Merge `509d6c5` (K.4c `21d3728`, no rebase): one registry `PCRE_ROW_PROBLEMS` (`delay_js`, `defer_all_js`) for every pattern feature; one empty-row policy (`pcreFeatureProblems`: trimmed-empty → empty reason, `''` also `match-all` — PHP: a filter `''` excludes every script for Defer too); the JS-compile path is deleted (`compile.ts` + test, `rowProblem`'s compile branch, `regexErrorDetail`, `REGEX_REASON_LEAD`; `compileMatcher` test-only → deleted); `compileFirstMatch` on the memoized `compileByteMatcher` drives both features; `utils/bounded-memo.ts` (FIFO, entry + estimated-byte bounds, an over-bound value is never kept) backs the analysis, compiled-regex, risk-verdict and Defer-set memos; transforms translate once for validation and matching; `DELAY_TYPE_ATTRIBUTE` has one import path (`delay-js-match.ts`). Fixes `22066c4`:
  - **C1:** `undoDelayRenames` (the one rename-back helper, Delay subject and Defer defer-time tag) keeps a keep/undo choice per rename only when `delay-js-rewrite.ts` — a pure forward model of `replace_scripts` (type check with `U`, `type`/`src` renames, valueless-`type` removal, PCRE `\s`/`.`/`$`, PHP `trim`; 21 shapes PHP-pinned) — turns it back into the served text, a line feed after an undone `=` tried too (WP Rocket drops it, and it changes how later renames fell). Fitting readings that disagree → `undetermined` (left as served, reported), never an answer; none fit → `undetermined`; a delay type in another spelling → read approximately, `approximate` counted. Defer drops readings capturing no `src` (WP Rocket stamped the script, so it captured one). Up to 6 renames (3^n checks), more → undetermined. Exhaustive search of attribute texts up to 6 tokens (1.9 M) found no served text with two fitting originals but for the line-feed case.
  - **Parser (K.4a):** brace quantifiers matched in place without the 64-char window (`x{0…01}` = `x{1}`, 70 nines too big — both PCRE2 10.44/10.49); `(?[…])` → `unknown` at save (10.49 keeps `(?[[:alpha:]])`, 10.44 drops it).
  - **Picker:** values the DOM decoded accept WordPress's entity forms (`& ' " < >` named/decimal/hex, `{0,2}` leading zeros — no `*`/`+`/`<`/`%`/`?ver`); Lazyload stretches avoid them; URL rows keep the served text (`//` after the host, `#`, raw host, backslashes; only `?…` dropped); no nameable URL text → id row, else first line; a script with only parked `data-rocket-*` (not the engine's candidate) is not offered. Fixture `save/match-picked-delay-amp` re-spelled and re-derived (`--write`).
  - **Other:** `toByteString` encodes only non-ASCII runs (property test vs one `TextEncoder` pass, lone surrogates included); reasons cap the echoed construct at 48 code points (both features, tested at 2 048-char rows); memo bounds in estimated retained bytes (V8-measured: analysis ≈ 700 B + 1 B/char, compiled regex ≈ 250 B + ≤ 5 B/char): analysis 256 entries / 256 KiB, regexes 256 / 192 KiB, risk verdicts 512 / 96 KiB, Defer sets 16 / 128 KiB → ≤ 672 KiB of estimates per realm (measured ≤ 0.41 MiB over a memo-full-of-short-rows baseline, which is itself ≈ 0.35 MiB; before: 6–7 MiB).
- **Fuzz after integration (scratch; the auditor's `k4b-fz` tools against real save + `delay_js`, PHP 8.1.34 / PCRE2 10.49):** Delay realistic 3 × 500 pages (12 032 cases, 6 032 picked) — 0 wrong, 0 picked misses; adversarial 3 × 500 (10 505 cases, 4 505 picked) — 0 wrong on exact rebuilds, 448 + 16 wrong on lossy rebuilds all explained by the named residuals (classifier: WP Rocket's rename spacing, valueless-`type` removal, whitespace, tag spelling, ASCII case), 7 undetermined, 1 picked miss (valueless-`type` glue). Picks with WordPress encoding 2 × 1 500 pages (11 917 picked) — 0 misses (was 1 084 + 1 063); raw encoding (11 901 picked) — 17 misses, all a raw `>` inside a quoted value (WP Rocket's attribute capture ends there and trims: its own served markup breaks — named residual, like Lazyload's `gt`) or a numeric entity without `;`; broad picks (row also excludes others) 41–128 per run — short paths/hosts, not wrong. K.4c corpora replayed (`zz-audit`): mixed 24 935 / realistic 40 602 / adversarial 8 407 / ext 16 222 / PCRE2 10.44 19 145 scripts + c1 / c3 — **0 wrong**, 1–9 undetermined per corpus (line-feed-ambiguous originals); hostile c2 (source spelling WP Rocket's markers) 10 wrong (was 19) + 10 undetermined; page verdicts unchanged.
- **Wall time (Node 24.21, M4 Pro, load ≈ 7, median of 9; base = `bf3b6cb`):** 4.1 MiB mixed page, 400 delayed scripts × 50 rows: Delay 16.5 → 17.3 ms, Defer 12.8 ms; 5.5 MiB inline-heavy: Delay 23.8 → 31.4 ms; 4.1 MiB external-only: 18.2 → 17.9 ms (before the ASCII fast path: 75–90 ms on the earlier bench).
- **Budgets:** see the `build(budgets)` commit (owner accepted the growth: the translator ships in the overlay for live verdicts and in the backgrounds/worker for matching).

#### P10K.4c — Defer JS joined-alternation semantics

Depends:P10K.4a. Parallel:P10K.3, P10K.4b. Goal: Defer matching follows WP Rocket's single alternation, including its one page-wide failure mode.

- [x] Subject = the raw `src` value only (as served, whitespace and entities kept); drop the document-resolved form (`defer-js.test.ts:148/157` change). Match = the joined alternation semantics: a script is excluded when any row matches.
- [x] Set-level outcomes, computed once per set from K.4a: `joined-page-off` → **WP Rocket defers nothing on the page** (its pre-check) — the only page-level problem (O2 decides whether Apply mirrors or blocks); `joined-changes-matching` → row problem on the offending row, Defer stays on; `match-all` / empty alternative → row problem, every script excluded. `pcre-invalid` rows never reach this step (K.3 drops them).
- [x] Per-row match counts are best-effort (the row that matched first in list order); the report says so for Defer.

DoD: every Defer table row green or asserting its `engine` disposition; changed expectations listed; budgets green. Effort S · difficulty medium.

**Receipt (2026-10-22).** O1–O6 still open: recommended defaults encoded (O2 block: `joined-page-off` fails validation, so Apply is blocked).

- **Files:** new `lib/exclusion-builder/defer-js-rows.ts` (`deferRowSet(rows) → {problems, page, firstMatch}`, memoized per row set, bound 16; `deferRowProblems`) + test; `defer-js.ts` (subject = WP Rocket's own capture regex `#<script\s+(?:[^>]+[\s'"])?src\s*=…#i` over the served opening tag, PCRE `\s` + ASCII case written out; `deferSubject` exported; no `documentUrl` parameter); `validate.ts` (one registry `PCRE_ROW_PROBLEMS = {defer_all_js: deferRowProblems}` replaces the generic JS compile + `regex-risk` checks for that feature; trim-empty rows keep the generic reason; K.4b adds its `delay_js` row there); `report.ts` (`pageState?: 'unknown'`, `rowCountsBestEffort?: true`, row-driven runs only); `pcre/rows.ts` (`translationRowProblems`, `compileFirstMatch` — subject encoded once per script: 200 scripts × 500 rows 105 → 3 ms warm); `pcre/parse.ts` (stop as `unknown` at PCRE's 250-level nesting limit, one level kept for the wrapper — PHP: 250 alone kept, joined `NULL`; the parser overflowed the call stack at 1 875 levels); `lib/messaging/exclusion-session.ts` (report caveats parsed; invalid-row entries accept `problem` ∈ `PROBLEM_IDS` — an apply reply carrying one was rejected whole, latent since K.4a/K.4d). **`compile.ts` untouched** (Defer no longer calls `compilePatternRows`).
- **Subject:** raw value as served — whitespace and entities kept, the first quote ends it (`src="/it's.js"` → `/it`), the greedy prefix takes the last fitting `src=` (one inside `title=" src='/t.js'"` wins). `deferTimeTag` rebuilds what Defer captured from by text, never by attribute: its own stamp ` data-rocket-defer defer>` → `>` everywhere (it stamps every `>` of its match, one inside a quoted value too; exact — it never stamps a tag already holding `\s+defer`, which the stamp contains), then, on a tag Delay opened with its own `<script type="(text/)rocketlazyloadscript" `, `data-rocket-src=` → `src=` everywhere (Delay renames the first quoted `src=` text even inside another value or after a VT; Defer runs at 24, Delay at 26). Where an HTML parser ends the tag before WP Rocket's match does (VT before the quote, `>` in the value), the body's leading stamped run is read as the rest of the match. A marked script whose subject cannot be read back is left as served and counted `undetermined` (e.g. Delay's bare-`type` removal gluing `nomodule type src=` into `nomoduledata-rocket-src=`). All pinned on the real plugin. Unquoted / empty `src` captures nothing. PCRE `\s` has one declaring site, `PCRE_SPACE_CHARS` (`pcre/byte-set.ts`, pinned to `ESCAPE_SETS.s` over every UTF-16 unit; `lazyload.ts` uses it too).
- **Match:** OR of the K.4a row matchers in list order — exact because every row WP Rocket matches differently joined is a problem (no matcher). `match-all` / `joined-changes-matching` / `pcre-only` / `backtracking-risk` / `pcre-invalid` stay row problems; all of them fail validation (as before for any invalid row).
- **Page state for K.5b:** `deferRowSet(rows).page` (`on|off|unknown`). `off` → validation fails; the rows behind it carry `joined-page-off` **unless an outranking problem holds them** (`pcre-invalid` > `pcre-only` > `joined-page-off`: e.g. a filter-only `(` row) — so K.5b shows the page-level message from `page === 'off'`, naming the `joined-page-off` rows, else the outranking ones. `unknown` with a valid set → the transform applies matches as if on and the report says `pageState: 'unknown'`; K.5b should show a non-blocking lane note ("WP Rocket may defer no script on this page with these rows — not checked here"). Within the wire caps it practically needs a row problem (fuzz: 0 unknown valid sets; only the replay budget, past 500 rows, gets there).
- **Counts:** `rowCountsBestEffort: true` on row-driven Defer reports with > 1 row (first matching row in list order credited; WP Rocket's joined match names no row).
- **Replay:** 67 K.4c pending marks removed (69 fields: 33 `excluded`, 33 `problems`, 1 × `candidates/excluded/featureOff`); every Defer row green or asserting its disposition; table pending 229 → 160 fields (K.3 126 · K.4b 34). The replay's Defer `featureOff` is the engine's (`false`, or `'unknown'` from the report — never equal to WP Rocket's boolean). `pnpm parity:wp-rocket` reproduces byte-for-byte.
- **Differential fuzz (scratch, not committed):** K.2 harness copy + a `defer_delay` mode (real `DeferJS::defer_js` then `DelayJS\HTML::delay_js`), random served pages (2–9 scripts: quoting, case, whitespace/newlines, entities, `async`, inline, commented, `data-src`, `src=` inside another attribute, quote inside the value, non-ASCII/emoji, `#`, `%xx`) × row sets (half realistic, half mixed with 58 adversarial constructs and concatenations): **102 000 sets** (72 000 on PCRE2 10.49, 30 000 on 10.44 in the docker container), ~306 000 candidate scripts compared, ~52 800 excluded — **0 wrong answers** (excluded set, page off/on, page-off problem ⇒ page off). Realistic sets: 58 720, 0 `unknown`, 0 flagged. Mixed: ~22 % flagged (row problem; ~2 % `joined-page-off`), ~65 % of flagged sets have an `unknown` page.
- **Changed tests:** `defer-js.test.ts` document-URL block (`:148` resolved form, `:157` raw-with-URL) → raw-`src` subject tests pinned on PHP; `pipeline.test.ts` and `document-transform.test.ts` "threads the document URL" → "raw `src` whatever the document URL"; `validate.test.ts` Defer reasons are now the PCRE reading's (`pcre-invalid`), the over-deep too-complex row is pinned on Delay (Defer: `pcre-only`); `pick-target.test.ts` calls the Defer transform without a URL; `exclusion-session.test.ts` apply reply with `problem` now round-trips (expectation unchanged, behavior fixed). Fix round: `defer-js.test.ts` "prefers `data-rocket-src` over `src`" (a synthetic undelayed tag) → the PHP-observed delayed tag with two `src` (the last one fits); "a marker script without a URL" now reports `undetermined: 1` (WP Rocket only stamps scripts it captured a `src` from); new PHP-pinned tests for the stamped `>`, the VT-cut tag, the rename inside a value / after a VT, a source `data-rocket-src` on an undelayed tag, the `nomodule` join.
- **Budgets (not edited — K.4a's translator enters product bundles for the first time; K.4b's Delay matcher shares it):** background SW 131.9 → 167.1 KB raw (> 132.1) / 44.7 → 55.5 gz; overlay (both browsers) 182.7 → 217.9 (> 182.9) / 61.9 → 72.7 gz (> 62.1); Firefox background page 127.9 → 163.1 (> 128.1); Firefox engine worker 39.5 → 74.3 (> 39.7) / 14.8 → 25.4 gz (> 15); whole build 1 307.2 → 1 377.5 (> 1 307.4). `build` + `build:firefox` green.
- **Audit fix round (C1 + m1–m4):** the first subject rebuild undid Delay's rename only when a parser saw a `data-rocket-src` attribute and never undid Defer's own stamp — the auditor's harness (`defer_js` no rows → `delay_js` → `defer_js` rows, pre-check over `array_unique(array_merge(ext, rows))`) found 219 / 336 / 108 / 155 / 189 wrong (mixed / realistic / adversarial / ext list / PCRE2 10.44). Replayed on the fixed code against the same PHP verdicts: mixed 24 935 scripts, realistic 40 602, adversarial 8 407, ext list 16 222, 10.44 19 145 (+ earlier corpora 6 972 / 9 719) — **0 wrong, 0 undetermined**; page verdicts all agree except the m2 rows below (1 each in adversarial, ext, 10.44). A hostile corpus whose source already spells WP Rocket's own output (`data-rocket-defer` / ` data-rocket-defer defer>` in the source, or `data-rocket-src=` on a tag Delay then rewrites) stays wrong on 19 / 6 936 scripts (1 more undetermined): the served text cannot tell those apart from WP Rocket's writes.
- **Residuals / follow-ups:** (m1) a delayed source tag already spelling `data-rocket-src=`, and source markup carrying WP Rocket's markers (above); Delay's bare-`type` join (`nomoduledata-rocket-src=`) surfaces as `undetermined`, never as an answer. (m2) WP Rocket's `array_unique` drops repeated rows before joining, so two identical rows declaring a group name (`(?<n>a)\k<n>` twice) keep its page on, while `deferRowSet` on the undeduped rows reports `joined-page-off`: **K.3 must hand `deferRowSet` the stored (deduped) rows.** (m3) WP Rocket joins the user rows with its remote `defer_js_external_exclusions` list; the auditor's ext-list run (today's list) gave 0 wrong from the join, but a future list entry with an unscoped inline flag or a named group could change matching or turn the page off — not modeled. (m4, **P10L item**) `documentUrl` is now read by no transform yet still threaded pipeline → `document-transform` request → Firefox `transform-worker` request: remove the plumbing and its tests. (1) The plan says `pcre-invalid` rows never reach this step — true only after K.3; until then they reach it and can hide the page-off (above). (M1) `defer_inline_js`'s side effect (rows matching `/(jquery(?:.*)?\.js)/i` stop inline-jQuery wrapping) is not mirrored — its own sub-phase. (m5) Done at the K.4b integration: `deferTimeTags` reads through K.4b's `undoDelayRenames` (forward-checked readings), then undoes the Defer stamp; readings capturing no `src` are dropped, disagreeing ones are `undetermined`.

#### P10K.4d — Lazyload and ALR literal matchers

Depends:P10K.2 (literal rows — no translator). Parallel:P10K.4a, then P10K.3, P10K.4b, P10K.4c. Goal: literal rows match here what they match live.

- [x] Lazyload: case-sensitive `includes` against the rebuilt original `atts` (text after `<img`, up to the first `>`, src restored) and the trimmed `src`; rows `''`/`"0"` ignored as WP Rocket does. Residual (fixture `engine.residual`, `match-lazyload-gt-*`): a quoted `>` (`alt="a>b"`) breaks WP Rocket's own served markup — `data-lazy-src` lands inside `alt` — so the engine cannot see that image. Picture/iframe/background images stay out of scope (the engine restores `<img>` only) — named in the lane hint.
- [x] ALR: case-insensitive (ASCII) `includes` against the opening tag as libxml `saveHTML` re-serializes it — observed (run): lowercase attribute names, bare boolean attributes, double-quoted values except a value containing `"` keeps single quotes, `&#39;`/`&eacute;`/`&nbsp;` decoded to their characters while `&amp;`/`&lt;`/`&gt;`/`&quot;` stay encoded; the full rule comes from the K.2 fixtures, not this list — no end tag (`lazy-render.test.ts:49/67/163` change); an excluded element's descendants are excluded too; rows are literals (any text is valid); a set with an empty row next to other rows excludes every element (row problem `match-all`). An element whose libxml re-serialization differs from its source text (`CLASS='Hero'`, `&#39;`, `&eacute;`) is never hashed — never a candidate — while its children still are, and a row matching it still skips its subtree; a page without `<meta charset>` is read as Latin-1, so a non-ASCII row matches nothing there. Re-keying: expose the ids WP Rocket re-keys after an exclusion (the rows' `rekeyed`), for K.5a's note.
- [x] Changed expectations listed.

DoD: every Lazyload + ALR table row green (ALR incl. `hashed`/`rekeyed`) or asserting its `engine` disposition; budgets green. Effort S · difficulty medium.

**Receipt (2026-10-22, audit fix rounds 2–4 folded in).** DoD met; budgets raised by owner decision (rows below).

- **Files** (`lib/exclusion-builder/`): `literal-rows.ts` (literal features' empty-row rules + ASCII folding) · `libxml/serialize.ts` (pure opening-tag re-serializer) · `libxml/charset.ts` (how libxml reads the bytes) · `libxml/tree.ts` (element tree, linear) · `libxml/entities.ts` (libxml's 253 attribute entities) · `lazy-render-hashing.ts` (verdicts + hash walk; `lazyRenderRekeyed(servedHtml, rows, bytes?) → ScannedTag[] | null` for K.5a, `null` = unknown) · `lazy-render.ts`, `lazyload.ts` (matchers) · `validate.ts` (literal rows per feature; non-blocking `featureRowWarnings`) · `report.ts` (optional `undetermined`/`approximate` counts, additive; wire parser `lib/messaging/exclusion-session.ts`) · `pipeline.ts` + `lib/exclusion-session/{document-transform,transform-worker}.ts` (optional `bytes: {encoding, bom}` from the session's decoding, additive) · `errors.ts` (`InvalidExclusionRow.problem?: ProblemId`, type from `wp-rocket-parity-table.ts` until K.4a moves it) · `html-scan.ts` (`scanTokens`) · `scripts/wp-rocket-parity.php` (+ `absint`, `wp_allowed_protocols`, `array_any` copied verbatim from WP 7.1.3 `load.php`/`functions.php`/`compat.php`; check mode still byte-for-byte) · 4 new Lazyload fixture rows (`match-lazyload-{dimensions,absolute-src,data-src,self-closing}`, `--write`).
- **Lazyload:** subject = the `<noscript>` original WP Rocket serves after the lazy tag (`<img` any case, cut at its first `>`), else the served tag with only `src` restored — counted `approximate` in the report. `atts` = after `<img` to the first `>`, less a trailing `/` and then one PCRE `\s` (the ungreedy `\s?/?>`, PHP-checked), then the PHP-trimmed `src`. An empty `data-lazy-src` is a candidate (`esc_url` empties `data:`, `javascript:`, unknown-scheme sources): next to its `<noscript>` original it restores `src` from it, else it is counted `undetermined` (never silently skipped); a valueless carrier is no candidate. `''`/`"0"` ignored; whitespace-only rows stay a validation problem.
- **ALR tree** (libxml measured): 13 HTML 4 voids, `/>` honoured, 40-pair start-tag auto-close table, end-tag priorities resolved through per-name/per-priority indexes (no stack scan: linear), `html`/`head`/`body` ignored inside the body, nothing after `</body>`; no `<body>` → walked from the document (fragments only). Walk = 3 levels, processed tags, `<use>` skip, 200 cap.
- **ALR subject:** stamped element = served tag minus WP Rocket's stamp, cut at its first `>`; others re-serialized with libxml's own start-tag reading — attribute names `[A-Za-z_:.][-A-Za-z0-9_:.]*` (every ASCII first/next character measured; `.prop` bindings kept), bogus attributes (`@click`, `[x]`, `1a`, stray `/`) skipped to the next blank/`>`/`/>` regardless of quotes (`@click.away="open = false"` leaves `false`), a bogus attribute's `>` ends the tag; refs decoded (numeric `;` optional, named `;` required), `&<>` re-encoded except raw `<!--…-->` / `&{…}` spans; quote choice; URI escaping; cut at the first `>`. Refused (unknown): control characters, invalid numeric refs, a Latin-1 `&name` running into a non-ASCII byte (libxml drops part of the name), characters the text mode cannot rebuild, markup past libxml's tag end. Text mode from the session's bytes, per position (measured): libxml starts on Latin-1 (UTF-8 after a UTF-8 BOM) and switches at the first charset `<meta>` for the text after it — without a BOM to the declared charset unless non-ASCII precedes it; after a BOM to Latin-1 for an ISO-8859-1 label (non-ASCII before it or not) and UTF-8 for a UTF-8 one; any other label after a BOM is unknown (libxml switches for every label it knows — `l1`, `iso-8859-15` → Latin-1, `us-ascii` empties the value — and keeps UTF-8 only for unknown ones like `bogus`: not modelled). Without a BOM, a non-ASCII byte before any declaration plus a later `http-equiv` anywhere (libxml's `htmlFindEncoding` look-ahead searches the rest of the raw text for `HTTP-EQUIV` … `CONTENT` … `CHARSET=` and switches to UTF-8 on a hit — a refresh meta, even body text) makes the whole page unknown. Latin-1 = UTF-8 bytes → `Ã©`, windows-1252 bytes → as is ≤ U+00FF; other combinations → unknown for non-ASCII tags.
- **ALR outcome:** excluded = the candidate or a processed ancestor matches (outermost match's row gets the count); no match but an unknown on the chain → **undetermined** (left as served, `report.undetermined`); re-key = two walks, `null` when a walked element's match is unknown or a stamped element lies outside the walk. Rows: lone `''`/`"0"` inert; `''` next to others → `problem: 'match-all'`; whitespace-only rows valid literals plus a non-blocking `featureRowWarnings` entry (the whitespace-only Lazyload row stays blocking).
- **Evidence:** replay — K.4d 28 rows / 57 fields → 0 pending; 4 new Lazyload rows green; table pending 178 rows / 228 fields (K.3 79/126, K.4b 32/33, K.4c 67/69). Auditor corpora re-run (real plugin): serializer fuzz 9 000 tags — 0 wrong (was 8), refusals 53 % → 21 %; 85 real-page tags equal; ALR 2 440 random + 124 targeted pages — 0 wrong excluded (was 7), 0 wrong `rekeyed` (was 3), `rekeyed` unknown on 29 (26 are Latin-1 `&name`+byte pages); 745 real-page elements, 0 refused, 327/327 equal; Lazyload 1 200 noscript pages 0 wrong (was 7) + 1 500 with `width`/`height`/`https:`/`data:` sources 0 wrong; without `<noscript>` 559/600 equal, the rest reported `approximate`. Round 3 (new auditor suites): serializer fuzz 12 000 tags (`.`-leading names included) 0 wrong (was 259), 19 % refused; ALR 1 460 pages + `.prop` targets 0 wrong; byte differential 502 pages (BOM × metas × encodings) 0 wrong (was 5 excluded / 6 `rekeyed`), 5 undetermined, `rekeyed` unknown on 190; Lazyload 1 500 noscript pages 0 wrong, 600 stripped — every mismatch flagged (`approximate`/`undetermined`, was 59 silent). Round 4: `bytes2` 1 200 pages 0 wrong (was 57: 27 BOM + other label, 30 look-ahead), 28 undetermined, `rekeyed` unknown on 488; `bytes` 0 wrong; `names` 651/651; every earlier suite still 0 wrong. Perf: stamp + `<span>`×N + `</div>`×N at N = 60 000 (0.72 MB) 34 ms (was 16 s); 2.6 MB real page ≤ 70 ms.
- **Changed expectations:** `lazy-render.test.ts` — `:49` retitled (same assertions); `:67` `'></div>'` now 0 (no end tag), `'class="a">'` 1; void-element test `'lazyrender="1">'` now 0 (stamp not in WP Rocket's subject), `'src="a.jpg">'` 1; regex-literal row `'data-wpr-lazyrender="1" class="pagination"'` → `'<div class="pagination">'`; empty-row test `['']` now valid → `['', 'x']` throws; `:163` `class="pagination"` matches `CLASS=`. `validate.test.ts:55` Lazyload `''` ignored → the case uses `' '`. `lazyload.test.ts` "moves the lazy src back" report gains `approximate: 1` (no `<noscript>` copy). `document-transform.test.ts` "hands the runner…" request gains `bytes: {encoding: 'utf-8', bom: false}`. `lazyload.test.ts` "is not a candidate on a valueless or empty `data-lazy-src`": the empty carrier is now a candidate counted `undetermined` (WP Rocket writes it; was a silent miss), the valueless one still skipped. Serializer tests (`libxml/serialize.test.ts`, new in K.4d) moved from "refused" to libxml's measured output, backed by the fuzz: bogus attributes (`=x`, `/id=`, stray `/`, `@click`, `[a]`, `1a`, `-a`, `a$b`, `é=`) are skipped as libxml does; `&{a}`, `&amp;{a}`, `&#38;{` follow the raw `&{…}` rule; the old "unknown charset" hashing test (windows-1252 meta, UTF-8 bytes) became the undetermined-ancestor test (that page is now `undetermined`, not "kept"). `libxml/charset.test.ts` (round 3, new in K.4d): BOM + `bogus` label `utf-8` → `unknown` (round 4: labels outside the measured UTF-8/ISO-8859-1 sets fail closed). Replay: `rekeyed` from the engine.
- **Named limits:** baseline = no rows (WP Rocket's dynamic `lazy_rendering_exclusions` unknown here); `rocket_lrc_*` filters assumed default (else `rekeyed` `null`); later passes rewriting a hashed tag (background-image lazyload); WP Rocket's first-occurrence replacement of a duplicate opening tag (`rekeyed` `null`); `<textarea>`/`<title>` children; UTF-16 BOM and non-Latin single-byte pages → unknown for non-ASCII tags. Lazyload without `<noscript>`: removed `loading`, quote style, `src` position, `esc_url`'d `src` are not rebuilt (`approximate`).
- **Consumers:** both session backends run the shared pipeline (Chromium in-process, Firefox worker), now with the decoding facts. Gates: compile/lint/format/test green (275 files, 5 730 passed; coverage 98.64/97.06/100/99.26); `parity:wp-rocket` check, `build`, `build:firefox`, `check:budgets` green.
