#### P10K.4e — Defer JS inline-jQuery side effect

Depends:P10K.4c. Parallel:P10K.3, P10K.4b, P10K.6 (if K.4c closed first). Goal: the engine reproduces whether WP Rocket wraps inline jQuery scripts for the current Defer set (K.4c audit finding, 2026-10-22: the transform keeps the wrapping in every case, so wrapped inline code runs after the deferred scripts here while it runs inline on the live site).

WP Rocket 3.23.5.1 (verified 2026-10-22, `inc/Engine/Optimization/DeferJS/DeferJS.php`): `defer_inline_js` (`:103–155`, `rocket_buffer` priority 25, right after `defer_js` at 24 — `Subscriber.php:33–34`) wraps the content of an inline script in `window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {…});` when it matches `jQuery|\$\.\(|\$\(` (filter `rocket_defer_jquery_patterns`), skipping empty, `application/ld+json` and inline-excluded scripts. It **skips the whole pass** when the joined exclusion string — `implode('|', get_excluded())`: the dynamic `defer_js_external_exclusions` list + the stored rows, deduped, `#` → `\#` — matches `/(jquery(?:.*)?\.js)/i` (`:108–112`): a row like `jquery.min.js`, the safe-mode row `/jquery-?[0-9.]*(.min|.slim|.slim.min)?.js`, or `jquery` followed later by **any** row containing `.js` (the test runs on the joined text, not per row). Also: with an empty inline-exclusions list (`defer_js_inline_exclusions` + `rocket_defer_inline_exclusions`) nothing is wrapped (`:142`, `empty(…) || preg_match` → `continue`).

- [x] Harness first (`scripts/wp-rocket-parity.php`): Defer match rows also run the real `defer_inline_js` after `defer_js`, with a non-empty never-matching inline-exclusions sentinel (an empty list would hide the wrapping); new output field `inlineWrapped` (subject keys of wrapped inline scripts) in the table format + parser; `--write` regenerates; existing rows' outputs otherwise unchanged (check-mode proof).
- [x] Fixtures (K.2 table, PHP-derived): `jquery.min.js` row → no wrap; safe-mode row → no wrap; `jquery` + a later `foo.js` row → no wrap; `jquery` alone → wrap; `JQUERY.JS` (case) → no wrap; `jquery` across a `#`-escaped row; inline script without jQuery → not wrapped; `ld+json` → not wrapped; empty inline script; a set whose `defer_js` pre-check fails (`joined-page-off`) — record what `defer_inline_js` does then (it does not share that pre-check). New rows start `pending K.4e`.
- [x] Engine (Defer feature module): one pure predicate over the stored joined string (via the K.4a translator, same byte semantics), computed once per set; when it matches, the transform restores the wrapped inline scripts to their unwrapped content (the page WP Rocket would serve); else it keeps the wrapping. Reverse case (served unwrapped because the live set matches, the draft no longer does): wrap as WP Rocket would, or name it as a limit — decide from the fixtures and record why. Report field per restored script. Dynamic list: the extension cannot read it (remote data) — the predicate uses the stored rows only; named limit in the lane hint copy left to K.5a.
- [x] Tests first: predicate table (cases above), restore of wrapped content byte-exact, no change when the predicate is false; replay flips `inlineWrapped` pending fields.

DoD: every new fixture row asserted (no `pending K.4e` left); `pnpm parity:wp-rocket` check green; gates + budgets green. Effort S · difficulty medium.

**Receipt (2026-10-22, audit fix round folded in).** DoD met. **Default (owner can change): never wrap; undetermined wherever the live rows decide** (below).

- **Verified (run, both PHPs):** the skip test reads `implode('|', get_excluded())` = `array_unique(array_merge(ext, rows))` (first kept) → `#` → `\#` → joined with `|`; the regex runs on that escaped text, so it spans rows (`jquery|foo.js` skips, `foo.js|jquery` wraps, `foo.js|jquery|foo.js` dedupes → wraps; a row duplicating an external-list entry is dropped: real list + `jquery`, `/wp-includes/js/dist/i18n.min.js` → wraps). WP Rocket tests `ext|live|draft` — the tool never reads the live rows (the draft starts empty) — so live `/wp-includes/js/jquery/` or `jquery-migrate` + draft `foo.js` skips, live `foo.js` + draft `jquery`, `foo.js` wraps. PCRE `.` stops at `\n` only, `i` is ASCII-only. `defer_inline_js` has no pre-check (page-off `.*` still wraps). The `ld+json` test reads the whole element; the opening tag ends at the first `>` even inside a quoted value; `str_replace` replaces the element page-wide (attribute values, comments, across a comment's `-->`). Delay JS (26) then rewrites every script its regex meets, comment and attribute copies included. Today's lists: the external list names no `jquery`; the inline list holds `DOMContentLoaded` (no double wrap live; the harness sentinel double-wraps).
- **Certainty rule (`inlinePassUnder`, for a served page that shows the wrapping, which proves `ext|live` alone does not match):** **skipped** — Defer JS disabled, or **one draft row matches `/(jquery(?:.*)?\.js)/i` on its own** (`#`-escaped; dedupe cannot drop it, as its earlier twin would match alone and the page would not be wrapped); **runs** — **no draft row holds `.js` or `jquery`** (ASCII-case-insensitive, after the `#` escape; round 3: a draft `jquery` placed **above** a live `foo.js` skips — the draft's place among the live rows is unknown, so both halves count); **unknown** — everything else (a span across draft rows, a draft row completing a live `jquery` or `.js`, a draft row deduped against a live/ext entry). `skipped` is position-independent. Unknown → the wrapped scripts stay as served and count `undetermined` (Defer report); a Delay subject is read both ways and undetermined when the verdicts differ. The first fix's "rows-only test is exact" claim was false (live rows) and is withdrawn.
- **Unwrap (skipped only):** WP Rocket's own script match on the served page (comments skipped) collects each body that is exactly `OPEN + inner + CLOSE` with jQuery code and no `ld+json` in the element; every `OPEN + inner + CLOSE` on the page is then put back in one left-to-right pass (longest first) — page-wide, as `str_replace` wrote it. The pipeline replays the pass **before Delay JS** (`exclusionPipelineSteps`: first step; Delay and Defer get the replay via `AFTER_INLINE_PASS`), so a Delay restore stamping a live copy no longer splits it from its comment copy; Defer JS reports it (wrapped scripts are Defer candidates, an undone one a rewrite, `inlineUnwrapped ≤ rewritten`, validated in `lib/messaging/exclusion-session.ts`). Standalone transforms replay it themselves.
- **Reverse case (limit, default):** never re-wrapped — the engine only undoes WP Rocket's served work; a set adds to live rows that already skip.
- **Files:** `lib/exclusion-builder/defer-inline-js.ts` (`namesJqueryFile`, `inlinePassUnder`, `unwrapInlineScripts`, `replayInlinePass`) + test; `INLINE_WRAP_OPEN/CLOSE` in `lib/wp-rocket/constants.ts` (pinned in its test); `defer-js.ts`, `delay-js.ts` (`…AfterInlinePass` forms), `pipeline.ts`, `report.ts`, `tag-edit.ts` (`applyPatches`: one join instead of re-slicing per patch — 400 unwraps on 2.5 MiB 112.7 → 5.0 ms; throws on an inverted / out-of-range / non-integer patch naming it); harness `scripts/wp-rocket-parity.php` (`defer_js` → `defer_inline_js`, inline sentinel, `inlineWrapped`) + table (`inlineWrapped` output; authored `inlineUndetermined` reason on Defer rows whose outcome needs live rows — the replay then expects `'undetermined'`).
- **Table:** 13 rows `match-defer-inline-*`; 4 carry `inlineUndetermined` (`jquery`+`foo.js` both orders, `#` escape, dedupe); 306 rows, pending 0; existing rows only gained the empty `inlineWrapped`. `pnpm parity:wp-rocket` reproduces byte-for-byte.
- **Fuzz (scratch; the auditor's `k4e-fz` generator/oracle/comparator + live-row mode: real `defer_js` → `defer_inline_js` → `delay_js`, served with live rows, after with live + draft; modes sentinel / real lists / real + Delay rows / live rows):** PHP 8.1.34 / PCRE2 10.49 — 24 000 sets / 105 509 scripts; docker PHP 8.4.27 / PCRE2 10.44 — 24 000 sets / 105 356 scripts. **0 confident K.4e wrong answers outside r1/r2.** Per run family (local / docker): left as served + undetermined 889 / 906 sets; r1 876 / 893; r2 16 / 12; 0 flagged. Residuals: **(r1)** a source already in wrapper form (`OPEN + jQuery code + CLOSE`, anywhere: script, comment, attribute) — indistinguishable from WP Rocket's output, unwrapped when the pass is skipped; **(r2)** an end tag other than `</script>` (`</script >`): the pass wraps across the next element and Delay JS rewrites the end tag — **flagged since P10L.3** (left as served, undetermined) except **r2b**: an opener whose own body ends in `});`, spanning to a script with a body of its own, reads as one whole wrapper (unflaggable without flagging every page where an unwrapped script ends in `});`; see the P10L.3 receipt). **Round 3 (final rule, verifier's `k4e-fz2` tools):** predicate vs `preg_match` over `ext|live|draft`, 200 000 sets per PHP (8.1 / PCRE2 10.49 and docker 8.4 / 10.44), append and prepend order: append 0 wrong; prepend 2 + 4 wrong, every one with a live row holding a line break — unreachable (WP Rocket stores rows through `sanitize_text_field`, the overlay commits through `splitFieldText`, both drop line breaks). Full chain (sentinel / real / real + Delay / live append / live prepend): local 18 000 sets, docker 15 000 sets — **0 confident wrong outside r1/r2**; the remaining COUNT cases are Delay attribute copies (wrapper counts equal). **Not K.4e (follow-up for the Delay owner):** Delay JS restores never reach script copies inside comments or attribute values (WP Rocket's Delay regex rewrites them; the engine's restore follows parsed elements) — 545 / 545 sets differ there, wrapper counts equal.
- **Named limits:** filters `rocket_exclude_defer_js`, `rocket_defer_jquery_patterns`, `rocket_defer_inline_exclusions` assumed unregistered; the remote lists (a future external entry naming `jquery` is covered by the certainty rule only because a wrapped page proves `ext|live` alone does not match). **(r3)** an unclosed `<!--` in an inline body: WP Rocket strips comments before its inline scan, so that `<!--` runs to the next `-->` — possibly its own `<!-- wpr_defer_js -->` meta comment, present only when Defer JS ran past its pre-check — so when the Defer page state differs between the live run and the draft's, the scripts after it may be scanned in one run and not the other. Optional refinement (the `data-wpr-features` meta) not taken.
- **Wall time (Node 24.21, median of 9, 2.5 MiB, 400 wrapped + 400 deferred scripts, 50 Delay rows):** Defer step 3.9 ms (pass kept) / 5.0 ms (400 unwrapped); Delay step incl. the replay 7.9 / 8.7 ms.
- **Changed tests (all this phase's own):** `defer-inline-js.test.ts` — `inlineWrapRuns` → `inlinePassUnder` (three-state, PHP-run cases incl. the auditor's), "a copy of the body left unwrapped" now unwrapped (body-level, page-wide: it is r1, not WP Rocket output); `defer-js.test.ts` — wrapped scripts count as candidates (2 not 1) and rewrites, `['foo.js', 'jquery-ui']` now undetermined; round 3: `['jquery']` / `['jquery-ui']` now `unknown` (PHP-pinned prepend cases), `match-defer-inline-jquery-alone` gained `inlineUndetermined`, the replay reads `inlineWrapped` from the Defer step's own report (`undetermined`, `inlineUnwrapped` checked against the scripts it unwrapped); parity rows above gained `inlineUndetermined`; `exclusion-session.test.ts` — the Defer report patch carries candidates/rewritten ≥ the count.
- **Budgets (raw KiB `c83f35e` → this; gzip):** background 175.9 → 178.9 (58.6 → 59.6); Firefox background 171.9 → 174.9 (57.1 → 58.2); overlay ×2 228.7 → 228.9 (77.2 → 77.3); Firefox engine worker 78.3 → 81.1 (26.9 → 27.9); whole build 1 397.2 → 1 400.3 — rows set to measured + 0.1 in the `build(budgets)` commits.

#### P10K.5a — Overlay: stored-form notes, copy-all, lane hints, counts

Depends:P10K.3, P10K.4b, P10K.4c, P10K.4d, P10K.4e. Parallel:—. Goal: the user sees, before Apply, every row WP Rocket would store or match differently, and the draft holds the stored form from commit on (owner 2026-10-22, P10K rule 1).

- [x] **Commit-time conversion** — delivered by K.3 (`98e9212`: `commitRows`/`commitAll`/`restoreDraft` in `exclusions/draft.ts`, wired in the controller's commit, insert, paste, candidate, load and apply paths). K.5a consumes the controller's `commits()` (per-row diffs carrying the typed text) for notes, announcements and undo — e.g. a paste's one summary note + announcement ("N pasted rows changed to WP Rocket's stored form, M removed"), and the same notes when a typed-row draft converts on restore. Never while typing; Apply commits every row, the focused one included.
- [x] **Follow-up (verifier MINOR):** `commitRows` labels a second `(` as `invalid-pattern` where PHP says `duplicate` (the stored set is the same): the committed row's form is not added to the dedupe set when the pattern check drops it. Align the reason with WP Rocket's attribution before the note shows it.
- [x] **Notes (W-1 final words; W-2 tone):** one note slot per row (merged with the shipped invisible-characters note, so a row never stacks two): "WP Rocket stores this as `…` (you typed `…`)"; unverified: "WP Rocket's handling of this row isn't checked here — kept as typed"; safe mode per O3. Removed rows: an inline lane note (the paste-outcome line's slot) "Removed `…`: WP Rocket drops empty rows / `0` / a copy of row N / an invalid pattern". The "you typed" part lives only in memory for the overlay's life; it clears on the row's next edit.
- [x] **Announce** every conversion and removal through the shared `announcer.ts` (polite, newest wins), naming the row number and the outcome; nothing announced for `unchanged`.
- [x] **Undo (recommended default; owner can change):** each conversion/removal note offers **Undo**, valid until the next edit in that lane: it restores the typed text into its row (a removed row is re-inserted at its index) and moves focus into it for editing; leaving it commits again (same conversion, same note — the stored form stays the only committed state). Rejected: no undo (a removal loses the typed text, only the note shows it); "keep my text" (would break the decided mirroring). Native field undo stays reset after a programmatic value change (documented in the feedback round).
- [x] **A11y:** note linked to its row input via `aria-describedby`; Undo is a real button, keyboard-reachable right after its row in Tab order, accessible name "Undo change to row N" / "Restore removed row N"; focus never moves at commit (the user just left the row) — only Undo moves it; outcome never color-only (K-5); notes wrap at 320 px (D-1).
- [x] Tests first: commit conversion per diff kind (each table-driven from the K.3 helper, plus the K.2 save fixtures' typed → stored rows), paste batch, restore of a typed-row draft, row being typed not converted, duplicate removes the row being committed, unknown/safe-mode kept, Undo restores + re-commit converts again, Undo expires on the next lane edit, announcer text per outcome; the draft and persisted value hold only stored rows.
- [x] Copy-all copies the stored form (`exclusionFieldText` → the K.3 function); K.4a row problems (incl. `joined-changes-matching`, `match-all`) render through the existing invalid-row path.
- [x] ALR: copy-all copies the rows as a ready-to-paste note for the `rocket_lrc_exclusions` filter (no field is implied; the shipped copy hint stays); a lane note: "Excluding an element re-keys the elements after it: WP Rocket keeps them rendered until its lazy-render data is regenerated."
- [x] Lane hints (`copy.ts:52,60` and the Lazyload/ALR ones) reworded to the K.4 subjects ("…matched against the script element, case-insensitively", "…matched against the script's src value", "Text, matched against the image's attributes", "Text, matched case-insensitively against the element's opening tag"); Defer per-row match counts labelled best-effort.
- [x] Counts/validation/Apply read stored + runtime semantics (no UI-side re-derivation).
- [x] **K.4e report (recommended default; owner can change):** the Defer lane line "N of M deferred scripts restored" now counts wrapped inline scripts in M and must show `undetermined`. Any draft row holding `.js` or `jquery` that names no jQuery file alone makes every wrapped inline script undetermined: show one page-level line — "Whether inline jQuery stays wrapped depends on your existing Defer JS exclusions" — instead of a per-script count.
- [x] UI bar §D receipt (D-1 320 px, D-2, D-3, D-4, D-6), W-1/W-2 string deltas listed; live check on the docker site: a Delay JS row typed in different case matches, a typed `a  b*` row becomes its stored form on leaving it with the "you typed" note, Undo restores it for editing, a duplicate row is removed with its note, copy-all pastes into WP Rocket's field and saves to the same rows, a picked `+` row excludes live (owner-visible proof).

DoD: owner-visible flow above passes on Chromium; Firefox build green; gates + budgets green. Effort M · difficulty medium.

**Split (2026-10-22, worker proposal — owner can merge back):** K.5a was too large for one reviewable change. **K.5a.1** (done, receipt below) = notes, undo, announcements, the duplicate attribution. **K.5a.2** (open) = the unticked bullets above plus the receipts' follow-ups: one problem→reason table unifying `regexRowProblems` (`pcre/rows.ts`) with `literalRowProblem` (`validate.ts`) and the Delay backtracking example (`(a+)+` is literal there); wire `featureRowWarnings` (ALR whitespace-only rows, non-blocking); honest `undetermined`/`approximate` counts + the Defer line reworded + the inline-jQuery page line; new lane hints; ALR filter note + `lazyRenderRekeyed` lane note; copy-all edge cases; live check on the docker site incl. the WP Rocket field round trip. Named explicitly (audit 2026-10-22): (a) the K.4e named-limit hint in the Defer lane copy — the remote `defer_js_external_exclusions`/inline lists are not read and the `rocket_exclude_defer_js` / `rocket_defer_jquery_patterns` / `rocket_defer_inline_exclusions` filters are assumed unregistered (K.4e engine bullet + named limits); (b) "Defer per-row match counts labelled best-effort" (report `rowCountsBestEffort`); (c) copy-all edge cases — a row still being typed when Copy all is pressed (commits on the button's focus first, so the copy is the stored form), a lane whose rows are all blank (explained state), unverified rows (copied as kept), ALR's filter-note form, a selection-copy fallback page; (d) the docker live check re-proves K.5a.1's flows: the stored-as note + Undo, leaving the row again keeps them, duplicate removal + Restore.

**K.5a.1 receipt (2026-10-22).** O1–O6 still open: recommended defaults encoded (O3: conditional safe-mode note, row kept; Undo per the recommended default).

- **Files:** `entrypoints/exclusion-overlay.content/exclusions/row-notes.ts` (new, pure: `recordCommits`, `retireLane`, `forgetRow`, `takeUndo`, `isNews`, `isConversion`) + test · `controller.ts` (`notes()`, `undoCommit`, `commitRow` returns its commit; edit paths retire undo) · `exclusions/draft.ts` (`reinsertRow`; duplicate attribution) · `exclusions/lane.tsx` (merged note slot, Undo/Restore, removed-rows list, focus rescue) · `exclusions/mode.tsx` (announcement, once per batch) · `copy.ts` (`ROW_NOTE_COPY`, `commitAnnouncement`, `NoteText` runs) · `overlay.css` · e2e `tests/e2e/suites/exclusion-overlay-notes.spec.ts` + `tests/e2e/fixtures/exclusion/`.
- **Lifetimes (decided here; corrected in the audit round below):** a row note lasts until that row is edited or removed — a commit that leaves it as it is (leaving it again, an arrow pass, an apply, a restored draft's focus pass) keeps note and Undo; removal notes and the batch summary last until the next lane edit; undo ends with the next lane edit (edit/insert/paste/remove/pick; a commit, an undo, a disable toggle or another lane's edit is not one — a paste's batch stays undoable while its caret row commits). Consequence: Enter → typing in the new row retires the previous row's Undo (Shift+Tab back to it before typing). Removed rows go back after their anchor (see the audit round: order-independent across batches too). Removals listed: 3, the rest counted. A picked duplicate is no news (was published as a `dropped` commit on the existing row — would have listed a removal that never happened).
- **Duplicate attribution:** `commitRows` adds the form of a row dropped by the pattern check (or safe mode) to the dedupe set → the second `(` is `duplicate` (PHP fixture `save-delay-escaped-star`: `\*` invalid-pattern, `\.*` duplicate); an emptied row adds nothing (later empties stay `empty`).
- **Announcements:** mode-level effect over `notes().latest`, each batch object once (WeakSet: the panel mounts after the load, a deferred effect missed the restore batch; remounts never repeat). One change → "‹lane› row N: WP Rocket stores it as …" / "‹lane› row N removed: WP Rocket drops …"; more → the batch count. Flag-only news (unverified kept as typed, safe mode) is not announced. A pick's own "Added …" is announced after and wins (picked rows are their own stored form — K.4b test — so a pick has no conversion to lose). A paste-cap message likewise wins over the paste's count.
- **Focus:** never moves at commit, except a rescue: leaving a row whose commit removes it while focus heads to that row's own control (Tab from the input lands on its Remove button, gone with the row) → focus lands on its Restore button (else Add row), never on the page — e2e-proven in Chromium.
- **String deltas (W-1/W-2, all new):** "WP Rocket stores this as `…` (you typed `…`)." · "WP Rocket’s handling of this row isn’t checked here, so the preview doesn’t apply it; kept as typed." / "… Whether WP Rocket keeps it isn’t checked here, so the preview doesn’t apply it." (plan: "— kept as typed"; reworded: unverified rows keep their per-row-stage form and match nothing here, K.3) · "WP Rocket drops this row when Safe Mode for jQuery is on (that setting can’t be read from the page)." · "Removed `…`: WP Rocket drops rows left empty once saved / rows that read `0` / a copy of row N (another row) / patterns PCRE rejects: `…` (detail) / this row while Safe Mode for jQuery is on." · "N pasted/saved rows changed to WP Rocket’s stored form, M removed." / "N … rows removed: WP Rocket drops them when saving." · "N more rows removed." · buttons "Undo" / "Restore" with names "Undo change to ‹lane› row N" / "Restore removed ‹lane› row N" (plan: "… row N" without the lane — the lane is named because four lanes each have a row 1; visible "Restore" on removals: axe `label-content-name-mismatch` flagged "Undo" vs "Restore removed …", WCAG 2.5.3) · list name "‹lane› rows removed" · announcements above. Quoted row texts cut at 64 code points (`…`), always as code runs (a backtick in a row cannot break the note).
- **§D:** D-1 320/480/768/1280 px × light/dark on the fixture page (paste batch: a converted row, a converted row with invisible characters, a removed duplicate, the summary): no overflow in the overlay's scrollers/panel, notes wrap. D-2 `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` + both palettes: shadow root `getAnimations()` empty once settled (in-flight 0.01 ms transitions right after the paste); no new motion. D-3 keyboard-only walk (e2e): type → Tab (focus stays on Remove) → Tab → Undo → Enter (row focused, caret at end) → Tab (re-converts); duplicate → Tab → focus on Restore → Enter. D-4 axe over the overlay host, 8 runs: 0 violations (after the Restore label fix). D-6 grep over the changed files: empty. - **Tests:** `row-notes.test.ts` (diff kinds table, lifetimes, undo order, and every PHP save fixture of the K.2 table (text lines, safe mode unknown) committed as one paste: lane rows = PHP `stored`, removal notes = PHP `dropped` (index + reason), undo only on changed rows) · `draft.test.ts` (duplicate attribution, `reinsertRow`) · `controller.test.ts` (undo round trip, expiry per edit kind, removed-row restore, locked refusal, paste batch, restore notes + reset, apply notes, picked duplicate no news) · `copy.test.ts` (every note/announcement string) · `lane.test.tsx` (note + `aria-describedby`, Undo/Restore + focus + label in name, merged slot, remount, rescue, 3 + "more", locked) · `app.test.tsx` (restore announcement through the live region). **Changed:** `lane.test.tsx` invisible-note test reads the note's own id (moved from the `<p>` to the note's text span so Undo stays out of the description). - **Budgets (raw KiB `dd2bb78` → this; gzip):** overlay ×2 228.9 → 236.7 (77.3 → 79.8); whole build 1 400.3 → 1 408.2; rows → measured + 0.1 in `build(budgets)`.

**K.5a.1 audit rework (2026-10-22).** Supersedes the bullets above where they differ. Every finding reproduced first with the auditor's tests (`k5a1-audit-tests/`, ported into the repo's suites).

- **C1 (critical) — notes vanished on any re-commit.** `recordCommits` deleted a row's note when the row came back `unchanged`, and leaving a row always re-commits it: the paste target lost its note as focus moved to the caret row, Shift+Tab/Tab and arrow passes dropped Undo, Apply wiped every note, a restored draft's notes went on the first focus pass. Fix: a batch without news returns the notes untouched; only an edit or a removal (`forgetRow`) clears a row's note. Tests: controller (re-leave ×3, apply, paste target, restored draft + focus pass, rapid rounds, two lanes), lane (Shift+Tab→Tab, arrows), row-notes (unchanged batch is identity), e2e (keyboard re-entry, arrows + a blocked apply keep note and Undo).
- **M1 — O(rows × duplicates).** `copiedRow` ran `storedRowKey` over the lane per duplicate. Now `laneKeyIndex` (one pass) per lane and batch, built only when a duplicate is present; the list builds it once per render. Chromium (headless, fixture page, `PerformanceObserver` long tasks): 250-row lane + 250 reversed duplicates **7–17 ms** sync (was 41–62 ms), 250-row seed paste 23–33 ms, 499 converting lines 32–33 ms, Restore click — 0 long tasks in every run. Node (auditor's vitest perf): 42.4 → 4.3 ms.
- **M2 — long rows.** Notes quote both texts cut around their first difference (`utils/difference-excerpt.ts`, 64 code points, 12 of context, same start for both); the announcement uses the same excerpt. A change that only trimmed the ends says so in words: "WP Rocket stores this without the spaces at its ends." (announcement: "… stores it without the spaces at its ends.").
- **M3 — stale row numbers.** `RemovedNote` no longer stores `copyOf`; "a copy of row N" and Restore's "row N" are read from the lane as it is at render (`copiedRow` + `restoreIndex`).
- **Restore order across batches (A4).** A removed row now records its anchor (nearest kept row before it) and the rows removed before it under that anchor (this batch's and earlier ones'); it goes back after the anchor, past those that are back already, following a removed anchor's own place; former index as the fallback. Pinned in both orders: one batch, two batches (`[x,x,b,x,tail]`), duplicates elsewhere in the lane, a removed anchor. Two identical removals under one anchor can both read "row 1" until one is back (both truly go there).
- **Minor:** `controller.commits()` removed (no consumer; `notes()` carries the same per-row diffs) · removed-list name → `ROW_NOTE_COPY.removedListName` · "patterns PCRE rejects" → "patterns it can’t read" · e2e test 1's wrong comment (an edit of the row itself drops its note too) fixed and asserted · e2e legs clear the origin's saved draft first (the overlay reopens on it — later legs inherited earlier rows).
- **A10 (auditor):** "reset clears latest" failed only because `reset()` acts on an active session; ported against an active session (passes). Notes after a real apply's reload are gone by design (the typed text lives in the overlay's memory; rule 1).
- **Changed tests (and why):** controller K.3 tests read `notes().latest.news` / `notes().lanes…removed` instead of the removed `commits()` (same diffs); "lists a removed duplicate…" pins the anchor shape (`copyOf` moved to the announcement's news); `row-notes.test.ts` expectations on the old `batch`/`copyOf`/index-minus-batch model rewritten for anchors; `copy.test.ts` — the unverified/safe-mode merged-note cases now hit the ends-trim wording (their typed rows differ only by end spaces), "PCRE rejects" wording replaced.
- **Gates:** compile/lint/format/test green (289 files, 6 769 passed + 1 skipped; coverage 98.64/96.76/100/99.35), `parity:wp-rocket` byte-for-byte, `build`, `build:firefox`, `check:budgets` green. **e2e (Docker back up): full `pnpm test:e2e` serial — 195 passed, 8 failed, all in the known mega.wp-rocket.me set** (`options-kc-freshness` ×3, `site-badge-pipeline` ×4, `site-known-conflicts` "an empty storage is served through the ask path"); new suite 6/6; the auditor's spec 4/4 (axe 0 light/dark).

Wording otherwise unchanged.

ALR lane note while it holds rows: "Excluding an element re-keys the elements after it: WP Rocket keeps them rendered until its lazy-render data is regenerated." (static — `lazyRenderRekeyed` is not run per page; named limit).
- **Copy-all edge cases:** the lane commits before copying (`controller.commitLane`), so a row still being typed — Copy all activated without focus leaving it — is copied stored (unit) and live (the round trip below); all-blank lane explains instead of copying; unverified rows copied as kept; selection-copy fallback unchanged (its tests stand).
