#### P10K.5b — Overlay: Defer page-level problem + Apply behavior

Depends:P10K.5a (shared lane UI), P10K.4c. Parallel:—. Goal: a Defer set WP Rocket would refuse page-wide is visible as such, and Apply follows O2.

- [x] Only for `joined-page-off`: a lane-level message above the Defer rows naming the row(s) in their stored form (K.5a; rows `pcre-invalid` at save were already removed on commit) — "WP Rocket would stop deferring every script on this page because of `…`" (W-1 final words); no page-level message for any other Defer problem.
- [x] Name the row(s) responsible for page-off even when they are unverified (verifier MINOR): today an unverified row that breaks the joined pattern (e.g. `\p{L}\Qab` next to `a\.js`) turns the page `off` while no row carries a problem — `rowProblem` returns null for an unknown save before the page-off check.
- [x] Apply per O2 (default: blocked with the message, focus moves to it); the controller's apply gate (`exclusion-overlay.content/controller.ts:368/429` path) and the session prevalidation agree.
- [x] UI bar §D receipt for the message (D-1 320 px, D-3, D-4); live check: two rows sharing a group name show the message; unscoped-flag rows show only a row problem.

DoD: both cases receipted on Chromium; gates + budgets green. Effort S · difficulty easy.

**Receipt (2026-10-22).** O1–O6 still open: recommended defaults encoded (O2: page-off blocks Apply). 4 commits: engine fix · overlay · e2e leg · `build(budgets)`.

- **Engine (`lib/exclusion-builder/`):** `pcre/rows.ts` `rowProblem` checks the page-off before the unknown-save early return, so an unverified row behind a page-off carries `joined-page-off` (still no matcher) and the set fails validation — background and overlay alike (before: `\p{L}\Qab` + `a\.js` validated, page `off`, nothing named). `defer-js-rows.ts` `pageOffRows` (indices carrying `joined-page-off`; empty when only rows the save drops turn the page off — their `pcre-invalid` reason already says "it turns Defer JS off on the page"). `ROW_REASONS.joinedPageOff` reworded to the row's own fact (it read "Joined with the other rows" for a lone `.*` and repeated the page consequence the lane message now carries). Proof: the row is named whichever order; "page `off` ⇒ some row problem" pinned over 8 off sets; `parity:wp-rocket` byte-for-byte, replay unchanged.
- **Overlay:** new pure `exclusions/lane-page.ts` — registry `SET_READINGS = {deferJs: deferRowSet}`: the lane's non-blank rows through `saveExclusionRows` (rows the save drops — typed duplicates, PCRE-rejected — left out, as their commit will), read as one set → `{page, offRows: {id, position, stored}[], problems: Map<rowId, reason>}`. `controller.ts`: one memo per lane fed by that lane's array only; `rowProblem` = server reason ?? set reading ?? per-row check, so joined-only problems (`joined-page-off`, `joined-changes-matching`) mark rows **before** Apply (they showed only after the background refused); `lanePage(lane)`, `pageMessageFocus`; a blocked apply (and a refused one) focuses the page message when a lane has page-off rows, else the first invalid row. Gate agreement: on the committed draft the lane reading equals `prevalidateExclusionSet(draftToSet(draft))` row for row (7 sets, unit). `lane.tsx`: `PageOffMessage` above the rows — `role="group"` named by its lead, `tabindex="-1"` (focus target, visible 2 px ring on `:focus`, never a live region: it appears while typing), each row "Go to row N" + stored form as code (64 code points), 3 named + "And N more rows.", the fix line; the unknown-page lane note (hidden while the feature is disabled); the report's `pageState: 'unknown'` → `LaneCounts.pageUnknown` → count caveat, dropped while the lane note says the same of the current rows (one line, never two). `joined-changes-matching` stays a row problem only.
- **Decisions / deviations:** (1) No new notice kind: page-off rows are row problems, so the shipped `invalid` notice ("Fix N rows before applying") + focus on the message is the block; the message is the reason. (2) Message only when a row carries `joined-page-off` (above). (3) Live check's group-name rows are `(?'n'a)` / `(?'n'b)`: `(?<n>a)` doesn't survive WP Rocket's save (`sanitize_text_field` strips `<n>` as a tag → `(?a)`, the second copy is then dropped as `(?b)` invalid). Named groups are also `pcre-only` alone, so the live "fix" removes those rows; the unit/e2e unblock uses `.*` → `\.js$` and the unverified `\p{L}\Qab` → `\.min\.js$`.
- **String deltas (W-1/W-2, all new except the first):** row reason "This row makes WP Rocket’s Defer JS check fail (it checks all the rows joined as one pattern)" (was "Joined with the other rows, WP Rocket’s Defer JS check fails on this row: it would defer no script on the page") · lead "WP Rocket would stop deferring every script on this page because of this row:" / "… these rows:" (plan: "… because of `…`" — the rows follow as a list, each with Go to) · "Go to row N" · "And N more row(s)." · "Edit or remove it/them to apply." · lane note "WP Rocket may defer no script on this page with these rows — not checked here." · count caveat "These counts assume WP Rocket defers scripts on this page: with the applied rows, that isn’t checked here."
- **§D (scratch spec on the docker site `/fdt/?fdt-delay=1`, Chromium headless):** D-1 320/480/768/1280 × light/dark with the message holding a long unverified row: no horizontal overflow in the overlay's scrollers/panel/message, code wraps; D-2 `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`, both palettes: shadow root `getAnimations()` 0, no new motion; D-3 keyboard: Enter on Apply → focus on the message (outline solid) → Tab "Go to row 1" → Tab "Go to row 3" → Enter → row 3 focused; D-4 axe over the overlay host 0 violations ×8; D-6 grep over the changed files (CSS: added lines) empty; D-7 (P-3) a 500-row Defer lane, 8 keystrokes in Chromium: 0 long tasks (Node: set reading ≈ 22 ms median per keystroke at 500 long rows — save 11 + joined replay 11).
- **Live check (same spec, owner-visible):** `(?'n'a)`, `fdt-delayed`, `(?'n'b)` → message naming rows 1 and 3, Apply blocked ("Fix 2 rows before applying"), focus on the message; rows fixed → applied, Defer count shown. `(?-i)zzz` + `FDT-DELAYED-REFERENCE` → only row 1's `joined-changes-matching` problem, no message, Apply focuses the row. `\p{L}` + `fdt-delayed-reference` → unknown note; applied → "1 of 4 deferred and wrapped scripts restored", note once, no caveat; row edited to `zzz` → note gone, count caveat shown. Screenshots: `/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-sandyfigueroa-orca-workspaces-wpr-front-debugging-tool-p10-implementation/330f65c1-1ca4-4719-8855-d6c912324698/scratchpad/k5b-live/k5b-page-off-{320,480,768,1280}-{light,dark}.png` + `live-{group-name-page-off,group-name-focused-row,group-name-fixed-applied,unscoped-flag-row-problem,unknown-applied,unknown-counts-caveat}.png` (spec `zz-k5b-live.spec.ts` there).
- **Tests:** new `lane-page.test.ts` (lanes read, positions with blanks, unverified, dropped rows left out, joined-only problem, unknown, prevalidation agreement); `pcre/rows.test.ts` (unverified page-off named both orders; off ⇒ a problem); `defer-js-rows.test.ts` (`pageOffRows`); `validate.test.ts` (unverified page-off refused); `controller.test.ts` (block + message focus + fix unblocks; unverified block + session refuses; joined-only row problem before asking; lanes without a page; memo isolation); `lane.test.tsx` (message above rows, words, Go to; unverified + blank position; refused apply focuses it; 3 + more; no message for a joined-only problem; unknown note + disabled; caveat once); `counts.test.ts`, `copy.test.ts` (every new string). e2e notes suite: new leg (unverified page-off blocks, named, focus, Go to, fix unblocks, applied). **Changed:** `pcre/rows.test.ts` "reasons name the joined effect" reads `ROW_REASONS.joinedPageOff` (reworded); `counts.test.ts`/`copy.test.ts` count shapes gain `pageUnknown: false` (new field).
- **Gates:** compile/lint/format green; `pnpm test` 292 files, 6 822 passed + 1 skipped (coverage 98.64/96.76/100/99.35); `parity:wp-rocket` byte-for-byte; `build`, `build:firefox`, `check:budgets` green. **e2e:** full `pnpm test:e2e` serial (`E2E_FIXTURE_PORT=8897`) — 198 passed, 7 failed = the known set (`options-kc-freshness` ×3, `site-badge-pipeline` ×4); notes suite 8/8.
- **Budgets (raw KiB, gzip; rows → measured + 0.1 in `build(budgets)`):** overlay ×2 241.9 → 245.1 (81.8 → 82.8); background 179.6 → 179.7; Firefox background 175.6 → 175.7; whole build 1 414.2 → 1 417.3; Firefox engine worker unchanged.

**K.5b audit rework (2026-10-22).** Supersedes the bullets above where they differ. Audit: engine exact (9 394 sets vs PHP, 0 false/missed page-off), overlay ↔ background agree on every set; findings verified before fixing.

- **MAJOR — per-keystroke set reading (confirmed: 4× throttle 63–82 ms per keystroke at 500 rows).** (1) `field-text.ts` memoizes the per-row save stages by `(feature, text)` (bounded: 4 096 rows, 512 Ki chars; byte rows uncached), so a re-save pays only for the changed row (paste and commit paths benefit too). (2) New `exclusions/set-readings.ts`: each set-read lane is read 150 ms (`SET_READING_DELAY_MS`) after its last change, in its own task; a run whose lane changed since it was scheduled is dropped. Until the next reading lands, the last one speaks only for what it read unchanged — row problems of unchanged rows, and the page message only while **every** row it names is unchanged (an edit to a named row hides it at once; an edit elsewhere keeps it ≤ 150 ms, so nothing blinks while typing). (3) Apply reads every set-read lane synchronously after its commit (`readNow`), so the block stays exact before any pause; the fix-rows notice is judged only on settled readings. Single-row checks stay immediate. **Chromium, auditor's spec + a long-task/LoAF leg (`…/scratchpad/k5b-live/zz-k5b-audit-perf.spec.ts`), 499 rows + 1, sync handler ms:** keystrokes ×4 — before rework 63–82 → after 0.2–6.9 (pre-K.5b base `817359d`: 0.7–7.1); typing 10 keys at 60 ms intervals then 1.5 s idle (the debounced reading included) — 0 long tasks / 0 LoAF ≥ 50 ms at ×1 and ×4, plain/heavy/named sets. Paste ×1: 14–43 ms (before rework 44–53; base 14–34). **Paste ×4 stays over 50 ms: 60–119 ms sync, LoAF 83–167 ms — identical to the pre-K.5b base (60–119, 86–140)**: the paste's own commit of 499 rows (K.3/K.5a save + render), not the set reading; making it chunked is a separate change (follow-up).
- **MINOR 1 — page message's name: not reproduced.** The group already had `aria-labelledby` → its lead (the auditor's probe printed `aria-label`, which is unset); Chromium's accessible name is the lead text — now pinned in the notes e2e (`toHaveAccessibleName`, `role="group"`) besides the lane test's `getByRole('group', { name })`.
- **MINOR 2 — duplicate attribution (confirmed, aligned).** The lane reading now commits the uncommitted rows the way the editor's commit does: new `draft.commitLaneRows` (the lane half of `commitRows`); targets = rows edited since their last commit (controller-tracked: edit/undo/paste caret row add, any commit/remove/load/reset clears) ∪ rows not in their stored form; the rows already there win, so a typed copy of a page-off row is not named — the copy that stays is. With nothing uncommitted (the apply's commit of every row) the first copy stays, as before.
- **Tests:** new — `lane-page.test.ts` "names the copy that stays" (typed copy above / nothing uncommitted / non-stored row); controller: apply blocks before any pause, last reading kept for unchanged rows and dropped for changed ones, a scheduled reading the lane outran, the copy that stays across its commit. **Changed:** controller "clears the fix-rows notice" now fires the reading timer before expecting the notice gone (it is judged on a settled reading); the K.5b controller/lane tests drive the timer (lane tests: `readyController(schedule)`, timers off by default as before); `lane-page.test.ts` PCRE-rejected-row case passes the row as uncommitted (a committed draft can't hold it).
- **Gates:** compile/lint/format green; `pnpm test` 292 files, 6 826 passed + 1 skipped (coverage 98.64/96.76/100/99.36); `parity:wp-rocket` byte-for-byte; `build`, `build:firefox`, `check:budgets` green. e2e: notes suite 8/8, auditor’s spec + perf legs 10/10 (axe 0 light/dark, no 320 px overflow), docker live spec 12/12 (re-run on the rework).
- **Budgets (measured + 0.1):** overlay ×2 245.1 → 246.5 (82.8 → 83.2); background 179.7 → 179.9; Firefox background 175.7 → 175.9; Firefox engine worker 81.9 → 82.0 (memo); whole build 1 417.3 → 1 419.0.

**K.5b verifier fix (2026-10-22).** Verifier confirmed the perf fix (keystrokes back to base), the debounce under adversarial cases, the memo, and the UX decision (message kept ≤ 150 ms; Apply reads synchronously).

- **CRITICAL (confirmed, since `61629cb`): the overlay blocked an apply the session accepts.** `a\Qb` + `zz\E`: background valid (PHP agrees: `#(a\Qb|zz\E)#i` passes the pre-check), set reading no problem (page `unknown`), but `rowProblem` fell back to the single-row check, where `a\Qb` alone is `joined-page-off` → invalid count 1, Apply blocked even after `readNow`. Fix: `JOINED_ONLY_PROBLEMS` (`defer-js-rows.ts`: `joined-page-off`, `joined-changes-matching`) — the row validator drops them; the lane's set reading is their only reporter. Per-row problems stay immediate.
- **Tests:** the repro (pure + controller: apply goes out); an apply right after a fix before the pause (verifier D1; D2 was already covered by "blocks at once"); new property test `exclusions/set-agreement.test.ts` — over 1 200 seeded adversarial sets (the audit generator's fragments, ported), the editor's problem (set reading, else single-row check) equals `prevalidateExclusionSet`'s for every row the applied set holds, in three states (all typed, all committed, committed + a typed caret row — half the time a copy), and again on the committed set as at apply. Proven non-ornamental: it fails on the unfixed validator. Locally with the audit corpus (`SET_AGREEMENT_FILES=…/k5b-fz/c{1..4}.jsonl`): **10 594 sets, all agree**.
- **Gates:** compile/lint/format green; `pnpm test` 293 files, 6 830 passed + 1 skipped (coverage 98.65/96.81/100/99.36); `parity:wp-rocket` byte-for-byte; `build`, `build:firefox`, `check:budgets` green; notes e2e 8/8 + auditor's spec/perf legs 10/10 (typing ×1/×4: 0 long tasks; axe 0).
- **Recorded (no action):** 499-line paste at ×4 throttle 57–131 ms on both base and rework (P10L list). One debounced reading ≈ 11 ms in Node for 500 rows (≈ 45 ms at ×4) — near the 50 ms edge; no long task observed.
- **Budgets (measured + 0.1):** overlay ×2 246.5 → 246.6 (83.2 → 83.3); whole build 1 419.0 → 1 419.1.

#### P10K.5c — Warn, don't block: rows WP Rocket applies as-is (O1/O2 decided)

Owner 2026-10-22 (O1, O2 below). Depends:P10K.5b. Parallel:P10K.6. Goal: a row WP Rocket stores and applies so that it excludes every candidate (`match-all`) and a Defer JS set whose joined pre-check fails (`joined-page-off`) **warn and apply**, mirrored exactly; rows the engine cannot apply faithfully keep blocking.

- [x] Engine: transforms apply `match-all` (matches every candidate) and Defer page-off (WP Rocket defers nothing: every deferred script restored, the report says why) instead of throwing; parity fixtures with those dispositions assert WP Rocket's PHP outputs.
- [x] Validation + messaging: a non-blocking warnings channel (`featureRowWarnings` extended); background prevalidation and the overlay agree on problems **and** warnings (`set-agreement.test.ts`); additive contract fields, validated at the boundary.
- [x] UI: warnings in the row's one note slot; page-off message informative; "Fix N rows" counts blocking rows only; after Apply the counts carry the warning context; strings via `copy.ts`.
- [x] Tests (unit, component, property), e2e notes suite (page-off leg applies; new O1 leg), live docker check.

DoD: classification below encoded, both owner cases receipted on Chromium, gates + budgets green, full e2e serial. Effort M · difficulty medium.

**Receipt (2026-10-22).** 3 commits + `build(budgets)`: engine + overlay (one behavior: the two gates must agree) · e2e · plan.

| Problem / row                                                                                     | Before | Now   | Why                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ | ----- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `match-all` (`(?a)`-style, `.*` in Delay, empty alternative, ALR `''` next to others, exact `''`) | block  | warn  | WP Rocket excludes every candidate; the engine matches through the empty pattern — exact (12 fixtures assert the PHP outputs)                                      |
| `joined-page-off`                                                                                 | block  | warn  | WP Rocket defers no script; the engine restores every deferred script and leaves the inline-jQuery pass to its own rule (it has no pre-check) — exact (6 fixtures) |
| `joined-changes-matching`                                                                         | block  | block | Joined, the row (or the rows after it) matches differently: no exact matcher, so the engine cannot reproduce WP Rocket's result                                    |
| `pcre-only` · `pcre-invalid` · `backtracking-risk`                                                | block  | block | Not checked here · WP Rocket drops it · cannot run safely                                                                                                          |
| Entry without an id (whitespace-only row, wire caps, not stored form)                             | block  | block | Editor mistake / wire contract                                                                                                                                     |
| ALR whitespace-only row                                                                           | warn   | warn  | Unchanged (blank in the editor)                                                                                                                                    |

- **Engine (`lib/exclusion-builder/`):** `pcre/problems.ts` `NON_BLOCKING_PROBLEMS` (leaf, also read by the parity table); `errors.ts` `isBlockingProblem`, `throwIfBlocking`, `matchAllRows`; `validate.ts` `featureRowReading` → `{problems, warnings}` (`featureRowProblems` = blocking; `featureRowWarnings(feature, rowKind, rows)` = non-blocking problems + whitespace ALR rows, row order), `prevalidateExclusionSet` gains `warnings`; `pcre/rows.ts` a `match-all` row's matcher is the empty pattern (`(?a)` has no JS equivalent yet matches every subject); `delay-js-match.ts` `readDelayRows` (`compileDelayRows` = its matcher); Defer page-off restores every candidate (no row credited); with a match-all row Defer's `pageState: 'unknown'` is dropped (every script is restored whatever the pre-check says). `report.ts` (additive): `pageState: 'off'` + `pageOffRows`, `matchAllRows`; a page-off report credits no row. `lib/messaging/exclusion-session.ts` validates them (set rows, non-empty, `pageOffRows` only with `off`, no row credited / caveat beside `off`, none feature-wide).
- **Overlay:** `lane-page.ts` splits the set reading into `problems` + `warnings`; `rowVerdict(page, id, check)` — the one composition the controller and the property test use (set reading wins on rows it read, else the row alone; problem xor warning); `draft.ts` `createRowValidator` → `RowCheck {reason, blocking}` (joined-only ids still left to the set reading), `rowWarning` → `blankRowWarning`; `controller.ts` `rowWarning(lane, id, text) → {reason, kind: 'applied' | 'blank'}`, `invalidCount` blocking only, `pageMessageFocus` removed (nothing blocks on it any more); `lane.tsx` warning icon + `data-tone="warn"` in the note slot, page-off message in warn tone (no focus target), counts caveats via `rowsPhrase` (position, else the applied text as code); `counts.ts` `pageOffRows`, `matchAllRows`.
- **Parity fixtures (18 dispositions changed, PHP outputs untouched, `parity:wp-rocket` byte-for-byte):** new disposition kind `engine: {warning}` (parser: a `problem` must be blocking, a `warning` non-blocking); compared fields = WP Rocket's outputs + `warnings`. `match-all` ×12 (`match-delay-option-letter`, `-empty-row`; `match-defer-empty-alternative`, `-escaped-dot-star`, `-option-letter`, `-pcre-option-a-{d,s,w,p,t}`, `-pcre-option-r`; `match-alr-empty-with-other`) and `joined-page-off` ×6 (`match-defer-dummy-string`, `-dot-star`, `-duplicate-names`, `-duplicate-names-angle`, `-inline-page-off`, `-inline-page-off-jquery`): candidates/excluded/featureOff/inlineWrapped now asserted equal to WP Rocket. `match-defer-inline-page-off-jquery` gains `inlineUndetermined` (the jQuery match spans rows: a live row could change it — as the other spanning rows).
- **Changed tests (owner decision changed the behavior):** every test pinning these rows as blocking now pins them as applied warnings (`validate`, `defer-js`, `delay-js-match`, `lazy-render`, `lane-page`, controller K.5b block, lane K.5b focus, `copy` K.5b words, notes e2e page-off leg); tests that only needed _a_ blocking row swap `(?a)` for a backtracking-risk row (`controller` ×2, `app`, session `controller`, `exclusion-session` problem id, notes e2e blocked-apply leg); `(a|ab)*` guard test uses each feature's non-nullable risk shape (the old row is match-all, now a warning); count shapes gain `pageOffRows`/`matchAllRows`; `comparedFields` test uses a blocking id for `problem`.
- **New tests:** classification + helpers; `featureRowReading`; Defer page-off report, inline pass under page-off, match-all report; `readDelayRows`; report contract accept/reject (off, rows, match-all); `runDocumentPipeline` warn-only set; `rowVerdict`; validator warning + joined-only; controller warn-and-apply (page-off, unverified before any pause, match-all, blank vs server); lane warning note + counts caveats; `rowsPhrase`, caveat words; property test compares `{problem, warning}` per row (fragments `(?a)`, `|` added) — mutation-checked: ignoring the set reading's warnings fails it.
- **String deltas (W-1/W-2):** page-off lead "WP Rocket would stop deferring every script on this page because of this row:" → "WP Rocket defers no script on this page because of this row:" (/"these rows:"); "Edit or remove it to apply." → "Apply still runs and previews the page as WP Rocket serves it, with no script deferred. Edit or remove it (/them) to defer scripts again."; new row note "<reason>. Apply still runs and previews it as WP Rocket would."; new caveats "WP Rocket defers no script on this page because of row 2 (/rows 1 and 3 / the applied rows), so every deferred script is restored." and "Row 2 matches everything: WP Rocket excludes all delayed scripts with it, and so does this preview." (applied rows no longer in the lane: their text as code). "Fix N rows before applying" unchanged (blocking rows only).
- **§D (scratch spec on the docker site `/fdt/?fdt-delay=1`, Chromium headless, page-off message + Delay `(?a)` applied):** D-1 320/480/768/1280 × light/dark — no horizontal overflow (scrollers, panel, message, row notes, caveats); D-2 reduced motion — no motion added (colors/borders only); right after the apply's reload the existing panel/indicator/banner transitions run, 0 animations 1.5 s later; D-3 Go to row reachable and working, a warning never moves focus; D-4 axe over the overlay host 0 violations ×8; D-6 tokens only (`--status-warn`, `--status-warn-soft`).
- **Live check (owner-visible):** `(?'n'a)`, `fdt-delayed`, `(?'n'b)` → warning naming rows 1 and 3, no "Fix" notice, Apply goes through → "4 of 4 deferred and wrapped scripts restored" + "…because of rows 1 and 3, so every deferred script is restored.", message kept, page has 0 `script[defer]`/`[data-rocket-defer]` (served: 4). Delay `(?a)` → note warning, Apply → "5 of 5 delayed scripts restored" + "Row 1 matches everything…", 0 `rocketlazyloadscript` left. `(?-i)zzz` + `FDT-DELAYED-REFERENCE` → still "Fix 1 row before applying", focus on the row. Screenshots: `/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-sandyfigueroa-orca-workspaces-wpr-front-debugging-tool-p10-implementation/330f65c1-1ca4-4719-8855-d6c912324698/scratchpad/k5c-live/` (`live-{page-off-warning,page-off-applied,match-all-warning,match-all-applied,joined-changes-blocks}.png`, `k5c-{defer,delay}-{320,480,768,1280}-{light,dark}.png`; spec `zz-k5c-live.spec.ts` there).
- **Gates:** compile/lint/format green; `pnpm test` 293 files, 6 859 passed + 1 skipped (coverage 98.66/96.81/100/99.36); `parity:wp-rocket` byte-for-byte; `build`, `build:firefox`, `check:budgets` green. **e2e:** full `pnpm test:e2e` serial (`E2E_FIXTURE_PORT=8897`) — **206 passed, 0 failed** (205 + the new O1 leg; the known-conflicts legs pass on the new endpoint); notes suite 9/9.
- **Budgets (raw KiB, gzip; measured + 0.1 in `build(budgets)`):** background 180.0 → 181.6; overlay ×2 246.7 → 249.5 (83.4 → 84.1); Firefox background 176.0 → 177.6; Firefox engine worker 82.1 → 83.3 (28.3 → 28.7); whole build 1 419.2 → 1 423.6.

**K.5c audit fixes (2026-10-22).** Audit: no CRITICAL/MAJOR; PHP faithfulness confirmed on 1 024 newly applied warning cases, overlay ↔ background agreement holds, classification correct.

- **MINOR — screen readers:** the applied status banner (the polite live region) names each applied warning after "This page was rendered with your exclusions." — title "Applied, with warnings", warn tone; words = the lane label + its counts caveat (e.g. "Load JavaScript deferred: WP Rocket defers no script on this page because of rows 1 and 3, so every deferred script is restored."). `session-view.ts` `appliedWarnings(reports, set)` (rows by position in the applied set) → `view.warnings`; `copy.ts` `appliedWarningLine`, `COUNT_COPY.matchAll` shared with the lane caveat. Tests: session view, copy, banner model, notes e2e (`role="status"`).
- **MINOR — parser:** `pageState: 'off'` accepted on Defer JS reports only (test: Delay/Lazyload/ALR `off` refused).
- **Recorded in P10L (pre-existing, not fixed):** script markup inside attribute values; `applyPipeline` not guarding sets outside the stored form.

#### P10K.6 — PHP parity job in CI

Owner 2026-10-22: "If not much trouble, PHP CI is okay (as separated worker from the rest)". Depends:P10K.2. Parallel:every other P10K sub-phase (touches only `.github/workflows/`, `scripts/wp-rocket-parity.*`, the table header — `wp-rocket-parity-table.ts` header keys + the fixtures header —, docs; a K.4e harness change only re-runs it). Goal: CI re-derives the parity table with the real plugin and fails **visibly and separately** when the committed table is stale; the `gates` job is untouched and not slowed.

The trouble (verified 2026-10-22):

- **No plugin in CI.** `docker/wpr/wpr-rocket.zip` is git-ignored, built locally by `apvm build wp-rocket tag:v3.23.5.1` ([runbook](../local-site-runbook.md)). The plugin repo `wp-media/wp-rocket` is **public** (unauthenticated API 200, `gh api` `private:false`, `apvm info` "Private: no"), so a checkout needs no token — but the tag tree is not the zip: `vendor/` is git-ignored and the harness `require`s `vendor/autoload.php` (`scripts/wp-rocket-parity.php:119`); no `composer.lock` at the tag (404), so `composer install` resolves fresh dependency versions (not reproducible); its `post-install-cmd` runs mozart (a dev dependency) + `dump-autoload`. `inc/Dependencies/` is committed, but mozart re-composes it from the resolved dev packages: the Lazyload code the harness runs is `wp-media/rocket-lazyload-common` (`require-dev` `^3.0.22`, mozart → `inc/Dependencies/RocketLazyload/`; local zip 3.0.22, `Image.php` byte-identical to the tag's today), so a later build may vendor a newer one. **Corrected by the spike:** APVM runs `composer install --no-dev --no-scripts`, so mozart never runs and the committed `inc/Dependencies/` copy is what ships (receipt below).
- **Where the vendored versions are recorded.** The local zip ships a build-time root `wp-rocket/composer.lock` (`packages` + `packages-dev`, e.g. `wp-media/rocket-lazyload-common v3.0.22`); `vendor/composer/installed.json` is `dev:false` and lists only the 10 runtime packages (no `rocket-lazyload-common`) — so drift is read from the root `composer.lock`, not `installed.json`.
- **The zip is not byte-reproducible.** Entries carry build-time mtimes (1566 of 2228 stamped `2026-10-08 18:34`, the local build) and the root `composer.lock` is generated per build, so a CI-built zip never matches `pluginZipSha256`.
- **The header pins the exact inputs.** `pluginZipSha256` (zip bytes) and `php`/`pcre2`/`libxml` (8.1.34 / 10.49 / 2.9.13, local Homebrew) are compared by check mode (`describeStaleness` over `HEADER_KEYS`, `scripts/wp-rocket-parity.ts:327`, header built at `:464`), so a CI-built tree or another PHP build reports "stale" even when every row matches.
- **`php -n`.** The harness runs without php.ini; a runner PHP that ships `dom` (or `mbstring`/`iconv`) as shared modules has none of them loaded. Homebrew compiles them in (`php -n -m` lists them); the runner is unverified.

Checklist:

Owner decisions 2026-10-22 (final, via the supervisor): APVM CLI `v3.3.0` pinned + `checksums.txt`; setup-php pinned; two checks in the job — the reference tag must reproduce (fails; drift reported apart; `--ci` skips `pluginZipSha256`) and `tag:latest-stable` is compared **warn-only** (job summary + `::warning::`, "same as the reference" → skip); weekly cron + dispatch + the path filter; separate workflow, informational.

- [x] **Spike** — run locally (no push): the APVM 3.3.0 `apvm-linux-x64-gnu` binary in an amd64 `ubuntu:24.04` container (ondrej PHP 8.1 = what setup-php installs there), `ubuntu:26.04` + sury PHP 8.1 (setup-php's 26.04 source), the 3.3.0 darwin binary on the host, and the runner image manifest (`Ubuntu2404-Readme.md`, image 20261012). Findings in the receipt.
- [x] **Plugin source** — as decided (APVM `v3.3.0` + checksum, `GH_TOKEN` = job token, `APVM_NO_UPDATE_CHECK=1`, zip → `docker/wpr/wpr-rocket.zip`); the tag is read from the table (`v` + `header.plugin`), so a re-pin needs no workflow edit. setup-php `2.40.0` pinned by commit (`eb7c497…`), PHP 8.1 + composer, for the **build only**.
- [x] **Plugin identity + drift** — `pluginPackages` = the composer packages whose `vendor/` files the harness loaded (reported by the harness, not a hand list) with their root `composer.lock` versions; `--ci` reports **"table stale"** (rows, or any header key but the zip bytes / PHP build / packages, byte-for-byte after aligning those) and **"reference drift: rebuild locally and review"** (packages) as two errors, both when both occur; local check mode still compares everything incl. `pluginZipSha256`. Deviation: `wp-media/rocket-lazyload-common` is **not** in the list (spike (a)).
- [x] **Header environment** — the harness PHP is **not** setup-php's (spike (c)): it runs in a digest-pinned `php:8.4-cli-bookworm` image (PHP 8.4.27, bundled PCRE2 10.44, libxml 2.9.14, dom + mbstring compiled in — the K.2 cross-check environment), offline (`--network none`), workspace + temp dir mounted read-only. `--ci` reports `php`/`pcre2`/`libxml` differences as a notice. The script loads `dom`/`mbstring` with `-d extension=…` when `php -n` lacks them (auto-probed, unit-tested); mbstring is now required (WordPress' `utf8.php` branches on it).
- [x] **Latest stable** — `--compare --zip <zip>`: never fails (any failure → warning); same version as the table → notice + skip (checked before the harness runs); else `WP Rocket <v> differs from the table (WP Rocket <ref>) on N rows: <ids>` (≤ 20 ids in the title, all rows in the details) or "matches … on all N rows", plus package changes.
- [x] **Workflow** `.github/workflows/wp-rocket-parity.yml` — `pull_request` + `push` (`main`/`develop`/`feat/**`, option A below) path-filtered (one YAML anchor: `wp-rocket-parity*`, `pcre/problems.ts`, `stored-row-drops.ts` — both loaded by the table module —, `scripts/wp-rocket-parity.*`, the workflow), `schedule` Mondays 05:17 UTC, `workflow_dispatch`; `ubuntu-24.04` (pinned, not `-latest`); `contents: read`; concurrency per ref; `timeout-minutes: 15`. `actionlint` 1.7.12 (latest) clean.
- [x] **Informational** (not a required check; owner can change in branch protection).
- [x] Docs: AGENTS.md Commands row + CI paragraph; runbook re-pin paragraph; script/harness TSDoc ("never run in CI" dropped).
- [x] **Option A — `feat/**` push trigger (owner 2026-10-22).** GitHub runs `schedule` and `workflow_dispatch` only from the default branch's copy of a workflow (`develop`), so on `feat/p10` the file could be neither dispatched nor scheduled. `push` now also matches `feat/**` (same `*parity-paths` anchor; other triggers kept). Concurrency stays per ref with `cancel-in-progress`: a newer push supersedes the older run of the same branch; a PR run has its own ref (`refs/pull/N/merge`) and never cancels it. `actionlint` 1.7.12 clean.
- [x] **Proved in CI** — first push-triggered run on `feat/p10` green: run 39862491481 (table reproduced with a fresh APVM build of 3.23.5.1; latest-stable comparison skipped, latest stable = 3.23.5.1).

**Receipt (2026-10-22, before the first CI run).**

- **Spike (a) — what the harness loads** (`get_included_files` trace over a full run): 7 WordPress files (the `WP_FILES` list), 32 plugin files under `inc/` (incl. `inc/Dependencies/RocketLazyload/{Assets,Iframe,Image}.php`, `inc/Logger/Logger.php`), the composer autoloader, and one vendored package: `vendor/wp-media/apply-filters-typed/functions.php` (autoload `files`). APVM builds with `composer install --no-dev --no-scripts` — **mozart never runs**, so `inc/Dependencies/RocketLazyload/` is the tag's committed copy (present at `v3.23.5.1`), fixed by the tag; the lock's `rocket-lazyload-common` (`packages-dev`, v3.0.22) is a resolution record only. Hence `pluginPackages` = `{"wp-media/apply-filters-typed": "v1.2"}` (`require` `^1.0`, resolved fresh per build — the real drift risk).
- **Spike (b) — rows reproduce from a fresh build.** APVM 3.3.0 builds of `tag:v3.23.5.1` (darwin native; linux-x64 in an amd64 container) differ in zip bytes from the local zip (3 different sha256s) but reproduce every row and `pluginPackages`; the zip carries the root `composer.lock`. `tag:latest-stable` = `v3.23.5.1` @ `8e89a59` today (instant APVM cache hit in the same job), so the compare step currently skips; `tag:previous-stable` = `v3.23.5` matches the table on all 306 rows. Older tags may not build at all (`v3.22.1`: composer conflicts — a dev-master `roave/security-advisories` now rejects PHP 8.1, `league/flysystem` 1.x blocked by advisories): an upstream risk, informational job.
- **Spike (c) — runner.** `ubuntu-24.04` ships `rsync`, `zip`, `unzip`, `git`, `jq`, Docker, composer 2.10.3 and PHP 8.3.6; setup-php adds PHP 8.1 + composer. Under `php -n` the distro PHP 8.1.34 loads neither `dom` nor `mbstring` (both shared; `-d extension=` loads them). **The distro PHPs cannot reproduce the table:** ubuntu-24.04 (ondrej, system PCRE2 **10.42**) — 8 rows fail at save (`match-*-option-letter`, `match-defer-pcre-option-a-{d,s,w,p,t}`, `-r`: the 10.43+ option letters are invalid there); ubuntu-26.04 (sury, PCRE2 10.46, libxml **2.15.2**) — `match-alr-single-quoted-value` differs (`excluded`/`hashed`/`rekeyed`). The pinned `php:8.4-cli-bookworm` image reproduces all 306 rows (arm64 and amd64), as does Homebrew PHP 8.1.34 (PCRE2 10.49, libxml 2.9.13).
- **Spike (d) — time + caches** (amd64 emulated, so CI should be faster): APVM build cold 27–30 s, warm composer cache 16 s, APVM cache hit 4 s; harness ~3–8 s; WordPress zip ~30 MB once. Kept: composer's download cache (`actions/cache`, run-id key + prefix restore) and `.cache/wp-rocket-parity/` (key `header.wp` + `wpZipSha1`). **Not** kept across runs: the APVM artifact cache (a hit skips composer and would hide drift; it costs ~25 s). Expected job time ≈ 2–3 min.
- **Files:** `.github/workflows/wp-rocket-parity.yml` (new) · `scripts/wp-rocket-parity.ts` (`parseArgs` `--ci`/`--compare`/`--zip`, PHP probe + `-d extension`, `readPackageVersions`, `headerDifferences`/`rowDifferences`/`packageDifferences`, `ciFindings`, `compareFindings`/`sameVersionFindings`, `annotation` + `summaryMarkdown` → `::error/warning/notice` + `$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY` when `GITHUB_ACTIONS=true`) + `.test.ts` · `scripts/wp-rocket-parity.php` (requires mbstring; `env.vendorPackages`) · `lib/exclusion-builder/wp-rocket-parity-table.ts` (+ test: `pluginPackages` header key, non-empty `{name: version}`) · the table (header gains `pluginPackages`; rows unchanged; local check reproduces byte-for-byte) · AGENTS.md · runbook.
- **Local proof (no push):** the whole job dry-run on the host (`run:` steps in order with the `GITHUB_OUTPUT/ENV/PATH/STEP_SUMMARY` protocol; `uses:` steps played by the host; darwin APVM binary in place of the linux one): green — reference reproduces (notice: PHP 8.4.27 / PCRE2 10.44 / libxml 2.9.14 vs the header), latest stable skipped as the reference. A stale row fails the check step as "table stale" with the row diff while the latest-stable warning step still runs; a failed latest build → warning step. Fabricated zips: package bump → "reference drift" only; package bump + a Defer behavior change → both errors; `--compare` on a fake `3.99.0` → `differs … on 4 rows: match-defer-case-insensitive, …` warning, exit 0.
- **To prove in CI:** push `feat/p10` — the option-A `feat/**` trigger runs the job when the push touches a parity path (this one does: the workflow itself). Dispatch/cron work only once the file is on `develop`. Expect a green "WP Rocket parity" job (~2–3 min) whose summary shows "another PHP build than the table header" (php 8.1.34 → 8.4.27, pcre2 10.49 → 10.44, libxml 2.9.13 → 2.9.14), "the table reproduces with a fresh build of WP Rocket 3.23.5.1", and "WP Rocket 3.23.5.1 is the table's reference version; comparison skipped". DoD negatives (scratch `feat/**` branch, push it): edit one row's output → only this workflow fails, "table stale" + the row diff; edit `pluginPackages` (e.g. `v1.1`) → "reference drift: rebuild locally and review". `gates` (ci.yml) is untouched.
- **Unverified until the CI run:** the hosted runner itself (all evidence is from containers + the runner image manifest) and setup-php's 8.1 there (YAML anchors are documented as supported: docs.github.com "Reusing workflow configurations"; actionlint accepts them).

DoD: a push-triggered run on this branch is green; a deliberately stale table (scratch branch) fails only this workflow as "table stale" with the row diff, and an edited `pluginPackages` version fails it as "reference drift", while `gates` stays green; script flag tests + gates green. Effort M · difficulty medium (unchanged: the APVM step + drift report replace the owner's zip upload).

**Open owner decisions (recommended defaults in brackets):**

- **O1 — decided 2026-10-22 (owner, changed from the default):** a stored row that matches everything (`match-all`: `(?a)` and the other 10.43+ option letters, `.*` in Delay, an empty Defer alternative, an empty ALR row next to others) — "show the warning but let Apply run, previewing the row as 'matches everything'" → [P10K.5c](#p10k5c--warn-dont-block-rows-wp-rocket-applies-as-is-o1o2-decided).
- **O2 — decided 2026-10-22 (owner: "take best decision here. And show respective warnings, etc."):** decided to match O1 — Defer `joined-page-off` keeps the page message naming the rows (K.5b) but never blocks Apply; the preview is the page as WP Rocket serves it (no script deferred), the message and the counts saying why → [P10K.5c](#p10k5c--warn-dont-block-rows-wp-rocket-applies-as-is-o1o2-decided). Rows the engine cannot apply faithfully (`joined-changes-matching`, `pcre-only`, `pcre-invalid`, `backtracking-risk`, wire caps) keep blocking.
- **O3** Safe mode is unknowable from the page — [conditional note on the three safe-mode rows, set unchanged].
- **O4** Non-ASCII regex rows — [byte-string translation if K.2 proves it, else reject the listed constructs].
- **O5** Polynomial backtracking rows (JS seconds, PCRE instant) — [keep accepting; rely on the P10B wall-time budget and name the overrun row].
- **O6** Picked rows read less naturally (`a\x2Bb\.js`) — [accept; only `+ ? * < %` are encoded].
- **PHP in CI — decided 2026-10-22 (owner): yes, as a separate job** → [P10K.6](#p10k6--php-parity-job-in-ci). Until it lands, the harness stays a dev-time check (run when the table or the WP Rocket pin changes, and before every release).

### P10L — Deferred nits and flaky suite

Depends:P10K (field-text/lane edits land on K's shape). Parallel:P10M; the e2e flake item may run any time (disjoint files). Goal: close the recorded nits and make `site-side-by-side.spec.ts` deterministic.

**Split (2026-10-22, worker proposal — owner can merge back):** one item per commit; the list outgrew one review. **P10L.1** (done, receipt below) = the plan's nits, the flake, the engine guards and the rename-back bound. **P10L.2** (done, receipt below) = the rest, including the supervisor's P10K follow-ups not yet listed here.

#### P10L.1 — nits, flake, engine guards (done)

- [x] Receipt nit: the round-2 nit line's "no-break/Unicode spaces at the ends" is stale (round 3 flags any non-ASCII space anywhere) — mark it superseded inline, as the other round-2 lines are.
- [x] `hasInvisibleKeptCharacters` (`lib/exclusion-builder/field-text.ts`, `INVISIBLE_KEPT`) misses LRM/RLM (U+200E/U+200F): add them test-first; while there, verify the remaining invisible format characters a pasted row can carry (ALM U+061C, bidi embeddings/isolates U+202A–U+202E, U+2066–U+2069) and include or explicitly exclude each with the reason.
- [x] Paste outcome message cleared early (reproduce first, then fix; unit test the sequence).
- [x] Dead `documentUrl` in the transform path — removed; the backend/host `documentUrl` kept.
- [x] The Defer JS inline replay billed to Delay JS in an over-budget message — its own named step.
- [x] `applyPipeline` does not guard sets outside WP Rocket's stored form — guarded in the engine.
- [x] Flaky e2e `tests/e2e/suites/site-side-by-side.spec.ts` — root-caused (a product race), fixed, repeat-run.
- [x] (Supervisor follow-up, K.4b/K.4c integration) the rename-back search can exceed the 2 s budget on hostile pages — bounded.

**Receipt (2026-10-22, worktree `agent-adb4799e79f59c780`, base `a984389`):**

- **Invisible characters** (`fb0aaf2`): `INVISIBLE_KEPT` = non-ASCII whitespace | U+0085 | `\p{Default_Ignorable_Code_Point}` — LRM/RLM, ALM, U+202A–U+202E, U+2066–U+2069 included, plus every other default-ignorable (CGJ, U+2061–U+2064, U+180E, variation selectors, tag characters, Hangul fillers, the earlier zero-width set). Excluded: visible format characters (Arabic number signs U+0600–U+0605: `Cf` but not default-ignorable — they render). Proof the save keeps them all: new parity row `save-delay-bidi-format` (PHP 8.1.34/PCRE2 10.49; reproduced on PHP 8.4.27/PCRE2 10.44 — header-only diff). Note copy names "text-direction" characters.
- **Paste outcome** (`fc7c14f`): **does not reproduce** since the stored-form commits (K.3/K.5a) — every filled row but the caret row is committed at the paste and created rows are trimmed lines, so neither the frame-later focus move nor leaving the caret row changes the lane. New lane test drives the exact sequence (499 rows, target rendered only after a scroll, queued frame, leave the pasted row) and pins that only the next lane edit clears the message. No product change.
- **`documentUrl`** (`cacb0bf`): re-grepped; removed from `FeatureTransform`/`exclusionPipelineSteps`/`applyPipeline`, `lazyRenderTransform`'s `_documentUrl`, `DocumentTransformRequest`, `InterceptedDocumentBody` (the backend's `InterceptedDocument` declares its own) and the worker parser. Changed tests: pipeline/runner raw-`src` cases without a URL (same assertions); the worker parser now ignores an extra `documentUrl` (was: accepted; the bad-URL rejection row went with the field).
- **Replay step** (`b75166b`): the inline-pass replay is its own first step keyed `defer_all_js` (report `null`; Defer JS still reports the pass); `EXCLUSION_PIPELINE_STEP_IDS` declares the order, `featureReports` keeps one report per feature. Changed tests: step order/announcements expect the replay first; fake-clock overruns at the first step now name `defer_all_js` with no row (was `delay_js` + its row — the bug); the parity helper picks the feature's own step (`findLast`).
- **Stored-form guard** (`35befef`): `pipelineSetProblems` (rows outside the stored form via `notStoredRows`, else blocking problems) gates `applyPipeline` (throws) and `runDocumentPipeline` (`engine-failed`). Tests: two identical `(?'n'a)` Defer rows + a padded Delay row refused, naming each; the deduped row judged alone (`pcre-only`).
- **Flake root cause** (`9e15f86`): reproduced 1/160 then 2/240 (`--repeat-each`, traces). Not the test: **WP Rocket's Delay JS runtime on the host page** (`RocketLazyLoadScripts`, v2.0.5 on the docker site) listens at the window for a page's first interactions, stops them, loads its delayed scripts and replays each saved event on its original target — `composedPath()[0]` inside our shadow root. The side-by-side, strip and Known Conflicts UIs only isolated key events, so the first click on any of their controls ran twice: CDP showed both cached-frame reloads initiated by the refresh click handler, the second dispatched from the page's runtime ~40 ms later (8/8 first clicks doubled); the mobile switch flipping back explains the "engaged device" failure. Fix: the overlay's pair (stop every Solid-delegated input event at the shadow root + delegate Solid there) moved to `components/injected-ui/event-isolation.ts` (`isolateUiEvents`, one call does both, idempotent) and every injected UI uses it — also stops clicks on our UI from starting the page's delayed scripts. Test synchronization: the refresh legs wait for the reloaded document to finish loading on its real URL before reading the end-of-page footprint; the row pins that the host page saw none of the overlay clicks. Repeat runs after: **200/200** (25 × each of the suite's 8 tests, incl. both named ones). Unit: `event-isolation.test.tsx` (incl. the unisolated leak as control).
- **Rename-back bound** (`f688bf7`): plain choice first, line-feed placements only when it does not fit (submasks enumerated directly — same fitting set); `MAX_RENAME_CHOICES` 6 → 4 (81 checks; WP Rocket's own rewrite makes two; past it → undetermined, never wrong). Chromium, 4× CPU throttle, 2 000 hostile tags (before → after): 2 renames 73 → 62 ms, 4 renames 655 → 547 ms, 5 renames 2.1 s → 20 ms, 6 renames 7.0 s → 20 ms. New parity row `match-delay-rebuild-four-renames` (multi-line tag WP Rocket renames four times) pins the answer at the bound on both PHP/PCRE2 versions.
- **Budgets** (`0b78958`, measured + 0.1 KiB): isolated 131.8 raw / 45.2 gz (rows 132.0 / 45.4); overlay both builds 250.4 raw (250.6); Firefox engine worker 88.0 / 30.2 (88.1 / 30.4 — the stored-form guard brings the save model into the worker run); whole build 1425.1 (1425.3).
- **Gates:** compile, lint, format:check green; `pnpm test` 294 files, 6 905 passed (+1 skipped), coverage 98.67/96.82/100/99.36; `parity:wp-rocket` byte-for-byte; `build` + `build:firefox` + `check:budgets` green; full `pnpm test:e2e` (serial, headless Chromium, docker site) **206/206** (5.2 min).

#### P10L.2 — remaining items (done)

- [x] Anchor picked URL rows (supervisor follow-up): a picked path row also matches longer paths (`a\.js` matches `/js/a.js`) — anchor it (leading `/` or host, trailing `(?=[?#"'\s>])`) where WP Rocket's runtime keeps it exact; PHP-pin through the parity table; show the live match count when a row is picked if the UI bar asks for it.
- [x] Memo heap snapshot (supervisor follow-up): heap-snapshot the bounded memos (`utils/bounded-memo.ts` users) at their bounds in Chromium; record the retained sizes against the estimates (K.4b/c receipt: ≤ 672 KiB per realm).
- [x] 499-line paste at 4× throttle 57–131 ms (K.5b receipt): split the paste's commit + render into tasks under 50 ms each; measure before/after in Chromium at 4×.
- [x] Delay JS restores never reach script copies inside comments or attribute values (WP Rocket's Delay regex rewrites them; the engine restores parsed elements). Predates K.4e; low priority — fix or document the residual (parity fixture + reason).
- [x] Script markup inside an attribute value (`data-s="<script …>"`): WP Rocket's regex restores it, the engine's tokenizer does not (K.5c audit). Decide: mirror the regex's reach, or document the residual (parity fixture + reason).
- [x] Constructs PHP always drops that the parser leaves `unknown` (`(**)`, `(?(`, `(*UTF)` — K.4 verifier MINOR): drop only where proven on both PCRE2 versions (10.49 local, 10.44 docker).
- [x] (P10L.1 audit MINOR 1) the preempting worker blamed `EXCLUSION_PIPELINE_IDS[0]` for a run killed before any step.
- [x] (P10L.1 audit MINOR 2) event isolation opt-in per injected UI.
- [x] (P10L.1 audit NITs) U+FFF9–U+FFFB; note wording; the e2e page-click assertion's why; one Firefox check of the three injected UIs (not run — see receipt).

**Receipt (2026-10-22, worktree `agent-ad8597894c6bbdaab`, base `a87a62a`; one item per commit):**

- **Worker attribution** (`219e02d`): a run killed before the worker announced a step is billed to the first step's feature (`defer_all_js`, the replay — as the in-process runner bills setup time) with `row: null` (no row ran: it was starting or validating). Changed test: `transform-worker.test.ts` "bills the first step, naming no row…" expected `EXCLUSION_PIPELINE_IDS[0]` (Delay JS — the misattribution); it now pins `defer_all_js`, `row: null`.
- **Event isolation built in** (`1357162`): `mountShadowUi(container, shadow, view)` isolates the shadow root before anything renders; the four injected UIs pass their root instead of calling `isolateUiEvents`. Changed tests: the mount tests mount into a real shadow root (the new required argument) and pin isolate-before-render.
- **Invisible characters** (`c7d0032`): U+FFF9–U+FFFB (format, not default-ignorable, render as nothing) flagged; doc names them and the excluded Arabic number signs; note text adds emoji-variation and tag characters. New parity row `save-delay-annotation-anchors` (kept; header-only diff on 8.4.27/10.44). E2E (`5e4742e`): comment on why the bubbling-phase window counter proves isolation (WP Rocket's runtime listens there, `passive: false`, no capture — read in its `lazyload-scripts.min.js`).
- **Always-dropped constructs** (`1b1e8ed`): `(**` anywhere, an upper-case `(*NAME` that is no backtracking verb past the pattern's start, `(?(` at the end, `(?()` → `dropped`. Kept `unknown` by evidence: a leading `(*NAME` (the option set grows: `(*CASELESS_RESTRICT)` dropped on 10.44, kept on 10.49), verbs, lower-case alpha assertions, other conditions. Proof: parity row `save-defer-verbs-conditions` (Delay turns `*` into `(.*)`, so Defer carries it) and a differential fuzz of 48 974 rows on both PCRE2 versions — 0 mismatches; 6 628 rows moved from `unknown` to `dropped`.
- **Anchored URL rows** (`f79e083`): bounded before by the value's start (`^`, a quote, whitespace, an unquoted `\ssrc=` — never any `=`, a query's `?a=a.js` is not the URL) and, for a same-origin root path, also by any authority `//[^/\s"'>]{1,255}` (the CDN copy keeps matching) or the minify cache directory `cache\/min\/\d{1,20}`; another origin's row starts at `//host`; after by `(?=[?#"'\s>]|$)`. No `+`/`*` written. Proof (PHP-run): five new parity pairs `picked-{delay,defer}-anchored-{path,host,relative}` exclude exactly the same URL among `/js/a.js`, `/ba.js`, `/a.jsx`, `/a.js.php`, `sub.ex.com`, `ex.com.evil`, a query holding `a.js` — stored as typed; the four picked-spelling fixtures now carry the anchored rows (same excluded scripts). K.4b picker fuzz against the real plugin (`k4b-fz` tools + a realistic-overlap generator): 6 798 picked rows — rows also excluding a script of another path **226 → 7** (the remaining 103 broad rows name the same path on another host, by design); residuals: a raw quote inside a raw URL, another attribute equal to a bare relative path; random corpus 3 191 rows 19 → 1 (an inline first-line row); saves changed 0; misses +1 (a raw tab right after a path's `/`, which WP Rocket's own rename drops). Live match count on pick: not asked by the UI bar (rows show their match count after Apply). Changed tests: six exact-row expectations now expect the bounded rows (row format changed by design).
- **Delay copies in comments** (`6e21ff4`) and **in attribute values** (`04d85bc`): named residuals, not mirrored. A comment never runs; raw `<script` in an attribute value is never written by WordPress's escaping and matters only when page code injects it with a script-running API — or when WP Rocket's rewrite broke the value's quoting (a double-quoted value gains `type="…"`), which the preview keeps. Mirroring the regex means restoring raw-text regex regions that can span past the comment or attribute. Proof: parity row `match-delay-copy-in-comment` (served page shows the commented copies delayed); attribute copies cannot be a table row (the table keys subjects with the tokenizer), so the engine test carries the harness's served markup (PHP 8.1 and 8.4 identical; with the rows WP Rocket serves both values original).
- **Memos** (`9f729b4`): Chromium heap after GC, memo build minus a control build whose memos keep nothing, at the bounds. Before: rows split out of a field are views on its text, so analyses/sets pinned the whole field (300 edits of a 500-row Delay lane **58 MiB**); compiled regexes cost 3–80 B per source character once run (5 assumed): regex memo ≈ 3 MiB, Defer sets ≈ 3.2 MiB; fixed costs 3–4× the estimates; two-byte strings counted half (row forms 1.45 MiB vs "about a megabyte"). Fix: memos keep detached copies (`detachedString`: keys, the analysed row, a Defer set's rows, a row form's row) and weigh retained bytes (`stringBytes`, measured fixed costs, 80 B per compiled source character, also in a Defer set's weight); row-form bound now 1 MiB in bytes; other bounds unchanged. After (memo-only, worst shapes): risk ≤ 91 KiB (96), analysis + risk ≤ 120 KiB (352), regex + analysis + risk ≤ 133 KiB (544), Defer + analysis + regex ≤ 125 KiB (672), row forms ≤ 922 KiB (1 024), the 300-edit lane 0.94 MiB.
- **Paste in slices** (`b65a29c`): a paste committing > 64 rows commits a slice per task (`startCommit` = `commitRows` a slice at a time, same order and result), checking each kept row as it goes, then lands in one step (same draft, notes, undo, announcement, caret). `pasteRows` returns `pending` + `done`; editing, Apply and Reset wait (inputs read-only for the ≈ 0.2 s hold); a row commit meanwhile gets the paste re-landed on the new rows; closing settles `ignored`; the lane says nothing after unmount. Chromium, 4× throttle, 499 + 1 lines, 3 runs (Delay / Defer), paste task: plain paths 117–135 / 117–123 → **28–36 / 26–29 ms**; heavy rows 89–102 / 83–102 → 29–34 / 21–25; named groups 61–75 / 61–71 → 16–19 / 15–19; no later task > 50 ms (long-task observer; an 80 ms control task detected). The base `a87a62a` measured the same as before (the memo fix did not slow the paste).
- **Budgets** (`c059713`, measured + 0.1 KiB): background 182.6; Firefox background page 178.7; overlay both builds 253.1 raw / 85.5 gz; Firefox engine worker 89.0 / 30.7; whole build 1428.7.
- **Firefox check:** not run — no driver here loads an unpacked extension into stock Firefox (Playwright's BiDi Firefox has no add-on install; a manual `web-ext run` session needs GUI driving). Covered meanwhile by unit tests (happy-dom) and `build:firefox`.
- **Follow-ups:** the overlay's row validator cache (`createRowValidator`, 4 096 entries, not a bounded-memo user) keeps whole stored keys unbounded in bytes (long two-byte rows could pin several MiB) — bound it by bytes like the memos (done: audit fix round, `2bbeecc`). **Open:** none from this sub-phase — the Defer set-reading and Apply long tasks first recorded as follow-ups were a regression of `9f729b4`, fixed in audit round 3 (`c988b44`). Kept as a design note: should a lane's set reading ever exceed 50 ms again (wire-cap sets are never cached), slice it or move it off the main thread.
- **Gates:** compile, lint, format:check green; `pnpm test` 294 files, 6 970 passed (+1 skipped), coverage 98.67/96.81/100/99.36; `parity:wp-rocket` byte-for-byte (header-only diff on PHP 8.4.27/PCRE2 10.44); `build` + `build:firefox` + `check:budgets` green; full `pnpm test:e2e` (serial) **206/206** (5.1 min).

**Audit fix round (2026-10-22, worktree `agent-afc7b47331165f287`, from `df06e25` + `feat/p10`; one finding per commit):**

- **CRITICAL — sliced paste re-landed on committed rows** (`5a61efc`): a row commit during the ≈ 0.2 s hold (Tab, a click elsewhere, Apply's blur) changed the draft and the paste re-landed with the old selection (`'  ab'` caret 3 → `abs0.js` … `last`, not `as0.js` … `lastb`; a commit dropping the row — `0`, empty, duplicate, invalid — lost every pasted line as `ignored`). Fix: while `pasting()`, `commitRow`/`commitLane`/`apply`/`reset` queue as typed `HeldAction`s and run in order after the landing; the re-land branch is gone (nothing else changes the draft during a hold); close drops paste + queue (as the one-step path loses its pending autosave). Footer keeps Apply/Reset enabled during a paste-only hold. Rejected: re-landing on the committed draft with a remapped selection (a dropped row has no place to remap to). Tests: a `pasteSliceRows` dep runs each sequence on a one-step and a sliced controller and compares rows, notes, focus, applied sets — mid-caret, range, each drop reason, lane commit, Apply, Reset; close during the hold; app test blur + Apply during a 200-line paste. Changed: removed "pastes on the rows as they are when a row commit changed them meanwhile" (pinned the defect); replaced "holds apply and reset until the paste lands" (they now run after the landing, not dropped).
- **MINOR — typing / second paste dropped silently** (`254a20a`): not queued (their caret points into rows the landing rewrites). An editing key or paste on a locked row while pasting shows "Still adding the pasted rows: type or paste again once they are in." on the lane's paste-outcome line (cleared on landing), announced once per hold via the overlay live region; the lane is `aria-busy` meanwhile. W-2 delta: that string (new).
- **MINOR — row validator cache bounded in bytes** (`2bbeecc`): `createBoundedMemo`, 4 096 entries / 1 MiB, weight = 192 B fixed + `stringBytes(key)` + reason; key and reason detached. Chromium (heap after GC, validator minus a keep-nothing control, 4 096 rows at the cap, min of 3): two-byte 16 227 → **657 KiB** (243 kept), two-byte invalid Delay rows with reasons 17 428 → **1 001 KiB** (225), one-byte 8 008 → 637 KiB (466), short rows 165 → 250 KiB (4 096; the old cache had just restarted) — each within its weight (≈ 60 B fixed measured on short rows). Changed test: "restarts past capacity" → `{ entries: 2 }`, oldest-first eviction.
- **NITs (residuals, no code):** a picked row also matches the same path in another case (WP Rocket matches with `#i`; unavoidable); a bare relative row can match another tag's attribute — the "another attribute equal to a bare relative path" residual of the anchored-rows bullet above.
- **Budgets** (`e46e6bf`, measured + 0.1 KiB): overlay both builds 254.0 raw / 85.8 gz; whole build 1429.7.
- **Perf** (Chromium, 4× throttle, 500 lines into a new row, 3 runs; machine shared with another worker, load avg 6–18): paste task Delay plain 27–35 / heavy 29–34 / named groups 12–15 ms, Defer 26–28 / 34–40 / 12–13 ms, landing 61–132 ms after; paste + immediate blur the same. No long task, except the Defer regex-heavy shape (`(?:jquery|gtag|pN)[a-z0-9_-]{1,40}\.(?:min\.)?js`): one 50–56 ms task ≈ 150 ms after landing (the lane's set reading; profile dominated by regex `search`) — present on `df06e25` too, but **not pre-existing in P10L.2**: a regression of `9f729b4` (absent on `04d85bc`), fixed in round 3.
- **Live** (docker site `:8493`, built `chrome-mv3`, 4×): 132 lines pasted at caret 3 of `'  ab'`, then Tab at once / blur + Apply at once (hold confirmed): rows `alive-0.js` … `live-131.jsb` (132), the Apply applied that set.
- **Gates:** compile, lint, format:check; `pnpm test` 294 files, 6 984 passed (+1 skipped), coverage 98.67/96.81/100/99.36; `parity:wp-rocket` byte-for-byte; `build` + `build:firefox` + `check:budgets`; `pnpm test:e2e` (serial, port 8897) **206/206** (5.1 min), no retries.

**Audit fix round 2 (2026-10-22, same worktree, + `feat/p10` at `4f45a1d` (P10M) merged in `bc16c83`):** the 2 000-seed sliced-vs-one-step fuzz found 0 divergences; findings fixed test-first.

- **MAJOR — Copy all during the hold** (`7ac417e`): it committed the lane through the held `commitLane`, so it copied the pre-paste rows typed (`"  ab "`). Held instead of queued (a later copy would leave the click's user activation): `aria-disabled` while the paste lands, a click copies nothing and says the wait note; after the landing it copies what a one-step paste would (`ak0.js` … `k299.jsb`, 300 rows). Other draft readers during the hold: undo/restore, add row, picks and the page list's add are `editable`-gated with `aria-disabled` controls; counts and the empty-draft guidance only display; nothing else copies or exports.
- **MINORs** (`ec998eb`): (1) a paste left meanwhile asks no focus (it stays where Tab or a click put it) and the held leave commits the caret's row, as a Tab after a one-step paste; (2) Stop held in order like Apply/Reset (Apply then Stop drops Stop, as one-step), the footer treats it like Apply; (3) held actions run in their own task after the landing, editing held until then; (4) a throw in a slice or at its start settles `failed` (logged with context, editor free, only held commits run, `done` never rejects; the lane shows and announces "The paste could not be added. Paste it again, or fewer lines at a time."), a throwing held action is logged and the rest run; (5) closing saves an edit still in the autosave's 800 ms pause; a long paste still committing at close lands at once (remaining slices in one go), its held commits run, then the draft is saved — chosen over saving the visible draft because that would drop the pasted lines the user saw accepted; held Apply/Reset/Stop are dropped (a closing overlay starts nothing).
- **NITs:** the wait note is a neutral `xo-row-note` (info icon), problems keep `xo-row-error`; the `aria-busy` comment says every lane waits; TSDoc on `pasteOnly`/`actionHeld`/`resetHeld`. W-2: held string → "Still adding the pasted rows: try again once they are in." (covers Copy all), failed string new.
- **Changed tests:** "lands nothing when the overlay closes between slices" and "settles a paste cut short by closing as ignored" → closing lands and saves (finding 5); `bothPaths` "commit-row" → "leave the focused row" with focus compared apart (the old step committed the pasted row on both paths, which no user does after a one-step paste); `runSlices` and the app test's timer loop await between timers (the held actions' task is scheduled once the landing settled). New: Apply→Stop, Stop alone, trailing-space caret row left, throwing held Stop, close mid-slices / before the held run, edit flushed on close, slice/start failure (+ lane line), Copy all sliced vs one-step, the note's style, validator lane keys / oversize checks / hits (`73d7ead`, ported from the audit probe).
- **Budgets** (`e2f904e`, re-measured on the merged build, measured + 0.1 KiB): overlay both builds 255.6 raw / 86.4 gz; whole build 1431.3.
- **Perf** (4×, 500 lines, paste then Apply at once, 3 runs; load avg 5–14): Delay plain no long task, Delay heavy 50–55 ms, Defer plain 77–91, Defer heavy 99–106 — Apply's cost, wrongly called its own (pre-existing) here: Apply alone measured 50–53 / 72–80 / 90–119 ms, a regression of `9f729b4` fixed in round 3.
- **Live** (`:8493`, built `chrome-mv3`): Copy all at 6× right after a 300-line paste at caret 3 of `'  ab '` — held (`aria-disabled`, clipboard unchanged), after landing copies `ak0.js` … `k299.jsb`; focus moved to "Remove … row 1" during a 400-line hold at 20× stays there after landing; close right after a 10- and a 400-line paste → reopened with every row (10 / 400 stored). Screenshots: `scratchpad/l2r2/live/` (copy-1, copy-2, tab-after-landing, close-10/400-reopened).
- **Gates:** compile, lint, format:check; `pnpm test` 295 files, 7 002 passed (+1 skipped), coverage 98.67/96.81/100/99.36; `parity:wp-rocket` byte-for-byte; `build` + `build:firefox` + `check:budgets`; `pnpm test:e2e` (serial, port 8897) **207/207** (5.1 min), no retries.

**Audit fix round 3 (2026-10-22, same worktree; base `8a409a7`):** the 2 500-seed fuzz found 0 divergences; one MAJOR (a regression introduced in P10L.2 itself) and one MINOR, fixed test-first.

- **MAJOR — Apply and the Defer set reading long tasks since `9f729b4`** (`c988b44`). Profiled (memo hit/miss/evict counters in the built overlay, 500-row lane, the Apply click; the same reads in vitest on `04d85bc` vs HEAD): the Defer row set weighed its rows compiled (80 B/char) — a 500-row lane weighed 4.7–8 MB against 128 KiB, so it was never kept and every read (the set reading, Apply's `readNow`) re-translated the lane (`04d85bc`: second read a hit, 0 ms); the risk memo weighed 1 KiB per entry (~90 kept of 512), so a lane's risk verdicts were recomputed per translation and commit (`04d85bc`: 1 000 hits); Apply's commit of every row re-analysed each row through the 256-entry analysis memo (thrashing in both builds). The auditor's hypothesis held. Fix: Defer sets kept uncompiled (`compileMatcher()` builds a fresh lookup; the transform compiles once per document), weights measured uncompiled (a 500-row lane retains 101–136 KiB, weighs ≈ 260 KiB), bound 512 KiB; risk memo weighs safe verdicts small (256 B + 1.5 × source bytes; risky 1 KiB + …), 1 024 entries / 384 KiB; a save-check memo apart from the analyses (192 B + key, 1 024 / 384 KiB); `readNow` reads a lane only when its reading is not of its current rows (one reading per rows version). Guards: a 500-row lane re-read is the kept set; the risk and save memos keep two full lanes within their bytes and stay bounded with long and risky rows; Apply reuses an unchanged lane's reading. Changed tests: "never keeps a set heavier than its character bound" uses 200 × 2 000-character rows (80 now fit the raised bound); matching tests call `compileMatcher()` (API moved off the memoized set).
- **Perf** (Chromium, 4×, 500-row lane, 3 runs; load avg 2–8; Delay plain / heavy, Defer plain / heavy): Apply click 2 s after the paste `04d85bc` 37–41 / 29–31 / 38–40 / 30–34, `8a409a7` 40–41 / 49–52 / 72–81 / 82–98, **now 4–5 / 4–5 / 5–6 / 5 ms**; paste then Apply at once: no long task (was 57–61 ms Defer heavy); paste task 24–25 / 28–29 / 24–25 / 32–36 ms (named groups 11–12), landing 43–121 ms after, then no long task — the Defer set reading's 50–64 ms task is gone.
- **Heap** (Chromium after GC, a scenario minus the same scenario at 8 rows, i.e. net of code warm-up): risk memo at its bound 180 (risky) – 321 KiB (2 KiB rows) ≤ 384; analysis + risk + save memos at their bounds 506–636 KiB ≤ their 1 024 KiB; a Defer lane edited 300 times (every memo touched) 665 / 850 KiB (plain / patterns) — still ~1 MiB, against the 58 MiB before `9f729b4`.
- **MINOR — short paste unguarded** (`a91b5ef`): any throw while splitting or landing a paste now settles `failed` (logged as `[WPR FDT]` with lane and size); `landPaste` commits before changing anything — a throw in the commit leaves the draft as it was, though not a throw later in the landing (fixed in round 4); the lane shows and announces the failure for short and long pastes alike. Tests on both paths.
- **NIT:** the auditor's earlier live specs expected the last pasted row in view after a held leave; by design since round 2 (focus, and the scroll, stay where the user went).
- **Budgets** (`0729d72`, measured + 0.1 KiB): background 182.9; Firefox background page 179.0; Firefox engine worker 89.3 / 30.8; overlay both builds 256.1 / 86.5; whole build 1432.0.

**Audit fix round 4 (2026-10-22, same worktree; `92b2e23` passed, + `feat/p10` at `0a3917f` (P10M.1) merged in `265fd75`, budget `3bfa6e0`):**

- **MINOR — non-atomic landing** (`e1c7f1a`): a throw late in the landing (`recordCommits`, inside `takeCommit` after `setDraft`) settled `failed` ("could not be added") while the draft held the paste, the autosave was armed and no notes were recorded; the long path likewise. The landing now computes the commit, both notes and the cleared background reason first (a throw writes nothing), then writes them in one batch; a throw from a reaction to the writes counts as landed when the draft holds the paste (logged; notes and reason rolled back otherwise); focus and the autosave follow the draft (a throw there is logged, the paste landed). `takeCommit` computes its notes before writing too. Tests: a throw at each step — row split, edit note, commit notes, autosave timer, a reaction to the landed draft — on the short and the long path; outcome, draft, notes, autosave and focus agree (6 of the 10 failed before).
- **Budgets** (`e93641d`, measured + 0.1 KiB): overlay both builds 256.5 / 86.7; whole build 1432.7.
