#### P10L.3 — r2 flag + landing autosave + time-sliced paste + no long task after landing (done)

Depends:P10L.2. Goal: the engine's one silent wrong answer in the inline-jQuery area (K.4e residual r2) becomes a flag (K.4e certainty rule); a paste the user saw land survives a close; no task of a long paste — its slices, its landing, the readings and edits after it — breaks the 50 ms rule (`docs/ui-ux-bar.md`).

- [x] r2: a Defer JS wrapper spanning scripts (`</script >` + Delay JS) is left as served and counted undetermined unless the pass runs; Delay JS reads its pieces both ways; parity rows from the real plugin.
- [x] `landPaste`: autosave armed before the focus request, each step failing alone (P10L.2 audit MINOR).
- [x] Long paste: slices sized by elapsed time (injected clock), at least one row each; at-once bound by rows and characters (coordinator item 3).
- [x] Item 4 (coordinator): no long task after a long paste lands — causes verified by instrumentation and profiles, fixed at the cause, measured in Chromium at 4×, regression-guarded.

**Receipt (2026-10-22, worktree `agent-aba189f6f79dc69d5`, base `a36df2b`, `feat/p10` `50e0739` merged in `d24d68e`; commits `711b615` r2, `82780db` autosave, `3b91130` slices, `eeb4676` budgets):**

- **r2 — mirror vs flag (evidence):** mirroring is impossible: sources A (`i1 jQuery(1)</script >` · `g a(function(){});</script >` · `f b()`) and B (`… g a(function(){</script>` · `f b()});`) serve **byte-identical** pages through Defer + Delay JS, yet WP Rocket's pages under `jquery.min.js` differ (`g`/`f` bodies) — run on the real plugin (PHP 8.1 / PCRE2 10.49). Delay JS normalises only the end tags of scripts it delays (`DelayJS/HTML.php` `<\s*\/\s*script\s*>` → `</script>`), so without Delay the engine's own match spans like WP Rocket's and stays exact (row `…-spaced-end`).
- **Rule (`wrappedScripts`, `lib/exclusion-builder/defer-inline-js.ts`):** an opening script may span when its body is not one whole wrapper, or when the next script after the last opening has the closing alone (`});` — an empty script it spanned to); spanned scripts, and whole wrappers sharing their body, are not read back. Replay: `runs` keeps, `skipped`/`unknown` leave them as served, counted undetermined (new `spanned` field; Defer reports them as generic undetermined — the existing "left as served … can't be read from this page" line, not the live-rows `inlineUndetermined` caveat — so no new copy). Delay JS on such a page also reads each piece without the opening / closing (`spanningWrapperReading`); disagreeing readings are undetermined. **r2b (named residual, unflaggable):** an opener whose own body ends in `});` spanning to a script with a body of its own reads as one whole wrapper (probe: `i1 jQuery(function(){});</script >` + `g var a=1;` → engine `jQuery(function(){`, WP Rocket `jQuery(function(){});`). Flagging it means flagging every whole wrapper followed by any unwrapped script ending in `});` (`$.ajax({});` — the K.4e table's own page): measured on the fuzz (5 × 1000 realistic sets) that looser rule leaves 84 more correct scripts as served and 28 fewer sets unwrapped, on pages with no `</script >` at all; the shipped rule changes nothing on realistic pages.
- **Parity (harness `throughDelayJs` on Defer rows: Delay JS, no rows, after Defer JS):** `match-defer-inline-spaced-end` (no Delay: exact, `inlineWrapped` []), `…-spaced-end-delayed` (`inlineUndetermined`: the end tag Delay rewrote; fails on `a36df2b`, passes now), `…-spaced-end-delayed-runs` (no rows: kept, [`i1`]). 324 rows, pending 0; byte-for-byte on PHP 8.1.34 / 10.49; PHP 8.4.27 / 10.49 local and docker `wprfdt-wordpress-1` 8.4.27 / 10.44 (read-only `php -n` exec via a stdin/`data://` shim) differ only in the header's `php`/`pcre2`/`libxml`.
- **Classifier (investigator's `an.fuzz.ts`, scratch, + a flag class: every mismatching facet left as served on a lane reporting undetermined; 5 seeds × 1000 sets per mode, both PHPs identical):**

  | mode · tree             | sets / scripts | wrong | flagged live-rows / page-unknown | r2 flagged        | r2 silent | r1 silent | unflagged |
  | ----------------------- | -------------- | ----- | -------------------------------- | ----------------- | --------- | --------- | --------- |
  | adversarial · `a36df2b` | 5000 / 24 168  | 1940  | 1329 / 258                       | 85 (incidental)   | **268**   | 0         | 0         |
  | adversarial · P10L.3    | 5000 / 24 168  | 1946  | 1331 / 258                       | 355 (+2 r1 pages) | **0**     | 0         | 0         |
  | realistic · both        | 5000 / 28 743  | 2133  | 2085 / 48                        | —                 | 0         | 0         | 0         |

  No new families; +6 scripts moved from right to flagged (Delay's both-way readings on spanning pages). Stale scratch-harness issues (not committed): `residualSourceForm`/`residualSpacedEnd`/`residuals` never filled; hard-coded `ROOT`/`OUT`/fixture paths into other sessions' scratch; no class for a flagged r2 (any r2 mismatch counted silent); docker mode needs `chain-eval.php`; the flag test is page-level (an unrelated undetermined script covers a mismatch).

- **Perf (transform, 400 scripts / 156 KB, 500 Defer rows, skipped / unknown / runs; median of 40, interleaved):** `a36df2b` 4.20–4.40 ms, P10L.3 4.19–4.50 ms — no regression.
- **Landing autosave:** focus-reaction throw step added to the landing-throw table (short + long; fails on `82780db^`); landed steps also assert the autosave armed and that closing saves the landed rows.
- **Time-sliced paste (`controller.ts`):** `PASTE_SLICE_MS` 16 ms elapsed per slice on `deps.clock` (default `performance.now`; at least one row), `PASTE_AT_ONCE` 64 rows **and** 16 KiB to commit (`fitsAtOnce`); 8 ms was tried first (+12–25 % total paste time at 4× from per-task overhead). Chromium 4× throttle, 500 pasted rows, long-task observer (3 runs each; scratch spec), before → after: **paste task** wire-cap 69–78 → 16–23 ms, class-heavy 243–272 → 21–25 ms; **slice long tasks** (≥ 50 ms) wire-cap Delay one of 51–56 per run (Defer none), class-heavy 183–206 each × 6 (both lanes) → **none**; realistic rows land in 68–74 ms (was 71–93), no long task either way. Land time: wire-cap Delay 865 → 967–992 ms, Defer 680 → 781–795; class-heavy Delay 3 060 → 3 532–3 637, Defer 2 930 → 3 412–3 483 (+12–18 %).
- **Open (not slices, present on `a36df2b` too) — fixed by item 4 below:** with these costly rows the tasks **after** the slices still break 50 ms: the landing task (wire-cap Delay ~370 ms, Defer ~330; class-heavy ~1.6 s, was 1.7–1.8 s) — Node repro: a `rowProblem` read of all 500 rows right after landing costs 463 ms at 1× (class-heavy), i.e. the slices' checks are not reused; **suspected, unverified:** the row-check memo (`ROW_CHECK_CACHE_LIMITS`, 1 MiB) holds fewer than 500 such rows and its oldest-first eviction misses on every read of an in-order scan; and a post-landing task (wire-cap ~150 / ~450 ms; Defer class-heavy ~2.4 s — the profile's top frames are the PCRE translator's byte sets, `emptySet`/`setSize`/`rangesSource`). Realistic rows: none.
- **Tests changed (why):** `defer-inline-js.test.ts` replay expectations gain `spanned: 0` (result shape grew); controller / lane / paste-failure suites inject a 1 ms-per-read clock (slices deterministic); the sliced-vs-at-once helper takes the at-once bounds and the pasted text (`PASTE_SLICE_ROWS` removed). **Added:** spanning-wrapper replay/unwrap/reading cases, Delay both-way verdicts on the real served page, the pipeline flag, `throughDelayJs` parser + case tests, wire-cap and class-heavy sliced-vs-at-once, one budget per slice, one row per slice on a slow clock, `fitsAtOnce`.
- **Gates:** compile, lint, format:check; `pnpm test` 295 files, 7 070 passed (+1 skipped), coverage 98.68/96.79/100/99.39; `parity:wp-rocket` byte-for-byte; `build` + `build:firefox` + `check:budgets` (`eeb4676`, measured + 0.1 KiB: background 184.0, Firefox page 180.0, overlay 256.8 / 86.8 both builds, Firefox engine worker 90.3 / 31.1, whole build 1434.1); `pnpm test:e2e` (serial, port 8897, load avg 3.3–4.4) **207/207** (5.1 min), no retries.

**Item 4 receipt (2026-10-22, same worktree; commits `18e466b` timers, `956c247` stepped engine, `f79f822` overlay, `6387caf` budget gate, `c603ca8` budgets):**

- **Causes (verified, not guessed):** Node per-phase CPU profiles (unminified) + hit/miss/evict counters on every bounded memo, 500 rows: Apply, a keystroke and the Defer JS set reading each spent ~97 % in `analyzeRow` (PCRE parse; self time in `byte-set` `emptySet`/`setSize`, `parse` `characterClass`) under `cachedAnalysis` misses. Per whole-lane read: analysis memo (`pcre/rows`, 256 KiB), row-form memo (`field-text`, 1 MiB), save memo (384 KiB), risk memo and the overlay's row-check memo (1 MiB) — **0 hits, 500 misses, 500 evictions**: the lane outweighs each bound and oldest-first eviction misses on every read of an in-order scan. The slices' checks were never reused: the landing (all rows' problems), Apply (`commitAll` re-saves every row, then `readNow`) and every keystroke (`invalidCount` over all rows) each re-analyzed the lane in one task. Heap snapshots (Chromium) then showed a second defect: the context's `setTimeout` keeps an invalidation listener per timer whose closure scope holds the callback, so every slice pinned what it read (~3 MiB of a set reading's analyses).
- **Fix:** (1) `RowCarry` (`exclusions/draft.ts`): a draft row object carries its own save (`readRowSave`, split from `saveExclusionRow` with `withRowsAhead` for the dedupe) and check — `WeakMap`, text copies shared with the row; seeded by the paste's slices; used by every commit, commit-target scan and row-problem read. (2) Set readings run in time-boxed slices (`startLanePage` → `startDeferRowSet` → `startRegexRowsTranslation`, `utils/stepped`, `TASK_SLICE_MS`), a change cancels the reading in flight. (3) Apply reads a stale lane now; if one slice does not finish it the draft is held (`checking()`, queued actions, label "Applying…") and Apply runs once read; closing meanwhile commits the queued rows and saves, applies nothing. (4) `contextScheduler` (`context-timer.ts`) removes its listener on fire/cancel. Memo bounds unchanged (the carry makes their size irrelevant to these paths). The byte-set hot path was left as is: with nothing re-analyzed, no task remains over 50 ms, and engine output had to stay byte-identical.
- **Same results:** rows, row problems, warnings, page readings, applied sets and notes dumped for every shape × lane (Node harness) — md5-identical before/after; `pnpm parity:wp-rocket` byte-for-byte; the inline-pass classifier (5 × 1000 sets × 2 modes × both PCRE2) byte-identical logs.
- **Chromium, 4× throttle, 500 rows, long-task observer, 3 runs** (tasks ≥ 50 ms; landing ms):

  | lane · rows         | paste: before → after              | paste + Apply: before → after          | typing in row 500: before → after          | lands in              |
  | ------------------- | ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | --------------------- |
  | Delay · wire-cap    | 374–388, 151–168 → **none**        | 364–366, 151–164, 323–330 → **none**   | 351–355, 335–338 → **none**                | 998–1023 → 521–543    |
  | Delay · class-heavy | 1572–1643 → **none**               | 1551–1673 × 2 → **none**               | 1621–1725 × 2 → **none**                   | 3523–3632 → 1925–1959 |
  | Delay · realistic   | none → none                        | none → none                            | none → none                                | 97–131 → 94–96        |
  | Defer · wire-cap    | 323–334, 51–53, 443–446 → **none** | 328–337, 52–59, 732–743 → **none**     | 330–334, 293–305, 446–467 → **none**       | 811–821 → 470–481     |
  | Defer · class-heavy | 1590–1626, 2336–2401 → **none**    | 1538–1629, 4777–4943, 81–84 → **none** | 1653–1737, 1624–1754, 2479–2577 → **none** | 3463–3470 → 1893–1944 |
  | Defer · realistic   | none → none                        | none → none                            | none → none                                | 94–120 → 95–97        |

- **Heap** (Chromium, `Runtime.getHeapUsage` after two forced GCs, retained by the paste 3 s after landing, KiB, before → after): Delay realistic 1 460 → 1 516, wire-cap 6 124 → 5 398, class-heavy 7 561 → 6 065–6 828; Defer realistic 1 586 → 1 633, wire-cap 5 377 → 5 330, class-heavy 6 157–6 925 → 6 203–6 971. The carry costs ~0.1 KiB per row (+47–55 KiB per 500 realistic rows); before the timer fix the Defer wire-cap paste retained 9 243 KiB (the leak above).
- **Regression guards:** `controller-carry.test.ts` (counting `featureRowReading`, `readRowSave`, `storedRowKey`): after a 200-row paste lands, re-reading every row's problem and an Apply read nothing; typing in one row of 150 reads that row only — mutation-proven (an uncarried check fails both); the Apply hold, its at-once path, close-while-waiting; a reading dropped on change. Units: carry once-per-object + shared text + commits equal with/without it; stepped translation / row set / lane page equal to at once; `readRowSave + withRowsAhead = saveExclusionRow` on every drop kind; memo counters + `peek`; `stepFor`; `contextScheduler` (no listener left after fire, cancel, invalidation).
- **Tests changed (why):** `startCommit`'s `onKept` gets the kept row in a hooks object (API grew); the 500-row "Apply reuses its reading" test fires the reading's 0 ms slices first (a reading spans tasks now); `PASTE_SLICE_MS` renamed `TASK_SLICE_MS` (it boxes both slice kinds). No expectation loosened.
- **Budgets nondeterminism (found):** the build is deterministic (overlay md5-identical over three builds); the measurement was not — a non-interactive shell ran Homebrew's Node 26 (zlib 1.2.12) instead of the `.nvmrc` Node 24 (zlib 1.3.2.1-motley), which compress the same bytes 0.3 KB apart (overlay 87.3 vs 87.6). `check:budgets` now refuses a Node whose major differs from `.nvmrc` and prints the Node/zlib it used (`6387caf`; CI already reads `.nvmrc`).
- **Parity interpreter (exact):** the committed table is generated by the default `php` = `/opt/homebrew/Cellar/php@8.1/8.1.34_4/bin/php` (PHP 8.1.34 NTS, PCRE2 10.49 2026-09-28, libxml 2.9.13 — the header's values). 8.4.27 is the CI image (`php:8.4-cli-bookworm`, PCRE2 10.44) and the docker container; local `php@8.4` 8.4.27 / PCRE2 10.49 reproduces every row (header-only diff).
- **Budgets** (`c603ca8`, measured + 0.1 KiB on Node 24): background 184.8, Firefox page 180.8, overlay 259.2 / 87.7 both builds, Firefox engine worker 91.1 / 31.4, whole build 1437.3.
- **Gates:** compile, lint, format:check; `pnpm test` 298 files, 7 108 passed (+1 skipped), coverage 98.68/96.79/100/99.4; `parity:wp-rocket` byte-for-byte; `build` + `build:firefox` + `check:budgets`; `pnpm test:e2e` (serial, port 8897, load avg 3.9 → 2.6) **207/207** (5.1 min), no retries.

### P10M — "On this page" toggle state

Depends:P10K.5b (shared overlay CSS/toolbar). Parallel:P10L. Goal: the toolbar toggle that expands/collapses the page-candidates list visibly says whether the list is open.

Where (verified 2026-10-21): `entrypoints/exclusion-overlay.content/exclusions/mode.tsx` — toolbar `button.xo-button` with `List` icon, label "On this page" + count, `aria-expanded={listOpen()}`, `aria-controls="xo-candidates"`; the list renders under `<Show when={listOpen()}>` (`#xo-candidates`, `exclusions/candidates.tsx`); styles `entrypoints/exclusion-overlay.content/overlay.css` (`.xo-button`; `.xo-chip[aria-pressed='true']` is the existing selected-state precedent). Today both states look identical.

- [x] Open state styled from `[aria-expanded='true']` (tokens only; selected-state treatment consistent with the chip precedent; not color alone — K-5); trailing chevron that turns when open per M-2 "Disclosure chevron" (shadow CSS writes `--motion-fast` + `--ease-standard`, transform only, reduced motion collapses it — M-4/M-6).
- [x] K-3 disclosure pattern verified: `aria-expanded` toggles; `aria-controls` targets an element that exists (the list is unmounted while closed — keep the id resolvable, e.g. a persistent wrapper, or set `aria-controls` only when open); focus stays on the toggle (K-2); disabled state (`aria-disabled` while not ready) still reads as disabled in both states.
- [x] Unit tests: attribute + class per state, chevron present, disabled arm. §D receipt: D-1 (320 px), D-2 light/dark + reduced motion, D-3, D-4, D-6.

DoD: open/closed distinguishable at a glance in both palettes and to AT; §D receipted; budgets green. Effort S · difficulty easy.

**Receipt (2026-10-22, worktree `agent-a3c5657b4a62361cd`, base `ce81745`).** DoD met. Commits: `9b37850` toggle + tests · `fe2cbe2` `build(budgets)` · `de66c81` receipt · audit round (MINOR/NIT): `4115970` held-hover fix + stricter CSS test + e2e leg · this receipt update (budgets unchanged).

- **Open state:** `.xo-disclosure[aria-expanded='true']` takes the pressed chip's fill (`--accent-strong` / `--on-accent`, hover = the primary button's mix); trailing `chevron-down` (per-icon import) turns 180° (`transition: transform var(--motion-fast) var(--ease-standard)`) — fill + direction, never colour alone. Live computed: light closed `surface-2`/`fg-primary` → open rgb(67 56 202)/white; dark rgb(23 27 33) → rgb(79 70 229)/white; chevron `none` → `matrix(-1,0,0,-1,0,0)`.
- **`aria-controls` set only while open** (chosen over a persistent wrapper): the list mounts only open (no scan/virtualizer cost closed), so the attribute never names a missing id and no empty node sits in the panel's flow; a hidden wrapper would add nothing for AT (a hidden target is not exposed). Focus stays on the toggle both ways (unit + live, keyboard Enter); `aria-disabled` (not ready, e.g. reloading) dims both states via the existing `[aria-disabled='true']` rule, a press changes nothing, and hover leaves it still (audit NIT: the open hover rule and the shared `.xo-button:hover` skip `[aria-disabled='true']`, the shared one via `:where` so specificity and the variants' precedence stay; the primary/ghost hovers keep the old behavior — fixing them is more than a one-selector change).
- **Tests** (`app.test.tsx`, new block, 6): closed attributes + chevron (trailing, `aria-hidden`) + no list; open/close in place with `aria-controls` resolving and removed, focus kept; disabled arm closed and open; the CSS contract (open fill, chevron motion tokens, rotation, held-hover scoping) read as text — each declaration as the cascade leaves it for the selector, so order-independent and later overrides count (audit MINOR: the first form matched one rule's substring in declaration order); the reduced-motion reset (`tokens.css`, imported first into the shadow sheet; the overlay never re-enables motion). Against the full old code 4 fail; the open+disabled arm (the old static id resolved while open) and the reduced-motion pin pass on both. E2E leg in `exclusion-overlay-notes.spec.ts` where the shadow CSS applies: computed fill differs open vs closed, chevron `matrix(-1,0,0,-1,0,0)` open / `none` closed, transition 0.12 s → < 1 ms under emulated reduced motion. No pre-existing test changed.
- **Budgets** (measured + 0.1 KiB): overlay both builds 250.66 raw / 84.49 gz → rows 250.8 / 84.6; whole build 1425.36 → 1425.5.
- **Gates:** compile, lint, format, test (6910 passed), parity, build, build:firefox, check:budgets green; e2e 206/206 serial (port 8898), no flake; after the audit round 207/207.
- **§D (live, docker site `127.0.0.1:8493`, headless Chromium + `.output/chrome-mv3`):** D-1 320 px both states/palettes: `.xo-body` 294 = 294, `.xo-toolbar` 270 = 270, toggle fully inside. D-2 light + dark (emulated `prefers-color-scheme`) both states; the chevron runs a `transform` CSSTransition without reduced motion; with `reduce` emulated, transitions collapse to 0.01 ms and the shadow root's `getAnimations()` is empty once settled. D-3 keyboard: Tab to the toggle, Enter opens/closes, focus ring visible, focus kept. D-4 axe (scoped to the shadow `.xo-layer`): 0 violations closed + open, light + dark, and open at 320 px — but axe cannot judge contrast there: `color-contrast` is _incomplete_ on every node ("Unable to determine contrast ratio", 25–40 nodes, 0 passes), so contrast is by hand from the token hex values (WCAG ratio; panel surface = `--surface-1`):

#### P10M.1 — Dark accent contrast + disabled hover

Depends:P10M. Goal: close P10M's named limit (owner 2026-10-22: "do whatever is best") — the dark `--accent-strong` fill reaches 3:1 against every surface it sits on with white text ≥ 4.5:1 on it and on each hover/press variant — and the P10M audit leftovers: held (disabled / `aria-disabled`) controls never change on hover, a cascade-proof CSS contract test, the e2e pinning the open text.

- [x] Inventory every `accent-strong` fill + the surface under it (live, both palettes) and its hover/press derivations; pick the dark value by measurement, hue kept.
- [x] Pin fill-vs-surface 3:1 + the derived hover/press fills in `theme.test.ts`, test-first.
- [x] Held hovers: overlay rules + `components/ui` primitives + any panel control with the same defect; enabled controls' computed styles unchanged.
- [x] `cascaded` accounts for later longhands and stronger selectors; e2e asserts the open `--on-accent` text.

**Receipt (2026-10-22, worktree `agent-a95f2f8393c9643e3`, base `4f45a1d`).** DoD met. Commits: `17f214d` token + contrast pins · `d4e6b94` held hover + CSS contract reader · `b57f338` e2e open text · `83d0f3d` `build(budgets)` · this receipt.

Derived fills: panel `hover:brightness-110` / `active:brightness-95` (Button, FilterBar chip; Chromium scales sRGB channels, measured `79,70,229 → 87,77,252`), overlay hover `color-mix(… accent-strong N%, fg-primary)` (primary + open toggle). ### P10N — Cross-surface sync: exclusion-builder state in detection surfaces

Owner request R-1 (2026-10-22, recorded under Decision gates): a builder-disabled feature (e.g. Delay JS) is really off on the page, yet the devtools panel still shows it active; the panel and the on-page detection strip must say it is disabled/modified by the Exclusion Builder, and every surface must react to the others' state.

**Placement: after P10M, before P10H.** The fix rides the transform pipeline and the detection payload, so it lands after P10K (final row semantics and the elements the transforms stamp) and P10L (removes `documentUrl` from the same transform signatures — serialize, don't conflict). It precedes P10H so the Firefox wiring receipt covers the new signal on both engines in one pass, and P10.1/P10J so the suites and gates include it. N.0 is read-only and may run any time (parallel K.5b/K.6/L/M).

**N.0 findings (live 2026-10-22, tree `a984389`; replaces the preliminary read).** Scratch Playwright spec (headless Chromium, unpacked build, real CDP session backend, fixture port 8897) on the docker site's `/fdt/` (cached; meta declares `wpr_delay_js wpr_defer_js wpr_minify_js wpr_lazyload_images`) and, for ALR, a fixture-server page serving the generator meta with `wpr_automatic_lazy_rendering` + three `data-wpr-lazyrender` elements + `#rocket-lazyrender-inline-css` (the docker site never applies ALR: beacon-only). Per case: overlay → Apply → reload through the session → `getWprDetections`, strip card (DOM), panel Detections card, Copy report; then the engine (`parsePage` + `runChecks`) over the document snapshot with and without the WP Rocket generator meta (scratch vitest). Scratch code and screenshots not committed. No WP Rocket setting was changed.

| Case                                          | Payload row                      | Panel card        | Strip chip / tooltip                                       | Report row                                                    | Markup alone (meta removed)                    |
| --------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- |
| Baseline `/fdt/`                              | delay/defer/lazy present, meta   | Active · Meta     | ok · "applied on this page: 2 scripts" / 1 / 1 images      | Active · Generator meta (Delay: Version `2.0.5`)              | all three present (footprint)                  |
| Delay JS disabled (feature-wide)              | `present: true`, meta, 0 delayed | **Active · Meta** | **ok · "applied on this page: 0 scripts"**                 | **Active · Generator meta**, version `—` (controller removed) | **absent**                                     |
| Delay JS rows (`navigation`, 1 of 2)          | present, meta, 1 delayed         | Active · Meta     | ok · 1 scripts                                             | Active · Generator meta                                       | present (correct)                              |
| Defer JS disabled (feature-wide)              | present, meta                    | **Active · Meta** | **ok · "0 scripts"**                                       | **Active · Generator meta**                                   | **absent**                                     |
| Defer JS page-off (row `\p{L}\Qab`)           | present, meta                    | **Active · Meta** | **ok · "0 scripts"**                                       | **Active · Generator meta**                                   | **absent**                                     |
| Lazyload row (`fdt-lcp`, the only lazy image) | present, meta, `images: 1`       | Active · Meta     | ok · "1 images" (count = every `<img>`, `describe.ts:219`) | Active · Generator meta                                       | present (lazyload script + inline config stay) |
| ALR disabled (feature-wide)                   | present, meta, 0 elements        | **Active · Meta** | **ok · "0 elements"**                                      | **Active · Generator meta**                                   | **absent**                                     |
| ALR row (`id="alr-two"`, 1 of 3)              | present, meta, 2 elements        | Active · Meta     | ok · 2 elements                                            | Active · Generator meta                                       | present (correct)                              |

- **Cause confirmed:** meta authority alone keeps every feature-wide/page-off row "Active" — `applyFeatureMetaAuthority` (`lib/detection/feature-meta.ts:150`, declared → `present: true, source: 'meta'`) called last in `runChecks` (`lib/detection/run-detection.ts:75–103`); without the meta the same documents read absent for Delay/Defer/ALR. Row-level cases are correctly "Active". Nothing in the rewrite touches the meta (grep: no `generator`/`data-wpr-features`/`FEATURE_META_TOKENS` under `lib/exclusion-builder/`, `lib/exclusion-session/`). The surfaces even contradict themselves: "applied on this page: 0 scripts" under an ok chip (`hudStateLine`, `lib/detection/hud.ts:343`).
- **Single root, three surfaces** (anchors re-verified at `a984389`): data hub snapshot `XMLSerializer` (`entrypoints/isolated.content/data-hub.ts:292`) → `processSerializedPage` (`:182`); panel `readHudFeature` via `lib/panel/detections-view-model.ts:141/235`; strip `entrypoints/isolated.content/inspector/strip-state.ts:270/282` (chip `chipStateForFeature` `:148`); report `lib/panel/support-report.ts:330`. All read the rewritten document (counts drop to 0), so the fix lives in the payload, not per surface.
- **Correction — feature-wide disables do leave in-band marks:** every element a disable/page-off restores is stamped `data-wpr-fdt-excluded="<feature>"` (`stampExclusionMarker`, `lib/exclusion-builder/tag-edit.ts:197`; callers `delay-js.ts:256`, `defer-js.ts:249`, `lazyload.ts:243`, `lazy-render.ts:97`). Live: delay-off stamped both scripts `delay_js`, alr-off all three elements. But they cannot carry the verdict: (1) "disabled" vs "every row matched" look identical; (2) a page whose feature has no candidates gets no stamp; (3) detection reads the stamp only on scripts (`fdtExcluded`, `lib/detection/detect-optimizations.ts:108`) and as an image lazyload reason (`LAZYLOAD_EXCLUSIONS_LIST`, `lib/wp-rocket/constants.ts:146`); ALR stamps are not parsed at all. ⇒ a document-level marker is still needed for "disabled"; the per-element stamps serve the "N excluded" count.
- **Feature → rows it can affect:** Delay JS → `delay_js` only (+ its version: the controller removal empties "delay library version"); Defer JS (+ inline jQuery wrapping) → `defer_all_js` only (delay-off left Defer present by markup and vice versa); Lazyload rows → `lazyload` images only (the lane's own copy: pictures, iframes, CSS backgrounds not previewed; `lazyload_iframes`/`lazyload_css_bg_img` untouched; the footprint never goes absent, so rows never flip the verdict); ALR → `automatic_lazy_rendering` only (location hashes stay: the beacon family is untouched).
- **Stop does not reload.** Live: 0 main-frame navigations, same `performance.timeOrigin` 5 s after Stop; the session record is cleared (`session:exclusion-sessions` → `{}`), the rewritten document stays displayed, the overlay says "Session stopped — Reload to see the page without exclusions" with Reload (`banner-model.ts:200–207`; machine `endSession` has no reload effect, `lib/exclusion-session/machine.ts:114–118`; only Apply's `armed` reloads, `:151–165` → `lib/exclusion-session/controller.ts:466`). Surfaces after Stop still describe the rewritten document (delay 0 scripts); after a manual reload they recover to the served verdict (2 scripts, version `2.0.5`). ⇒ an in-band fact stays true for the displayed document after Stop; an out-of-band join (B) would drop the modifier while the page is still rewritten.
- **Firefox (static only, not run live):** the same shared detection (`lib/detection/`, data hub) and the same pipeline run there — `INTERCEPTION_BACKENDS` picks `STREAM_FILTER_BACKEND` on Firefox (`entrypoints/background/interception-backends.ts`) → the shared transform runner/`rewriteDocument` (`lib/exclusion-session/document-transform.ts:301`) — and nothing Firefox-specific touches the meta, so the gap is expected identically. The panel builder entry is hidden there (`builder-entry.tsx:97`, `build !== 'firefox'`) until P10H. P10H receipts the Firefox arm live.
- **Harness note:** the standalone panel page in the browser tier reads the status-bar builder entry as "could not be read" (its sender has a tab; the background answers only tab-less senders) — real DevTools only; N.5 cannot assert that badge.
- **Cross-module inventory (confirmed 2026-10-22; record only, each gap defaults to "later phase"):** device simulation — `deviceSimulationChanged` (sent `entrypoints/background/device-simulation.ts:400`) is consumed only by `ua-relay.content.ts:51` and the isolated side-by-side (`isolated.content/side-by-side/index.tsx:548`): panel/strip never say the tab is simulated. Known conflicts — no reference under `entrypoints/panel/` or `entrypoints/popup/` (on-page only). Toolbar badge — presence/cache only (`entrypoints/background/badge.ts:83–96`), no session input. Popup — still the WXT starter template (`entrypoints/popup/App.tsx`), deferred to P8. Already synced (patterns to reuse): appearance (`components/injected-ui/live-theme.ts`), bypass (`cacheState`), KC DB (`knownConflictsStore.watch`), builder session → panel status bar + overlay pushes.

**Options (refined by the evidence; D decided 2026-10-22):**

| Option                                                   | How                                                                                                                                                                                                       | For                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Against                                                                                                                                                                   |
| -------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| A — in-band document marker                              | Last pipeline step adds one bounded marker naming each feature the document had turned off (disabled or Defer page-off); detection parses it like the feature meta; per-element stamps give "N excluded". | Exact per document, including after Stop (no reload; page still rewritten) and over-budget/failed documents (served unmodified ⇒ no marker); no DCL race; no new messaging; both backends share the pipeline; panel, strip, report get it from one payload. | One more page-visible node (page-forgeable — same trust as every footprint, mis-describes only that page); kept out of candidates, stored-form copy and the parity table. |
| B — out-of-band join                                     | Surfaces read the session status, promote `matchServedDocument`, compose at render.                                                                                                                       | No extra DOM change.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | **Wrong after Stop** (session gone, page still rewritten); `cant-tell` matches; three surfaces repeat the join; new strip subscription; capture race.                     |
| A′ — infer from per-element stamps only                  | Read existing `data-wpr-fdt-excluded` stamps; "all candidates stamped" ⇒ disabled.                                                                                                                        | No new node.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Cannot tell disabled from all-rows-matched; zero-candidate pages unmarked; ALR stamps unparsed today. Rejected.                                                           |
| C — rewrite/strip the meta tokens                        | Remove the disabled token from `data-wpr-features`.                                                                                                                                                       | Zero detection change.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | **Rejected:** detection would misreport what WP Rocket served.                                                                                                            |
| D — A for the verdict, existing pushes for the tab state | A decides the per-row modifier; the existing session pushes keep the panel status bar's tab-level session state; the strip's tab-level line reads the marker (O-N5).                                      | Honest served fact + exact in-document modifier; no new channel.                                                                                                                                                                                            | Two mechanisms (one concern each).                                                                                                                                        |

**Owner decisions — decided 2026-10-22 (all six):**

- **O-N1 — D, with the marker below (decided; O-N4 folded in).** Owner: "make sure whatever you add … it is unique for this extension … think in the best solution". The page itself carries one builder note; every surface reads it; it stays correct after Stop (the page is the rewritten one until reload).
  - **Name:** `<meta name="wpr-fdt-exclusion-builder" content="…">` — this extension's `wpr-fdt-` namespace (as `data-wpr-fdt-excluded`, `wpr-fdt-inspector`); WP Rocket's own feature meta is `name="generator"` (`GENERATOR_META_SELECTOR`, `lib/wp-rocket/constants.ts:286`). One constant declared beside `FDT_EXCLUDED_RESOURCE` (`lib/wp-rocket/constants.ts:19`, verified), pinned in `constants.test.ts`.
  - **Content grammar (bounded, closed):** `v1`, then one token per feature the rewrite changed, in `EXCLUSION_FEATURES` order (`lib/exclusion-builder/set.ts:91`), each id at most once, single spaces: `delay_js=off|excluded:<n>`, `defer_all_js=off|page-off|excluded:<n>`, `automatic_lazy_rendering=off|excluded:<n>`, `lazyload=excluded:<n>` (no toggle). `off` = feature-wide disable; `page-off` = Defer's joined pre-check fails; `excluded:<n>` = elements the rows restored, `n` = `[1-9][0-9]{0,3}` (> 9999 clamps to 9999 on write). ≥ 1 feature token, ≤ 5 tokens, ≤ 128 chars; anything else (unknown id/state, repeat, order, case, extra attributes such as `charset`/`http-equiv`) ⇒ no fact, never a partial one. Example: `v1 delay_js=off defer_all_js=page-off lazyload=excluded:3`. The `v1` prefix makes the format ours and versionable (a later `v2` is an additive parser branch).
  - **Write rule:** the last pipeline step, whenever the pipeline runs on a document, removes **every** existing meta of that name (site-served, stale or spoofed) and, if at least one feature changed the document, appends exactly one at the end of `<head>` (never earlier: it must not push a `<meta charset>` out of the 1024-byte prescan). Over-budget/failed documents are served unmodified (no note).
  - **Read rule (spoof):** a fact exists only when the document has **exactly one** meta of that name, inside `<head>`, whose content parses fully; zero, several, misplaced or malformed ⇒ no fact. On a page the builder did not touch, a site copy is therefore ignored unless it carries this exact format.
  - **Accepted risk:** a page can still forge a well-formed note on a page the builder did not rewrite; it only misleads the page owner's own debugging (same trust as the generator meta and every footprint). No cheap robust guard: a session nonce breaks the after-Stop reading (the session record is cleared while the rewritten page stays) and needs a new messaging hop.
  - **Never page content:** not an overlay candidate (`page-scan.ts` scans `PICKABLE_SELECTOR` only — pinned by a test), not in the stored-form copy / copy-all, not in the WP Rocket parity table (the step runs after the transforms the table pins; the harness never runs it), and detection reads it only through its own parser — no other check, footprint, reference-engine pass or report dump lists it.
- **O-N2 — decided as recommended:** keep the served verdict ("Active": WP Rocket served it), add the modifier, never flip to "Not active". Feature-wide: "Disabled in this tab by the Exclusion Builder". Defer page-off: "Off in this tab: an Exclusion Builder row stops WP Rocket deferring". Rows: "N excluded by the Exclusion Builder". The modifier replaces the contradictory "applied on this page: 0 …" line.
- **O-N3 — decided as recommended:** the P6G.12 filter puts a builder-disabled feature (`off`/`page-off`) under **Inactive** (not running in this tab); the card still says WP Rocket served it. `excluded:<n>` stays Active.
- **O-N4 — folded into O-N1** (the owner asked what the "standard format" is: it is the O-N1 content grammar).
- **O-N5 — decided: now, in P10N, not P12B** (owner: "it must somehow give a 'hint' of features deactivated by the exclusions builder"). The strip shows a tab-level "Exclusion Builder rewrote this page" line plus a hint of what it turned off / excluded, from the same marker and the same copy as the panel → [P10N.4](#p10n4--on-page-strip-ui). In-band: no new strip subscription, correct after Stop. P12B does not depend on it.
- **O-N6 — decided: fix it** (owner: "if it mentions all images are lazyloaded, it is misleading"): the lazy-image count is only the images actually lazy-loaded (`lazyloadDetected`); excluded ones show in the modifier → [P10N.2](#p10n2--shared-reading--copy).

#### P10N.0 — Spike: confirm, decide (no product code) — done 2026-10-22

Depends:— (current tree). Parallel:P10K.5b, P10K.6, P10L, P10M. Goal: every preliminary answer confirmed or corrected with `file:line` anchors, the owner's choices recorded.

- [x] Live five cases (+ Defer feature-wide, ALR rows) on Chromium; served snapshots run with and without the meta → findings above.
- [x] Feature → row map → findings above.
- [x] Stop: no reload; rewritten page stays; recovery on manual reload.
- [x] Firefox: static reading only (same gap expected); live arm → P10H.
- [x] Cross-module inventory confirmed (no scope added).
- [x] Owner decided O-N1–O-N6 (2026-10-22, above).

DoD: findings + decisions recorded here; N.1–N.5 adjusted to option D. Effort S · difficulty medium.

#### P10N.1 — Contracts: builder marker + fact, additive (pure)

Depends:P10N.0, P10L. Parallel:—. Goal: the rewrite writes the O-N1 marker and the detection payload carries an honest per-row builder fact; old payloads stay valid.

- [ ] Marker vocabulary: the meta-name constant beside `FDT_EXCLUDED_RESOURCE` (`lib/wp-rocket/constants.ts`) + the grammar's serializer and parser in one pure module shared by writer and reader (colocated test: every token, bounds, clamp, each rejection of the read rule).
- [ ] A final pipeline step in `lib/exclusion-builder/` per the O-N1 write rule: tokens from the step reports (`featureWide` / Defer page state / restored counts + `rewritten`/`regionRemovals`); removes every existing copy; appends one only when a feature changed the document; test-first (per toggle, page-off, rows, served copies 0/1/many removed, idempotent, never on an unmodified/over-budget document, inside the P10B budget). Parity table untouched.
- [ ] `lib/detection/` reads the marker per the O-N1 read rule (bounded like `feature-meta.ts`; violation ⇒ no fact, never invents one) and derives per row `builder?: { off: 'feature' | 'page' } | { excluded: number }`. Additive optional field on the four `WprDetections` rows (`lib/wp-rocket/types.ts`), validator in `lib/messaging/validate.ts` (unknown values rejected, absence tolerated), caps in `lib/messaging/payload-bounds.ts`. The `data-wpr-fdt-excluded` stamps keep their per-element roles; the count comes from the marker (no stamp-counting pass).
- [ ] Never page content (O-N1): tests that the meta is no overlay candidate (`page-scan.ts`), absent from copy-all, listed by no other check or report dump.
- [ ] Reference engine (`lib/detection/reference/`) and `reference-parity.test.ts`: decide (record why) whether the field is compared or excluded — the reference duplicates control flow only.

DoD: marker writer/reader tests (present/absent/hostile/duplicate/misplaced/degraded), contract tests, old-payload tolerance, gates + budgets green. Effort M · difficulty medium.

#### P10N.2 — Shared reading + copy

Depends:P10N.1. Parallel:—. Goal: one declaring site for how a builder fact reads, used by every surface (panel card, strip chip, strip tab-level line, report).

- [ ] `HudReading` gains the modifier (`lib/detection/hud.ts`); `hudStateLine` + chip/tooltip copy (O-N2: feature-wide, page-off, rows) and the strip's tab-level line + per-feature hint copy (O-N5) declared once beside `SOURCE_COPY`; the verdict itself is unchanged; the modifier line replaces "applied on this page: 0 …".
- [ ] Support report row (`lib/panel/support-report.ts`) states served verdict + modifier; Delay's empty version cell reads as explained by the modifier.
- [ ] O-N6 (decided): lazy-image count = `lazyloadDetected` images only (`lib/detection/describe.ts:219`). **The existing `describe.test.ts` assertion pinning the every-`<img>` count changes by owner decision (O-N6, 2026-10-22)** — state it in the commit message.
- [ ] Unit tests: every verdict × modifier, the tab-level line per token, copy strings pinned.

DoD: tests + gates green. Effort S · difficulty easy.

#### P10N.3 — Devtools panel UI

Depends:P10N.2. Parallel:P10N.4. Goal: the Detections card says the feature is disabled/modified in this tab.

- [ ] Card modifier badge + tooltip (`entrypoints/panel/pages/detections/**`, `lib/panel/detections-view-model.ts`), tokens/primitives only, not color alone (K-5); the summary strip counts per O-N3.
- [ ] P6G.12 filter: if shipped, classify per O-N3 (`off`/`page-off` ⇒ Inactive, `excluded` stays Active; + its unit test); if not, add the rule to P6G.12's checklist.
- [ ] Refresh: Apply reloads → the panel's existing reload re-renders. Stop does **not** reload: the modifier must stay while the rewritten page is displayed and go after the user reloads (verify both).
- [ ] Unit/page tests; UI bar §D receipt (D-1 320 px, D-2 both palettes, D-3, D-4).

DoD: card/report/filter correct for the N.0 cases; §D receipted; gates + budgets green. Effort S · difficulty easy.

#### P10N.4 — On-page strip UI

Depends:P10N.2. Parallel:P10N.3. Goal: the strip says the same as the panel — per-chip modifier plus (O-N5) a tab-level "Exclusion Builder rewrote this page" line hinting what it turned off / excluded.

- [ ] `chipStateForFeature` / row tooltip (`entrypoints/isolated.content/inspector/strip-state.ts:148/169`) render the modifier; shadow-isolated tokens only.
- [ ] Tab-level line (O-N5): shown only when the payload carries a builder fact, N.2 copy; names each feature turned off (`off`/`page-off`) and each excluded count, e.g. "Exclusion Builder rewrote this page — Delay JS off · Defer JS off on this page · 3 images excluded". Keyboard/screen-reader reachable in the strip's existing structure; stays after Stop, goes on reload (in-band, no new subscription).
- [ ] Unit tests (`strip-state.test.ts`: line absent/present, every token, wording equals the panel's); §D receipt (D-1 320 px, D-2 both palettes, D-3, D-4); strip bundle budget re-measured. P12B inherits the shape (no rework).

DoD: strip matches the panel for the N.0 cases; the tab-level line present exactly on rewritten documents; gates + budgets green. Effort S–M · difficulty easy (was S: + the O-N5 line).

#### P10N.5 — Browser suite + live check

Depends:P10N.3, P10N.4. Parallel:—. Goal: the sync proven end to end on Chromium.

- [ ] `tests/e2e/` spec over real sessions (the session backend runs under the harness — proven by N.0 and `exclusion-overlay-notes`): docker `/fdt/` for Delay off / Delay rows / Defer off / Defer page-off / Lazyload row, a fixture page serving the ALR meta + elements for ALR off / rows; assert the marker (exactly one, O-N1 content), payload fact, panel card, strip chip + tab-level line, report row. Spoof: a fixture page serving a duplicate marker without a session ⇒ no fact; a served copy is replaced after Apply. Stop (no reload: modifier and strip line stay) → reload (served verdict back). The panel status-bar entry is not assertable in the harness (N.0 harness note).
- [ ] Owner live check in real DevTools on the docker site: the same cases, the status-bar badge, the strip line, then Stop → reload → every surface back to the served verdict.

DoD: spec green with the tier; live check recorded; Firefox arm handed to P10H. Effort S · difficulty medium.

### P10H — Firefox wiring

Depends:P10E+F, G for the shared wiring shape; P10K–P10N first (owner order 2026-10-21; P10N added 2026-10-22). Parallel:—.

- [ ] Firefox apply flow end-to-end; streaming backend's applied-state surfaced in the same overlay contract.
- [ ] Cross-browser receipt: same exclusion set, both engines, same engine report.
- [ ] P10N on Firefox: panel card + strip show the builder modifier for the five P10N.0 cases (the panel builder entry becomes available here).
- [ ] Verify Firefox shares the isolated world between the manifest content script and `scripting.executeScript` files (the navigation anchor); if not, the overlay falls back to the read-at-mount anchor and degrades to "can't verify".
- [ ] Re-check the page-key guard's order against the page's inline `<head>` scripts on the real flow (Apply's reload, cold starts) — fine on reload/fresh navigation in P10F's final round.

DoD: Firefox parity with the shared flow (no fallback exists — G-3); no Chromium regression. Effort S · difficulty easy.

### P10.1 — Browser suites (this file, local tier)

Depends:P10G(+P10H), P10K–P10N; P4.1 harness. Scope shaped by P10A's Playwright-coexistence finding:

- [ ] Overlay editor suites: compose/validate/counts, pick flows, state matrix arms, Stop/Reset, mode indicator (backend stubbed at the message seam); P10K stored-form notes + copy-all stored text; P10M toggle open/closed; P10N.5's spec joins the tier.
- [ ] If CDP coexists: one interception arm asserting the fulfilled document applied the engine transform; else interception stays manual-evidenced and the suite covers everything around it — the gap stated, never hidden.
- [ ] Keyboard/axe (D-3/D-4), width matrix (D-1), reduced motion (D-2); Firefox arms manual per harness rules unless re-verified.

DoD: suites green with the tier; suite scope vs manual evidence recorded. Effort M · difficulty medium.

### P10J — Gates, budgets, docs

Depends:all above (P10A–P10N, P10.1). Parallel:—.

- [ ] Full gates: `pnpm compile && pnpm lint && pnpm format:check && pnpm test`; `pnpm build` (+ `:firefox`) and `pnpm check:budgets` with new rows/receipted deltas.
- [ ] AGENTS.md: `debugger` permission (why + warning), new lib module, any new script; runbook rows (apply modes, DevTools interplay, Reset semantics); README active-work line closed with receipts; master train slot confirmed by owner.
- [ ] Every G-gate receipted; every behavior delta vs the source inventory dispositioned with evidence.
- [ ] Policy-block check (`DeveloperToolsAvailability = 2`) stays **deferred, check later (owner 2026-10-21)** — not a P10 close blocker; carry it to P15's open-verification ledger.

DoD: all gates green, docs current, P10 closable pending owner live-check. Effort S · difficulty easy.

## Non-goals

- No file-import/export flow (superseded); no byte-identical old-notice mandate; **no simulation fallback of any kind — interception unavailable = the G-3 warning state** (DNR and DOM-hook simulation both rejected by owner 2026-10-20; [P21](./21-phase-21-wpr-simulation.md)'s WP Rocket feature simulation is a separate owner-requested capability riding the interception, not a fallback); no page-load performance instrumentation (P14's domain); no exclusion _recommendations_ engine (P6's copy-as-exclusion vocabulary remains the generator); no i18n; no new dependency; no POST-navigation applies (the apply targets a GET reload; POST endpoints out of scope).
- RUCSS tester, beacon/LCP, and script-runtime implementations stay in P13/P14 — P10 ships only the shell, session lifecycle, and pipeline seam (no tester config/results UI here).
